local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

local Core
repeat
	Core = _G.KickArenaCore
	if not Core then
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until Core

local Ext
repeat
	Ext = _G.KickArenaExt
	if not (Ext and Ext.ready) then
		Ext = nil
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until Ext

local FX = Ext.FX
local now = Ext.now
local rootOf = Ext.rootOf

local function flatDir(v, fallback)
	local f = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
	return f.Magnitude > 0.05 and f.Unit or (fallback or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
end

local modules = {}
local cds = {}

local function setCooldown(player, secs)
	local kick = player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick")
	cds[player] = cds[player] or {}
	cds[player][kick] = { endAt = now() + secs, total = secs }
	player:SetAttribute("XCooldownEnd", now() + secs)
	player:SetAttribute("XCooldown", secs)
end

local function onCooldown(player)
	local c = cds[player] and cds[player][player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick")]
	return c ~= nil and now() < c.endAt
end

local function groundBelow(from, ignore, depth)
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
	return Workspace:Raycast(from, Vector3.new(0, -(depth or 40), 0), params)
end

local function newPart(parent, name, size, cf, color, material, extra)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	p.Name = name
	p.Size = size
	p.CFrame = cf
	p.Anchored = true
	p.Color = color
	p.Material = material or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	for k, v in pairs(extra or {}) do
		p[k] = v
	end
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

local function chars()
	local list = {}
	for _, m in ipairs(Ext.allTargets()) do
		table.insert(list, m)
	end
	return list
end

-- TIME STOP (God's Kick)
local godPending, godOwner = nil, nil
function Ext.godHit(model, attacker, dir)
	if not godPending or attacker ~= godOwner then
		return
	end
	local e = godPending[model] or { hits = 0 }
	e.hits += 1
	e.dir = dir
	godPending[model] = e
	local root = rootOf(model)
	if root then
		FX:FireAllClients("GodMark", root.Position)
	end
end

modules.God = {
	e = function(player, char, _, hrp)
		if onCooldown(player) or Workspace:GetAttribute("KickTimeStop") then
			return
		end
		setCooldown(player, 60)
		Workspace:SetAttribute("KickTimeStop", true)
		godPending, godOwner = {}, player
		local frozen = {}
		for _, m in ipairs(Ext.allTargets()) do
			local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m)
			if m ~= char and (not plr or plr:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true) then
				local entry = { model = m, parts = {}, mark = m:GetAttribute("Frozen") ~= true }
				for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("BasePart") and not d.Anchored then
						d.Anchored = true
						entry.parts[d] = true
					end
				end
				if entry.mark then
					m:SetAttribute("Frozen", true)
				end
				table.insert(frozen, entry)
			end
		end
		FX:FireAllClients("TimeStop", true, hrp.Position)
		task.delay(10, function()
			local pending = godPending or {}
			godPending, godOwner = nil, nil
			for _, e in ipairs(frozen) do
				for d in pairs(e.parts) do
					if d.Parent then
						d.Anchored = false
					end
				end
				if e.mark and e.model.Parent then
					e.model:SetAttribute("Frozen", false)
				end
			end
			Workspace:SetAttribute("KickTimeStop", false)
			FX:FireAllClients("TimeStop", false)
			task.defer(function()
				for model, e in pairs(pending) do
					if model.Parent then
						local root = rootOf(model)
						if root then
							FX:FireAllClients("GodLight", root.Position)
							Core.ragdoll(model, Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model), e.dir, 450)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end)
	end,
}

-- WOOD JAIL (hit hook)
function Ext.jail(model, dur)
	local root = rootOf(model)
	if not root or model:GetAttribute("Frozen") == true or model:GetAttribute("Jailed") == true then
		return false
	end
	model:SetAttribute("Jailed", true)
	model:SetAttribute("Frozen", true)
	local wasAnchored = root.Anchored
	root.Anchored = true
	local hit = groundBelow(root.Position, { model }, 12)
	local baseY = hit and hit.Position.Y or root.Position.Y - 3
	local c = Vector3.new(root.Position.X, baseY, root.Position.Z)
	local cage = Instance.new("Folder")
	cage.Name = "WoodCage"
	local wood = Color3.fromRGB(150, 102, 62)
	local function plank(size, pos)
		newPart(cage, "Plank", size, CFrame.new(c + pos), wood, Enum.Material.Wood, { CanCollide = false, CanTouch = false })
	end
	for i = -2, 2 do
		plank(Vector3.new(0.7, 9, 1), Vector3.new(i * 1.5, 4.5, 3.4))
		plank(Vector3.new(0.7, 9, 1), Vector3.new(i * 1.5, 4.5, -3.4))
		plank(Vector3.new(1, 9, 0.7), Vector3.new(3.4, 4.5, i * 1.5))
		plank(Vector3.new(1, 9, 0.7), Vector3.new(-3.4, 4.5, i * 1.5))
	end
	for i = -1, 1 do
		plank(Vector3.new(8, 0.7, 1), Vector3.new(0, 9.2, i * 2.4))
	end
	cage.Parent = Workspace
	FX:FireAllClients("Jail", c, true)
	task.delay(dur, function()
		FX:FireAllClients("Jail", c, false)
		cage:Destroy()
		if model.Parent then
			model:SetAttribute("Jailed", false)
			model:SetAttribute("Frozen", false)
			if root.Parent and not wasAnchored and not Core.isStoneForm(model) then
				root.Anchored = false
			end
		end
	end)
	return true
end

-- PAINT (hit hook)
function Ext.paint(model, plr, dur)
	local root = rootOf(model)
	Ext.applyLayer(model, "Paint", function()
		return Color3.fromHSV(math.random(), 0.9, 1)
	end, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, 0, dur)
	if root then
		FX:FireAllClients("PaintBurst", root.Position)
	end
	if plr then
		FX:FireClient(plr, "PaintScreen", dur)
	end
end

-- WIND
modules.Wind = {
	e = function(player, char, _, hrp)
		if onCooldown(player) then
			return
		end
		local best, bd = nil, 24
		for _, m in ipairs(Ext.allTargets()) do
			local r = rootOf(m)
			local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if m ~= char and r and h and h.Health > 0 and not Core.isStoneForm(m)
				and m:GetAttribute("KickImmune") ~= true and m:GetAttribute("Frozen") ~= true then
				local d = (r.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
				if d < bd then
					best, bd = m, d
				end
			end
		end
		if not best then
			return
		end
		setCooldown(player, 10)
		local r = rootOf(best)
		local look = flatDir(r.Position - hrp.Position, flatDir(hrp.CFrame.LookVector))
		hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + look)
		FX:FireAllClients("Wind", hrp.Position, look)
		Core.ragdoll(best, Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(best), look, 24)
		best:SetAttribute("LastHitBy", player.UserId)
		best:SetAttribute("LastHitAt", now())
		FX:FireAllClients("WindHit", r.Position, look)
	end,
}

-- MIRRORS
local mirrors = {}

local function killMirror(m, shatter)
	for _, list in pairs(mirrors) do
		local i = table.find(list, m)
		if i then
			table.remove(list, i)
		end
	end
	if m.Parent then
		if shatter then
			FX:FireAllClients("MirrorShatter", m:GetPivot().Position)
		end
		m:Destroy()
	end
end

function Ext.hitMirror(model, attacker)
	local hp = (model:GetAttribute("MirrorHP") or 10) - 2
	model:SetAttribute("MirrorHP", hp)
	local pane = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if pane then
		FX:FireAllClients("MirrorHit", pane.Position)
	end
	local bar = model:FindFirstChild("Bar", true)
	if bar then
		bar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.max(hp, 0) / 10, 1)
	end
	if hp <= 0 then
		killMirror(model, true)
	end
end

local function mirrorStrike(player, model)
	local pane = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not pane then
		return
	end
	for _, m in ipairs(Ext.allTargets()) do
		local r = rootOf(m)
		local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if m ~= player.Character and r and h and h.Health > 0 and not Core.isStoneForm(m)
			and m:GetAttribute("KickImmune") ~= true and m:GetAttribute("Frozen") ~= true
			and m:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") ~= true and (r.Position - pane.Position).Magnitude <= 13 then
			local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m)
			FX:FireAllClients("MirrorBeam", pane.Position, r.Position)
			Core.ragdoll(m, plr, flatDir(r.Position - pane.Position), math.min(450, 30 + 50 * 1.2 + 40))
			m:SetAttribute("LastHitBy", player.UserId)
			m:SetAttribute("LastHitAt", now())
			Core.awardKicks(player, 1)
		end
	end
end

modules.Mirror = {
	e = function(player, char, _, hrp)
		if onCooldown(player) then
			return
		end
		local look = flatDir(hrp.CFrame.LookVector)
		local hit = groundBelow(hrp.Position + look * 9 + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), { char }, 30)
		if not hit then
			Core.notify(player, "No ground in front of you", false)
			return
		end
		setCooldown(player, 5)
		local list = mirrors[player] or {}
		mirrors[player] = list
		local center = hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4.2, 0)
		local base = hit.Position
		local lean = CFrame.lookAt(base, base - look) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(10), 0, 0)
		local model = Instance.new("Model")
		model.Name = "BattleMirror"
		local pane = Ext.buildMirror(model, lean, "HumanoidRootPart", true)
		local hum = Instance.new("Humanoid")
		hum.MaxHealth = 10
		hum.Health = 10
		hum.WalkSpeed = 0
		hum.RequiresNeck = false
		hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
		hum.HealthDisplayType = Enum.HumanoidHealthDisplayType.AlwaysOff
		hum.NameDisplayDistance = 0
		hum.Parent = model
		model:SetAttribute("IsMirror", true)
		model:SetAttribute("MirrorHP", 10)
		-- old wooden brace holds the mirror up
		local from = (lean * CFrame.new(0, 11.2, 0.6)).Position
		local to = base + look * 5.5 + Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0)
		newPart(model, "Brace", Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, (from - to).Magnitude), CFrame.lookAt((from + to) / 2, to), Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 26), Enum.Material.Wood, { CanCollide = false })
		local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 9)
		gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 8.4, 0)
		gui.MaxDistance = 60
		gui.Adornee = pane
		gui.Parent = pane
		local back = Instance.new("Frame")
		back.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		back.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 8)
		back.BorderSizePixel = 0
		back.Parent = gui
		Instance.new("UICorner", back).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		Instance.new("UIStroke", back).Color = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
		local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
		bar.Name = "Bar"
		bar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
		bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
		bar.Parent = back
		Instance.new("UICorner", bar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		model.Parent = Workspace
		FX:FireAllClients("MirrorSpawn", center)
		table.insert(list, model)
		while #list > 3 do
			killMirror(table.remove(list, 1), true)
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			while model.Parent do
				task.wait(1.2)
				if not model.Parent then
					break
				end
				if not player.Parent or player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") ~= true then
					killMirror(model, true)
					break
				end
				mirrorStrike(player, model)
			end
		end)
	end,
	off = function(player)
		local list = mirrors[player]
		if list then
			for i = #list, 1, -1 do
				killMirror(list[i], true)
			end
		end
	end,
}

-- SMILE BALL
local balls = {}

local function setHidden(char, store, on, alpha)
	if on then
		for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
				store[d] = { d.Transparency, d.CanCollide, d.Massless }
				d.Transparency = alpha
				d.CanCollide = false
				d.Massless = true
			elseif d:IsA("Decal") then
				store[d] = { d.Transparency }
				d.Transparency = alpha
			end
		end
	else
		for d, v in pairs(store) do
			if d.Parent then
				d.Transparency = v[1]
				if v[2] ~= nil then
					d.CanCollide = v[2]
					d.Massless = v[3]
				end
			end
		end
		table.clear(store)
	end
end

local function ballOff(player)
	local st = balls[player]
	if not st then
		return
	end
	balls[player] = nil
	st.conn:Disconnect()
	local char = st.char
	local pos = st.ball.Position
	if char.Parent then
		st.weld:Destroy()
		st.ball:Destroy()
		setHidden(char, st.store, false)
		local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if hum then
			hum.PlatformStand = false
			hum.AutoRotate = true
		end
		if hrp then
			hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			char:PivotTo(CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 3.4, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(hrp.Orientation.Y), 0))
			pcall(function()
				hrp:SetNetworkOwner(player)
			end)
		end
		char:SetAttribute("KickImmune", false)
	else
		st.ball:Destroy()
	end
	player:SetAttribute("XActive", false)
	player:SetAttribute("NoKick", false)
	FX:FireAllClients("Ball", pos, false)
end

local function ballOn(player, char, hum, hrp)
	local ball = Instance.new("Part")
	ball.Name = "SmileBall"
	ball.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
	ball.Size = Vector3.new(7, 7, 7)
	ball.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	ball.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 60)
	ball.CustomPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(0.7, 0.1, 0.35, 1, 1)
	ball.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0))
	ball.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	ball.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	local function dot(offset, size)
		local d = Instance.new("Part")
		d.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
		d.Size = size
		d.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
		d.Color = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
		d.CanCollide = false
		d.CanQuery = false
		d.CanTouch = false
		d.Massless = true
		d.CFrame = ball.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset)
		d.Parent = ball
		local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
		w.Part0 = ball
		w.Part1 = d
		w.Parent = d
	end
	for _, s in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		dot(Vector3.new(-1.2, 0.9, 3.15 * s), Vector3.new(0.9, 1.3, 0.5))
		dot(Vector3.new(1.2, 0.9, 3.15 * s), Vector3.new(0.9, 1.3, 0.5))
		for _, m in ipairs({ { -1.7, -0.3 }, { -1.0, -0.95 }, { 0, -1.25 }, { 1.0, -0.95 }, { 1.7, -0.3 } }) do
			local x, y = m[1], m[2]
			local z = math.sqrt(math.max(3.5 * 3.5 - x * x - y * y, 0.5)) - 0.15
			dot(Vector3.new(x, y, z * s), Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
		end
	end
	local store = {}
	setHidden(char, store, true, 1)
	hum.PlatformStand = true
	hum.AutoRotate = false
	hrp.CFrame = ball.CFrame
	hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
	weld.Part0 = ball
	weld.Part1 = hrp
	weld.Parent = ball
	ball.Parent = char
	pcall(function()
		ball:SetNetworkOwner(player)
	end)
	player:SetAttribute("XActive", true)
	player:SetAttribute("NoKick", true)
	char:SetAttribute("KickImmune", true)
	local hitAt = {}
	local conn = ball.Touched:Connect(function(other)
		local m = other:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		if not m or m == char then
			return
		end
		local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local r = rootOf(m)
		local speed = ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
		if not h or not r or h.Health <= 0 or speed < 20 then
			return
		end
		if hitAt[m] and os.clock() - hitAt[m] < 1 then
			return
		end
		hitAt[m] = os.clock()
		if m:GetAttribute("IsMirror") then
			Ext.hitMirror(m, player)
			return
		end
		if Core.isStoneForm(m) or m:GetAttribute("KickImmune") == true or m:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") == true then
			return
		end
		local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m)
		Core.ragdoll(m, plr, flatDir(ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity, flatDir(r.Position - ball.Position)), math.min(450, 120 + speed * 2))
		m:SetAttribute("LastHitBy", player.UserId)
		m:SetAttribute("LastHitAt", now())
		Core.awardKicks(player, 1)
		FX:FireAllClients("Smash", r.Position)
	end)
	balls[player] = { ball = ball, weld = weld, conn = conn, char = char, store = store }
	FX:FireAllClients("Ball", ball.Position, true)
end

modules.Smile = {
	e = function(player, char, hum, hrp)
		if onCooldown(player) then
			return
		end
		setCooldown(player, 1)
		if balls[player] then
			ballOff(player)
		else
			ballOn(player, char, hum, hrp)
		end
	end,
	off = ballOff,
}

-- VANISH (666)
local vanished = {}

local function vanishOff(player)
	local st = vanished[player]
	if not st then
		return
	end
	vanished[player] = nil
	setHidden(st.char, st.store, false)
	local hum = st.char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hum then
		hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.Viewer
	end
	player:SetAttribute("XActive", false)
	local root = rootOf(st.char)
	if root then
		FX:FireAllClients("Vanish", root.Position, false)
	end
end

modules.Devil = {
	e = function(player, char, hum, hrp)
		if onCooldown(player) then
			return
		end
		setCooldown(player, 1)
		if vanished[player] then
			vanishOff(player)
			return
		end
		local store = {}
		for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
				store[d] = { d.Transparency }
				d.Transparency = 1
			elseif d:IsA("Decal") then
				store[d] = { d.Transparency }
				d.Transparency = 1
			end
		end
		hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
		vanished[player] = { char = char, store = store }
		player:SetAttribute("XActive", true)
		FX:FireAllClients("Vanish", hrp.Position, true)
	end,
	off = vanishOff,
}

-- SPECTATOR
local ghosts = {}

local function specOff(player)
	local st = ghosts[player]
	if st then
		ghosts[player] = nil
		setHidden(st.char, st.store, false)
		st.char:SetAttribute("KickImmune", false)
	end
	if player:GetAttribute("NoKick") == true and not balls[player] then
		player:SetAttribute("NoKick", false)
	end
	player:SetAttribute("XActive", false)
end

local function specOn(player)
	local char = player.Character
	if not char or ghosts[player] or not Core.getAlive(player) then
		return
	end
	local store = {}
	for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
			store[d] = { d.Transparency }
			d.Transparency = math.max(d.Transparency, 0.55)
		end
	end
	ghosts[player] = { char = char, store = store }
	char:SetAttribute("KickImmune", true)
	player:SetAttribute("NoKick", true)
end

modules.Spectator = {
	on = specOn,
	e = function(player)
		if onCooldown(player) then
			return
		end
		setCooldown(player, 0.6)
		player:SetAttribute("XActive", player:GetAttribute("XActive") ~= true)
	end,
	off = specOff,
}

-- MASTER TYCOON
local tycoons = {}

local UPGRADES = {
	{ id = "m2", label = "2x Money", price = 50 },
	{ id = "m3", label = "3x Money", price = 150 },
	{ id = "d1", label = "+1 Dropper", price = 200 },
	{ id = "auto", label = "Auto Drop", price = 250 },
	{ id = "d2", label = "+1 Dropper", price = 300 },
	{ id = "kp", label = "2x Kick Power", price = 360 },
	{ id = "sp", label = "2x Speed", price = 400 },
	{ id = "god", label = "God Mode", price = 1000 },
}
local BASE_COIN = 5
local DROP_EVERY = 1.4
local GREEN = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 140)

local function tryPlace(char, hum, hrp)
	if hum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air or math.abs(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y) > 6 then
		return nil, "Stand on solid ground to build your tycoon"
	end
	local look = flatDir(hrp.CFrame.LookVector)
	local c = hrp.Position + look * 20
	local flatCF = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(c.X, hrp.Position.Y, c.Z), Vector3.new(c.X, hrp.Position.Y, c.Z) + look)
	local ignore = {}
	for _, m in ipairs(Ext.allTargets()) do
		table.insert(ignore, m)
	end
	for _, st in pairs(tycoons) do
		table.insert(ignore, st.model)
	end
	local lo, hi, sum, n = math.huge, -math.huge, 0, 0
	for _, o in ipairs({ { 0, 0 }, { -19, -13 }, { 19, -13 }, { -19, 13 }, { 19, 13 }, { 0, -13 }, { 0, 13 }, { -19, 0 }, { 19, 0 } }) do
		local p = (flatCF * CFrame.new(o[1], 0, o[2])).Position
		local hit = groundBelow(p + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), ignore, 16)
		if not hit or hit.Normal.Y < 0.9 then
			return nil, "Not enough flat ground here. Find a bigger open area"
		end
		lo = math.min(lo, hit.Position.Y)
		hi = math.max(hi, hit.Position.Y)
		sum += hit.Position.Y
		n += 1
	end
	if hi - lo > 1.5 then
		return nil, "The ground is too uneven here. Find a flat area"
	end
	local y = sum / n
	return CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(c.X, y, c.Z), Vector3.new(c.X, y, c.Z) + look)
end

local function recompute(st)
	local owner = st.owner
	local power = st.bought.god and 100 or (st.bought.kp and 2 or 1)
	local speed = st.bought.god and 3 or (st.bought.sp and 2 or 1)
	local jump = st.bought.god and 2 or 1
	owner:SetAttribute("KickPower", power)
	owner:SetAttribute("TycoonGod", st.bought.god == true)
	if owner.Character then
		Ext.setMult(owner.Character, speed, jump)
	end
end

local function refreshDisplays(st)
	st.owner:SetAttribute("TycoonCash", st.cash)
	st.cashLabel.Text = "CASH  $" .. Core.formatNumber(st.cash)
	st.rateLabel.Text = ("x%d money  |  $%d per coin  |  %d dropper%s  |  %s"):format(st.mult, BASE_COIN * st.mult, st.droppers, st.droppers == 1 and "" or "s", st.auto and "AUTO" or "press DROP")
	for _, c in ipairs(st.model:GetChildren()) do
		if c.Name == "Coin" and c:GetAttribute("CashValue") then
			c:SetAttribute("CashValue", BASE_COIN * st.mult)
		end
	end
	for _, up in ipairs(UPGRADES) do
		local pad = st.pads[up.id]
		if st.bought[up.id] then
			pad.part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 190, 110)
			pad.price.Text = "OWNED"
			pad.prompt.Enabled = false
		else
			local ok = st.cash >= up.price
			pad.part.Color = ok and Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 70) or Color3.fromRGB(110, 114, 130)
			pad.price.Text = "$" .. up.price
		end
	end
end

local TYC_SCALE = 0.55

local function addDropper(st, index)
	local x = -14 + (index - 1) * 7
	local cf = st.cf
	local group = st.scaled and Instance.new("Model") or st.model
	local dark = Color3.fromRGB(58, 62, 76)
	newPart(group, "Hopper", Vector3.new(3.6, 3, 3.6), cf * CFrame.new(x, 10.5, -6), dark, Enum.Material.Metal)
	newPart(group, "HopperBand", Vector3.new(3.8, 0.4, 3.8), cf * CFrame.new(x, 11.6, -6), GREEN, Enum.Material.Neon, { CanCollide = false })
	newPart(group, "Nozzle", Vector3.new(1.4, 1.2, 1.4), cf * CFrame.new(x, 8.4, -6), Color3.fromRGB(34, 36, 44), Enum.Material.Metal)
	newPart(group, "Leg", Vector3.new(0.9, 10, 0.9), cf * CFrame.new(x, 6, -10.6), dark, Enum.Material.Metal)
	newPart(group, "Arm", Vector3.new(0.7, 0.7, 3.4), cf * CFrame.new(x, 10.5, -9.1), dark, Enum.Material.Metal, { CanCollide = false })
	if st.scaled then
		group.WorldPivot = cf
		group:ScaleTo(TYC_SCALE)
		group.Parent = st.model
	end
end

local function spawnCoin(st, index)
	if st.coins >= 40 then
		return
	end
	local x = (-14 + (index - 1) * 7) * TYC_SCALE
	local coin = Instance.new("Part")
	coin.Name = "Coin"
	coin.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
	coin.Size = Vector3.new(0.35, 1.6, 1.6) * TYC_SCALE
	coin.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 60)
	coin.Material = Enum.Material.Metal
	coin.Reflectance = 0.2
	coin.CFrame = st.cf * CFrame.new(x, 7.4 * TYC_SCALE, -6 * TYC_SCALE) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
	coin:SetAttribute("CashValue", BASE_COIN * st.mult)
	coin.Parent = st.model
	st.coins += 1
	coin.Destroying:Connect(function()
		st.coins -= 1
	end)
	Debris:AddItem(coin, 25)
end

local function buy(st, up)
	local owner = st.owner
	if st.bought[up.id] or st.dead then
		return
	end
	if st.cash < up.price then
		Core.notify(owner, ("Not enough cash. You need $%d more"):format(up.price - st.cash), false)
		return
	end
	st.cash -= up.price
	st.bought[up.id] = true
	if up.id == "m2" then
		st.mult *= 2
	elseif up.id == "m3" then
		st.mult *= 3
	elseif up.id == "auto" then
		st.auto = true
		if st.dropBtn then
			st.dropBtn:Destroy()
			st.dropBtn = nil
		end
	elseif up.id == "d1" or up.id == "d2" then
		st.droppers += 1
		addDropper(st, st.droppers)
	end
	recompute(st)
	refreshDisplays(st)
	FX:FireAllClients("Buy", st.pads[up.id].part.Position, up.id == "god")
	Core.notify(owner, "Purchased: " .. up.label, true)
end

local function removeTycoon(player, explode)
	local st = tycoons[player]
	if not st then
		return
	end
	tycoons[player] = nil
	st.dead = true
	player:SetAttribute("XActive", false)
	player:SetAttribute("TycoonCash", 0)
	player:SetAttribute("KickPower", 1)
	player:SetAttribute("TycoonGod", false)
	if player.Character then
		Ext.setMult(player.Character, 1, 1)
	end
	if explode and st.model.Parent then
		local pos = st.cf.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
		FX:FireAllClients("Explode", pos, 26)
		for _, d in ipairs(st.model:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then
				local v = d.Position - pos
				v = v.Magnitude > 0.1 and v.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
				d.Anchored = false
				d.CanCollide = false
				d.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v * math.random(35, 80) + Vector3.new(0, math.random(25, 60), 0)
				d.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-8, 8), math.random(-8, 8), math.random(-8, 8))
			end
		end
		Debris:AddItem(st.model, 3)
	else
		st.model:Destroy()
	end
end

local function buildTycoon(player, cf)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Tycoon_" .. player.Name
	local st = {
		owner = player, model = model, cf = cf, cash = 0, mult = 1, droppers = 1, coins = 0,
		bought = {}, pads = {}, dead = false,
	}
	local concrete = Color3.fromRGB(38, 42, 52)
	newPart(model, "Base", Vector3.new(40, 1, 28), cf * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0), concrete, Enum.Material.Concrete)
	for _, e in ipairs({ { 0, -13.8, 40.4, 0.5 }, { 0, 13.8, 40.4, 0.5 } }) do
		newPart(model, "Trim", Vector3.new(e[3], 0.2, e[4]), cf * CFrame.new(e[1], 1.05, e[2]), GREEN, Enum.Material.Neon, { CanCollide = false })
	end
	local belt = newPart(model, "Conveyor", Vector3.new(32, 0.6, 3.6), cf * CFrame.new(-1, 1.3, -6), Color3.fromRGB(24, 26, 32), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
	belt.AssemblyLinearVelocity = cf:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(8, 0, 0))
	for i = -1, 1, 2 do
		newPart(model, "Rail", Vector3.new(32, 1.2, 0.4), cf * CFrame.new(-1, 1.6, -6 + i * 2), Color3.fromRGB(70, 74, 88), Enum.Material.Metal)
	end
	newPart(model, "BinFloor", Vector3.new(5, 0.5, 6), cf * CFrame.new(17.5, 1.25, -6), Color3.fromRGB(30, 32, 40), Enum.Material.Metal)
	newPart(model, "BinWall", Vector3.new(0.5, 3, 6), cf * CFrame.new(19.8, 2.5, -6), Color3.fromRGB(58, 62, 76), Enum.Material.Metal)
	newPart(model, "BinGlow", Vector3.new(4.4, 0.2, 5.4), cf * CFrame.new(17.3, 1.55, -6), GREEN, Enum.Material.Neon, { CanCollide = false })
	local trigger = newPart(model, "Collector", Vector3.new(5, 4, 6), cf * CFrame.new(17.2, 3.4, -6), GREEN, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, { Transparency = 1, CanCollide = false })
	trigger.Touched:Connect(function(part)
		local value = part:GetAttribute("CashValue")
		if value and part.Parent and not st.dead then
			part:SetAttribute("CashValue", nil)
			part:Destroy()
			st.cash += value
			refreshDisplays(st)
			FX:FireClient(player, "Cash", trigger.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), value)
		end
	end)
	addDropper(st, 1)

	newPart(model, "SignPost", Vector3.new(0.8, 5, 0.8), cf * CFrame.new(-8, 3.5, 11), Color3.fromRGB(58, 62, 76), Enum.Material.Metal)
	newPart(model, "SignPost", Vector3.new(0.8, 5, 0.8), cf * CFrame.new(8, 3.5, 11), Color3.fromRGB(58, 62, 76), Enum.Material.Metal)
	local sign = newPart(model, "Sign", Vector3.new(18, 5, 0.6), cf * CFrame.new(0, 7, 11), Color3.fromRGB(16, 18, 26), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
	local sg = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
	sg.Face = Enum.NormalId.Back
	sg.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.PixelsPerStud
	sg.PixelsPerStud = 40
	sg.Parent = sign
	local function label(text, y, h, size, color)
		local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		l.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, y)
		l.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, h)
		l.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
		l.TextScaled = true
		l.TextColor3 = color
		l.Text = text
		l.Parent = sg
		return l
	end
	label(string.upper(player.DisplayName) .. "'S TYCOON", 0.06, 0.24, 1, Color3.fromRGB(190, 196, 215))
	st.cashLabel = label("CASH  $0", 0.32, 0.38, 1, GREEN)
	st.rateLabel = label("", 0.72, 0.2, 1, Color3.fromRGB(150, 158, 178))

	for i, up in ipairs(UPGRADES) do
		local x = -17.15 + (i - 1) * 4.9
		local part = newPart(model, "Pad_" .. up.id, Vector3.new(4.2, 0.5, 4.2), cf * CFrame.new(x, 1.25, 6), Color3.fromRGB(110, 114, 130), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
		local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(110, 44)
		gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
		gui.MaxDistance = 70
		gui.Adornee = part
		gui.Parent = part
		local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
		title.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
		title.TextSize = 12
		title.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		title.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
		title.TextWrapped = true
		title.Text = up.label
		title.Parent = gui
		local price = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		price.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		price.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 24)
		price.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
		price.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
		price.TextSize = 14
		price.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 110)
		price.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
		price.Parent = gui
		local prompt = Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
		prompt.ActionText = "Buy"
		prompt.ObjectText = up.label
		prompt.KeyboardKeyCode = Enum.KeyCode.F
		prompt.HoldDuration = 0.2
		prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 9
		prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
		prompt.Parent = part
		prompt.Triggered:Connect(function(plr)
			if plr == player then
				buy(st, up)
			else
				Core.notify(plr, "This is not your tycoon", false)
			end
		end)
		st.pads[up.id] = { part = part, price = price, prompt = prompt }
	end

	local btn = newPart(model, "DropButton", Vector3.new(4.4, 0.9, 4.4), cf * CFrame.new(-12, 1.45, 1.5), Color3.fromRGB(60, 200, 120), Enum.Material.Neon)

modules.Tycoon = {
	e = function(player, char, hum, hrp)
		if tycoons[player] then
			Core.notify(player, "Your tycoon is already built. Press Q to remove it", false)
			return
		end
		if onCooldown(player) then
			return
		end
		local cf, err = tryPlace(char, hum, hrp)
		if not cf then
			Core.notify(player, err, false)
			return
		end
		setCooldown(player, 2)
		buildTycoon(player, cf)
		player:SetAttribute("XActive", true)
	end,
	q = function(player)
		if tycoons[player] then
			removeTycoon(player, true)
			setCooldown(player, 2)
		end
	end,
	off = function(player)
		removeTycoon(player, true)
	end,
}

local glory = {}

local function gloryOff(player)
	local st = glory[player]
	if not st then
		return
	end
	glory[player] = nil
	st.alive = false
	for _, o in ipairs(st.items) do
		if o.Parent then
			o:Destroy()
		end
	end
	for d, v in pairs(st.store) do
		if d.Parent then
			d.Color = v[1]
			d.Material = v[2]
		end
	end
	if st.char.Parent then
		st.char:SetAttribute("KickImmune", false)
	end
	player:SetAttribute("XActive", false)
	if not balls[player] and not ghosts[player] then
		player:SetAttribute("NoKick", false)
	end
end

-- DISPATCH
local lastAct = {}
Ext.Action.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, key)
	if key ~= "E" and key ~= "Q" then
		return
	end
	local t = os.clock()
	if lastAct[player] and t - lastAct[player] < 0.25 then
		return
	end
	lastAct[player] = t
	if player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") ~= true then
		return
	end
	local char, hum, hrp = Core.getAlive(player)
	local mod = modules[player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick")]
	if not char or not mod then
		return
	end
	if char:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") or char:GetAttribute("Frozen") or player:GetAttribute("StoneActive") then
		return
	end
	local fn = key == "E" and mod.e or mod.q
	if fn then
		fn(player, char, hum, hrp)
	end
end)

local function sync(player)
	local kick = player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick")
	local active = player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true
	for name, mod in pairs(modules) do
		if mod.off and not (active and kick == name) and not (name == "Smile" and player:GetAttribute("ForceSmile") == true) then
			mod.off(player)
		end
	end
	if active and modules[kick] and modules[kick].on then
		modules[kick].on(player)
	end
	local c = cds[player] and cds[player][kick]
	player:SetAttribute("XCooldownEnd", c and c.endAt or 0)
	player:SetAttribute("XCooldown", c and c.total or 1)
end

local function hook(player)
	player:SetAttribute("XActive", false)
	player:SetAttribute("NoKick", false)
	player:SetAttribute("KickPower", 1)
	player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("EquippedKick"):Connect(function()
		sync(player)
	end)
	player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("ArenaActive"):Connect(function()
		sync(player)
	end)
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hook)
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	hook(p)
end

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	removeTycoon(player, false)
	ballOff(player)
	vanishOff(player)
	specOff(player)
	gloryOff(player)
	if modules.Mirror.off then
		modules.Mirror.off(player)
	end
	cds[player] = nil
	lastAct[player] = nil
end)

	Core = _G.KickArenaCore

		task.wait(0.2)
	end

_G.KickArenaExt = Ext

local function mkRemote(class, name)
	local r = Instance.new(class)
	r.Name = name
	r.Parent = remotes
	return r
end
local FX = mkRemote("RemoteEvent", "ExtraFX")
local ActionRemote = mkRemote("RemoteEvent", "ExtraAction")
local AdminRemote = mkRemote("RemoteFunction", "ExtraAdmin")
Ext.FX = FX
Ext.Action = ActionRemote

local function now()
	return Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
end

local function rootOf(model)
	return model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
end
Ext.now = now
Ext.rootOf = rootOf

local function allTargets()
	local list = {}
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local c = p.Character
		if c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
			table.insert(list, c)
		end
	end
	local d = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Dummies")
	if d then
		for _, m in ipairs(d:GetChildren()) do
			if m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
				table.insert(list, m)
			end
		end
	end
	return list
end
Ext.allTargets = allTargets

local tracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function refreshMove(model)
	local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not hum then
		tracked[model] = nil
		return
	end
	local t = tracked[model]
	local stunned = (model:GetAttribute("StunUntil") or 0) > now()
	local sm = model:GetAttribute("SpeedMult") or 1
	local jm = model:GetAttribute("JumpMult") or 1
	if not (stunned or sm ~= 1 or jm ~= 1) then
		if t then
			hum.WalkSpeed = t.ws
			hum.JumpPower = t.jp
			hum.JumpHeight = t.jh
			tracked[model] = nil
		end
		return
	end
	if not t then
		t = {
			ws = hum.WalkSpeed > 0 and hum.WalkSpeed or 16,
			jp = hum.JumpPower > 0 and hum.JumpPower or 50,
			jh = hum.JumpHeight > 0 and hum.JumpHeight or 7.2,
		}
		tracked[model] = t
	end
	if stunned then
		hum.WalkSpeed = 0
		hum.JumpPower = 0
		hum.JumpHeight = 0
	else
		hum.WalkSpeed = t.ws * sm
		hum.JumpPower = t.jp * jm
		hum.JumpHeight = t.jh * jm
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(0.25)
		for model in pairs(tracked) do
			refreshMove(model)
		end
	end
end)

function Ext.stun(model, dur)
	model:SetAttribute("StunUntil", math.max(model:GetAttribute("StunUntil") or 0, now() + dur))
	refreshMove(model)
end

function Ext.setMult(model, speed, jump)
	model:SetAttribute("SpeedMult", speed)
	model:SetAttribute("JumpMult", jump)
	refreshMove(model)
end

local PRIORITY = { "Gold", "Scorch", "Paint", "Rainbow" }
local looks = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function renderLook(model)
	local L = looks[model]
	if not L then
		return
	end
	local t = os.clock()
	local top = nil
	for _, name in ipairs(PRIORITY) do
		local layer = L.layers[name]
		if layer and layer.expire <= t then
			L.layers[name] = nil
			layer = nil
		end
		if layer and not top then
			top = layer
		end
	end
	for part, base in pairs(L.base) do
		if part.Parent then
			if top then
				part.Color = type(top.color) == "function" and top.color(part) or top.color
				part.Material = top.material
				part.Reflectance = top.refl
			else
				part.Color, part.Material, part.Reflectance = base[1], base[2], base[3]
			end
		end
	end
	if not top then
		looks[model] = nil
	end
end

local function applyLayer(model, name, color, material, refl, dur)
	if Core.isStoneForm(model) or model:GetAttribute("Frozen") == true then
		return
	end
	local L = looks[model]
	if not L then
		L = { base = {}, layers = {} }
		for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
				L.base[d] = { d.Color, d.Material, d.Reflectance }
			end
		end
		looks[model] = L
	end
	L.layers[name] = { color = color, material = material, refl = refl, expire = os.clock() + dur }
	renderLook(model)
	task.delay(dur + 0.05, renderLook, model)
end
Ext.applyLayer = applyLayer

local function randomDir()
	local v = Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, 0, math.random() - 0.5)
	return v.Magnitude > 0.05 and v.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
end

local GOLD = Color3.fromRGB(255, 196, 40)

function Ext.gold(model, dur)
	applyLayer(model, "Gold", GOLD, Enum.Material.Metal, 0.35, dur)
	Ext.stun(model, dur)
	FX:FireAllClients("Gold", model, dur)
end

function Ext.onStone(player)
	if math.random() >= math.random(1, 5) / 100 then
		return
	end
	task.wait(0.45)
	local char = player.Character
	if not char or player:GetAttribute("StoneActive") ~= true then
		return
	end
	local saved = {}
	for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
			saved[d] = d.Reflectance
			d.Color = GOLD
			d.Material = Enum.Material.Metal
			d.Reflectance = 0.3
		end
	end
	FX:FireAllClients("GoldForm", char)
	Core.notify(player, "Your stone body turned into solid gold!", true)
	Core.unlockKick(player, "Gold")
	local conn
	conn = player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("StoneActive"):Connect(function()
		if player:GetAttribute("StoneActive") ~= true then
			conn:Disconnect()
			for d, r in pairs(saved) do
				if d.Parent then
					d.Reflectance = r
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

local RAIN_CHANCE = 0.01
local STRIKE_CHANCE = 0.005
local RAIN_MIN, RAIN_MAX = 25, 40
local RAIN_PAUSE = 45
local raining = false
local nextRain = 0
Workspace:SetAttribute("KickRain", false)

local function sheltered(model)
	local root = rootOf(model)
	if not root then
		return true
	end
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { model }
	local hit = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), Vector3.new(0, 150, 0), params)
	return hit ~= nil and hit.Instance.CanCollide
end

local function scorch(model)
	applyLayer(model, "Scorch", Color3.fromRGB(26, 24, 24), Enum.Material.Slate, 0, 6)
	local root = rootOf(model)
	if root then
		local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
		att.Parent = root
		local smoke = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
		smoke.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
		smoke.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45))
		smoke.Size = NumberSequence.new(1.2, 2.6)
		smoke.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.35, 1)
		smoke.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1, 1.6)
		smoke.Speed = NumberRange.new(2, 4)
		smoke.Rate = 28
		smoke.Parent = att
		task.delay(5, function()
			smoke.Enabled = false
		end)
		task.delay(7, function()
			att:Destroy()
		end)
	end
end

function Ext.strike(model, plr, helped)
	local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local root = rootOf(model)
	if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 or Core.isStoneForm(model) then
		return false
	end
	FX:FireAllClients("Bolt", root.Position)
	scorch(model)
	Core.ragdoll(model, plr, randomDir(), 45)
	if plr and plr.Parent then
		Core.notify(plr, helped and "A shock called the storm down on you!" or "You were struck by lightning!", false)
		Core.unlockKick(plr, "Lightning")
	end
	return true
end

function Ext.shock(model, dur, plr)
	Ext.stun(model, dur)
	FX:FireAllClients("Shock", model, dur)
	if Workspace:GetAttribute("KickRain") then
		task.delay(0.2, Ext.strike, model, plr, true)
	end
end

function Ext.startRain(dur)
	if raining then
		return false, "It is already raining"
	end
	raining = true
	Workspace:SetAttribute("KickRain", true)
	local t0 = os.clock()
	task.spawn(function()
		while os.clock() - t0 < dur do
			task.wait(1)
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local char = p.Character
				if char and p:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true and math.random() < STRIKE_CHANCE and not sheltered(char) then
					Ext.strike(char, p, false)
				end
			end
		end
		raining = false
		nextRain = os.clock() + RAIN_PAUSE
		Workspace:SetAttribute("KickRain", false)
	end)
	return true, "Rain started"
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(1)
		if not raining and os.clock() >= nextRain and math.random() < RAIN_CHANCE then
			Ext.startRain(math.random(RAIN_MIN, RAIN_MAX))
		end
	end
end)

local PASS_PAY = 2094142758

local function applyPass(player, id)
	if id == PASS_DOUBLE then
		player:SetAttribute("PassKickMult", 2)
		player:SetAttribute("OwnsDouble", true)
	elseif id == PASS_VIP then
		player:SetAttribute("IsVIP", true)
	elseif id == PASS_PAY then
		player:SetAttribute("OwnsPay", true)
	end
end

local function loadPasses(player)
	for _, id in ipairs({ PASS_DOUBLE, PASS_VIP, PASS_PAY }) do
		local ok, owns = pcall(MarketplaceService.UserOwnsGamePassAsync, MarketplaceService, player.UserId, id)
		if ok and owns then
			applyPass(player, id)
		end
	end
end

MarketplaceService.PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished:Connect(function(player, id, purchased)
	if purchased then
		applyPass(player, id)
		Core.notify(player, id == PASS_VIP and "VIP unlocked! Welcome to the club" or id == PASS_PAY and "Pay Kick unlocked!" or "2x Kicks unlocked!", true)
	end
end)

		Workspace:SetAttribute("PaintingTime", false)
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			Core.notify(p, "Painting Time is over", true)
			Core.unlockKick(p, "Painting")
		end
	end)
	return true, "Painting Time started for 1 minute"
end

local mirrorProp = nil
local enterMirror

local function removeMirrorProp(shatter)
	if mirrorProp then
		if shatter then
			FX:FireAllClients("MirrorShatter", mirrorProp:GetPivot().Position)
		end
		mirrorProp:Destroy()
		mirrorProp = nil
	end
end

local function bw(parent, name, size, cf, shade, material, extra)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	p.Name = name
	p.Size = size
	p.CFrame = cf
	p.Anchored = true
	p.CanCollide = false
	p.Material = material or Enum.Material.Wood
	p.Color = Color3.fromRGB(shade, shade, shade)
	for k, v in pairs(extra or {}) do
		p[k] = v
	end
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

-- Creepy black and white standing mirror. lean = CFrame at the bottom center, front (-Z) faces the viewer.
-- Returns the glass pane and the ghost frame (flickers)
function Ext.buildMirror(model, lean, paneName, solid)
	local function at(x, y, z)
		return lean * CFrame.new(x, y + 4.7, z)
	end
	local pane = bw(model, paneName, Vector3.new(5, 8.4, 0.3), at(0, 0, 0), 14, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, {
		CanCollide = solid, CanTouch = true,
	})
	-- thick dark carved frame
	bw(model, "FrameTop", Vector3.new(6.8, 0.9, 0.9), at(0, 4.65, 0), 12)
	bw(model, "FrameBottom", Vector3.new(6.8, 1.0, 0.9), at(0, -4.7, 0), 12)
	bw(model, "FrameLeft", Vector3.new(0.9, 10.2, 0.9), at(-2.95, 0, 0), 12)
	bw(model, "FrameRight", Vector3.new(0.9, 10.2, 0.9), at(2.95, 0, 0), 12)
	bw(model, "TrimTop", Vector3.new(5.2, 0.25, 1.1), at(0, 4.3, 0.02), 70, Enum.Material.Slate)
	bw(model, "TrimBottom", Vector3.new(5.2, 0.25, 1.1), at(0, -4.3, 0.02), 70, Enum.Material.Slate)
	bw(model, "TrimLeft", Vector3.new(0.25, 8.6, 1.1), at(-2.55, 0, 0.02), 70, Enum.Material.Slate)
	bw(model, "TrimRight", Vector3.new(0.25, 8.6, 1.1), at(2.55, 0, 0.02), 70, Enum.Material.Slate)
	-- gothic crown with spikes
	bw(model, "Crown", Vector3.new(3, 1.3, 0.9), at(0, 5.8, 0), 16)
	bw(model, "CrownSpike", Vector3.new(1, 1, 0.9), at(0, 6.9, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(45)), 16)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -2.6, 2.6 }) do
		bw(model, "Spike", Vector3.new(0.6, 1.7, 0.7), at(sx, 5.7, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(sx < 0 and 12 or -12)), 16)
		bw(model, "Foot", Vector3.new(1.3, 0.9, 1.3), at(sx * 1.1, -4.9, 0), 10)
	end
	bw(model, "Chip", Vector3.new(0.7, 0.7, 0.95), at(2.95, -1.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(35)), 40, Enum.Material.Slate)
	-- the glass image: pale smoky surface, cracks, and a figure that appears and disappears
	local sg = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
	sg.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
	sg.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.PixelsPerStud
	sg.PixelsPerStud = 40
	sg.LightInfluence = 0
	sg.Parent = pane
	local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
	bg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 190)
	bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
	bg.Parent = sg
	local g1 = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	g1.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120)),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)),
	})
	g1.Rotation = 70
	g1.Parent = bg
	local figure = Instance.new("Frame")
	figure.Name = "Figure"
	figure.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	figure.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	figure.Parent = sg
	local function blob(x, y, w, h, round)
		local f = Instance.new("Frame")
		f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		f.Position = UDim2.fromScale(x, y)
		f.Size = UDim2.fromScale(w, h)
		f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(4, 4, 4)
		f.BorderSizePixel = 0
		f.Parent = figure
		if round then
			Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		end
		return f
	end
	blob(0.5, 0.2, 0.17, 0.12, true)
	blob(0.5, 0.31, 0.32, 0.5, false)
	blob(0.3, 0.33, 0.07, 0.4, false)
	blob(0.7, 0.33, 0.07, 0.4, false)
	for _, ex in ipairs({ 0.46, 0.54 }) do
		local eye = blob(ex, 0.235, 0.025, 0.02, true)
		eye.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	end
	for _, c in ipairs({
		{ 0.26, 0.2, 0.5, -38 }, { 0.62, 0.42, 0.4, 52 }, { 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 14 }, { 0.78, 0.2, 0.3, -66 }, { 0.2, 0.7, 0.3, 74 },
	}) do
		local crack = Instance.new("Frame")
		crack.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		crack.Position = UDim2.fromScale(c[1], c[2])
		crack.Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, c[3], 0)
		crack.Rotation = c[4]
		crack.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(250, 250, 250)
		crack.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
		crack.BorderSizePixel = 0
		crack.Parent = sg
	end
	local vignette = Instance.new("Frame")
	vignette.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	vignette.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	vignette.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
	vignette.BorderSizePixel = 0
	vignette.Parent = sg
	local g2 = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	g2.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0.85), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0.85), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
	})
	g2.Parent = vignette
	local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
	att.Position = Vector3.new(0, -4, 0.8)
	att.Parent = pane
	local mist = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	mist.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
	mist.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
	mist.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 5) })
	mist.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.92), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 0.82), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
	mist.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(2.5, 4)
	mist.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.4, 1.2)
	mist.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(70, 15)
	mist.Rate = 7
	mist.Parent = att
	local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
	light.Color = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
	light.Range = 14
	light.Brightness = 1.1
	light.Parent = pane
	task.spawn(function()
		while model.Parent do
			task.wait(0.12 + math.random() * 0.8)
			light.Brightness = math.random() < 0.3 and 0.15 or 1.1
			figure.Visible = math.random() < 0.55
		end
	end)
	return pane
end

local function findLean(origin, ignore)
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
	local best = nil
	for i = 0, 7 do
		local a = i * math.pi / 4
		local dir = Vector3.new(math.cos(a), 0, math.sin(a))
		local r = Workspace:Raycast(origin + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), dir * 11, params)
		if r and math.abs(r.Normal.Y) < 0.5 and (not best or r.Distance < best.Distance) then
			best = r
		end
	end
	return best
end

function Ext.spawnMirrorProp(player)
	local char, _, hrp = Core.getAlive(player)
	if not char then
		return false, "You must be alive"
	end
	removeMirrorProp(false)
	local look = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
	local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
	flat = flat.Magnitude > 0.1 and flat.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
	local top = hrp.Position + flat * 14 + Vector3.new(0, 12, 0)
	local hit = Workspace:Raycast(top, Vector3.new(0, -40, 0), params)
	if not hit then
		return false, "No ground in front of you"
	end
	local ground = hit.Position
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "MysteryMirror"
	local TILT, DEPTH = 11, 2.9
	local wall = findLean(ground, { char })
	local face
	local base
	if wall then
		local n = Vector3.new(wall.Normal.X, 0, wall.Normal.Z).Unit
		face = n
		base = Vector3.new(wall.Position.X, ground.Y, wall.Position.Z) + n * DEPTH
	else
		face = -flat
		base = ground + flat * 2
		local slabCenter = base - face * (DEPTH + 0.75) + Vector3.new(0, 6.5, 0)
		bw(model, "LeanSlab", Vector3.new(11, 13, 1.5), CFrame.lookAt(slabCenter, slabCenter + face), 22, Enum.Material.Slate, { CanCollide = true })
		bw(model, "LeanSlabCrack", Vector3.new(0.4, 6, 1.6), CFrame.lookAt(slabCenter + Vector3.new(2.4, 2, 0), slabCenter + Vector3.new(2.4, 2, 0) + face) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(15)), 8, Enum.Material.Slate)
	end
	local lean = CFrame.lookAt(base, base + face) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(TILT), 0, 0)
	local pane = Ext.buildMirror(model, lean, "Pane", true)
	bw(model, "Shadow", Vector3.new(0.1, 10, 10), CFrame.new(base + face * 1.5 + Vector3.new(0, 0.07, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)), 0, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, { Transparency = 0.55, Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, CanTouch = false, CanQuery = false })
	model.PrimaryPart = pane
	model.Parent = Workspace
	mirrorProp = model
	pane.Touched:Connect(function(touch)
		local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(touch.Parent)
		if plr and plr:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true then
			task.spawn(enterMirror, plr)
		end
	end)
	task.delay(120, function()
		if mirrorProp == model then
			removeMirrorProp(true)
		end
	end)
	return true, "A mirror appeared nearby"
end

local entering = {}

function enterMirror(player)
	if entering[player] or not mirrorProp then
		return
	end
	entering[player] = true
	removeMirrorProp(true)
	Core.notify(player, "The mirror pulls you in...", true)
	task.wait(1.2)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		local options = Instance.new("TeleportOptions")
		options:SetTeleportData({ quest = "Mirror", back = game.PlaceId })
		TeleportService:TeleportAsync(MIRROR_PLACE_ID, { player }, options)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[KickArena] Mirror teleport failed: " .. tostring(err))
		Core.notify(player, "The mirror is silent right now. Try again later", false)
	end
	task.wait(5)
	entering[player] = nil
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(60)
		if not mirrorProp and math.random() < 0.10 then
			local list = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if p:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true and Core.getAlive(p) then
					table.insert(list, p)
				end
			end
			if #list > 0 then
				Ext.spawnMirrorProp(list[math.random(#list)])
			end
		end
	end
end)

local function creditKill(model)
	local uid = model:GetAttribute("LastHitBy")
	local at = model:GetAttribute("LastHitAt")
	model:SetAttribute("LastHitBy", nil)
	if uid and at and now() - at <= 10 then
		local killer = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(uid)
		if killer and killer.Parent and killer.UserId ~= (Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) or {}).UserId then
			Core.addKill(killer)
		end
	end
end

local function watchDeath(model, hum)
	hum.Died:Connect(function()
		creditKill(model)
	end)
end

Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
	if inst:IsA("Humanoid") and inst.Parent and inst.Parent:GetAttribute("IsDummy") then
		watchDeath(inst.Parent, inst)
	end
end)

local function hookCharacter(player, char)
	local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
	if not hum then
		return
	end
	watchDeath(char, hum)
end

local function onPlayer(player)
	player:SetAttribute("PassKickMult", 1)
	player:SetAttribute("OwnsDouble", false)
	player:SetAttribute("IsVIP", false)
	player:SetAttribute("OwnsPay", false)
	task.spawn(loadPasses, player)
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
		hookCharacter(player, char)
	end)
	if player.Character then
		task.spawn(hookCharacter, player, player.Character)
	end
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(onPlayer)
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	task.spawn(onPlayer, p)
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(2)
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local data = Core.questData[p]
			local kv = Core.getKicksValue(p)
			if data and kv and kv.Value >= 10000 and not data.Unlocked.Tycoon then
				Core.unlockKick(p, "Tycoon")
			end
		end
	end
end)

AdminRemote.OnServerInvoke = function(player, action)
	if player.UserId ~= OWNER_ID then
		return false, "Not allowed"
	end
	if action == "PaintTime" then
		return Ext.paintTime(60)
	elseif action == "Rain" then
		return Ext.startRain(30)
	elseif action == "Mirror" then
		return Ext.spawnMirrorProp(player)
	end
	return false, "Unknown action"
end

local function new(className, props, children)
	local inst = Instance.new(className)
	for key, value in pairs(props or {}) do
		inst[key] = value
	end
	for _, child in ipairs(children or {}) do
		child.Parent = inst
	end
	return inst
end
local function tween(inst, time, goal, style, direction)
	local t = TweenService:Create(
		inst,
		TweenInfo.new(time, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		goal
	)
	t:Play()
	return t
end
local function corner(radius)
	return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = radius })
end
local function getHumanoid()
	local char = player.Character
	return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end
local function isAlive()
	local hum = getHumanoid()
	return hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0
end
local function isRagdolled()
	local char = player.Character
	return char ~= nil and char:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") == true
end
local function isFrozen()
	local char = player.Character
	return char ~= nil and char:GetAttribute("Frozen") == true
end
local gui = new("ScreenGui", {
	Name = "KickArenaGui",
	ResetOnSpawn = false,
	ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
	Parent = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"),
})
local function playSfx(name, parent, volumeMul)
	local cfg = SFX[name]
	if not cfg or (parent and not parent.Parent) then
		return
	end
	local sound = new("Sound", {
		SoundId = cfg.Id,
		Volume = cfg.Volume * (volumeMul or 1),
		PlaybackSpeed = cfg.Pitch[1] + math.random() * (cfg.Pitch[2] - cfg.Pitch[1]),
		RollOffMinDistance = 10,
		RollOffMaxDistance = 80,
		Parent = parent or gui,
	})
	sound:Play()
	Debris:AddItem(sound, 3)
end
local function emitter(parent, o)
	local e = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	e.Texture = o.Texture or SMOKE
	e.Color = ColorSequence.new(o.Color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
	e.Size = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, o.SizeStart or 1),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, o.SizeEnd or 0),
	})
	e.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, o.TransStart or 0),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, o.TransEnd or 1),
	})
	e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(o.LifeMin or 0.4, o.LifeMax or 0.8)
	e.Speed = NumberRange.new(o.SpeedMin or 5, o.SpeedMax or 10)
	e.SpreadAngle = o.Spread or Vector2.new(180, 180)
	e.Acceleration = o.Accel or Vector3.zero
	e.LightEmission = o.Light or 0
	e.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
	e.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-120, 120)
	e.EmissionDirection = o.Direction or Enum.NormalId.Top
	e.Rate = o.Rate or 0
	e.Parent = parent
	if o.Count then
		e:Emit(o.Count)
	end
	return e
end
local function attachmentAt(position, direction)
	local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
	att.Parent = Workspace.Terrain
	att.WorldCFrame = direction and CFrame.lookAt(position, position + direction) or CFrame.new(position)
	Debris:AddItem(att, 3)
	return att
end
local STONE_GRAY = Color3.fromRGB(128, 130, 138)
local SAND_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(226, 196, 130)

	if st and st.Vfx == false then
		return
	end
	local att = attachmentAt(position, direction)
	if kind == "Normal" then

local function spawnChunk(cf, color, material, baseSize, velocity, transparency)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Vfx == false then
		return
	end
	local s = baseSize * (0.6 + math.random() * 0.8)
	local chunk = new("Part", {
		Size = Vector3.new(s * (0.8 + math.random() * 0.5), s, s * (0.8 + math.random() * 0.5)),
		CFrame = cf * CFrame.Angles(math.random() * 6.28, math.random() * 6.28, math.random() * 6.28),
		Color = color:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), math.random() * 0.25),
		Material = material,
		Transparency = transparency or 0,
			CanCollide = true,
			CanTouch = false,
			CanQuery = false,
			TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
		BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
		Parent = Workspace,
	})
	chunk.AssemblyLinearVelocity = velocity
	chunk.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 20, (math.random() - 0.5) * 20, (math.random() - 0.5) * 20)
	task.delay(1.1, function()
		if chunk.Parent then
			tween(chunk, 0.4, { Transparency = 1 })
		end
	end)
	Debris:AddItem(chunk, 1.6)
end
local function randomVelocity(spread, up)
	return Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * spread, up * (0.6 + math.random() * 0.8), (math.random() - 0.5) * spread)
end
local function novaRing(position, color, radius)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Vfx == false then
		return
	end
	local ring = new("Part", {
		Name = "NovaRing",
		Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder,
		Anchored = true,
		CanCollide = false,
		CanTouch = false,
		CanQuery = false,
		CastShadow = false,
		Material = Enum.Material.Neon,
		Color = color,
		Transparency = 0.35,
		Size = Vector3.new(0.3, 2, 2),
		CFrame = CFrame.new(position - Vector3.new(0, 2.8, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)),
		Parent = Workspace,
	})
	tween(ring, 0.55, { Size = Vector3.new(0.3, radius * 2, radius * 2), Transparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	Debris:AddItem(ring, 0.7)
end
local baseFov = (Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView) or 70
local function punchFov(amount, inTime, outTime)
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not cam then
		return
	end
	tween(cam, inTime, { FieldOfView = baseFov + amount }).Completed:Once(function()
		tween(cam, outTime, { FieldOfView = baseFov })
	end)
end
local shakeToken = 0
local function shake(intensity, duration)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Shake == false then
		return
	end
	local hum = getHumanoid()
	if not hum then
		return
	end
	shakeToken += 1
	local token = shakeToken
	local start = os.clock()
	task.spawn(function()
		while token == shakeToken and hum.Parent and os.clock() - start < duration do
			local fade = 1 - (os.clock() - start) / duration
			hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * intensity * fade, (math.random() - 0.5) * intensity * fade, 0)
			RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
		end
		if token == shakeToken and hum.Parent then
			hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero
		end
	end)
end

local toastHolder = new("Frame", {
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
	Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -22),
	Size = UDim2.fromOffset(330, 200),
	Parent = gui,
}, {
	new("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 8),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
		VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom,
	}),
})
local toastCount = 0
local activeToasts = {}
local function toast(message, kind)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Toasts == false and kind ~= "error" then
		return
	end
	toastCount += 1
	local color = kind == "error" and THEME.Red or kind == "success" and THEME.Green or THEME.Accent
	local scale = new("UIScale", { Scale = 0.9 })
	local group = new("CanvasGroup", {
		LayoutOrder = toastCount,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(330, 44),
		BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Panel,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
		GroupTransparency = 1,
		Parent = toastHolder,
	}, {
		corner(UDim.new(0, 12)),
		new("UIStroke", { Color = color, Transparency = 0.55, Thickness = 1.5 }),
		new("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -16),
			BackgroundColor3 = color,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
		}, { corner(UDim.new(1, 0)) }),
		new("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, 0),
			FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
			TextSize = 14,
			TextColor3 = THEME.Text,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextWrapped = true,
			Text = message,
		}),
		scale,
	})
	table.insert(activeToasts, group)
	while #activeToasts > 3 do
		local oldest = table.remove(activeToasts, 1)
		if oldest then
			oldest:Destroy()
		end
	end
	tween(group, 0.25, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
	tween(scale, 0.3, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	task.delay(3, function()
		if not group.Parent then
			return
		end
		tween(group, 0.35, { GroupTransparency = 1 }).Completed:Once(function()
			group:Destroy()
		end)
	end)
end
local function flash(target, success)
	if not target or not target.Parent then
		return
	end
	local h = new("Highlight", {
		FillColor = success and THEME.Green or THEME.Red,
		OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		FillTransparency = 0.3,
		OutlineTransparency = 0,
		Adornee = target,
		Parent = target,
	})
	tween(h, 0.9, { FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1 })
	Debris:AddItem(h, 1.1)
end
NotifyRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(message, success, target)
	local kind = success == true and "success" or success == false and "error" or "info"
	toast(message, kind)
	playSfx(success and "UISuccess" or "UIError")
	flash(target, success == true)
end)

local function sweepHalf(clipX, circleX)
	local clip = new("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(clipX, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = btn,
	})
	local circle = new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(circleX, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(2, 1),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = clip,
	}, { corner(UDim.new(1, 0)) })
	return new("UIGradient", { Transparency = SWEEP_SEQ, Rotation = 180, Parent = circle }), clip
end
local sweepLeft, clipLeft = sweepHalf(0, 0)
local sweepRight, clipRight = sweepHalf(0.5, -1)
local function setSweep(remaining)
	local a = math.clamp(remaining, 0, 1) * 360
	local first = math.min(a, 180)
	local second = math.max(a - 180, 0)
	clipLeft.Visible = a > 0
	clipRight.Visible = a > 180
	sweepLeft.Rotation = 180 - first
	sweepRight.Rotation = 360 - second
end
setSweep(0)

local function refreshAbility()
	local name = player:GetAttribute("AbilityName") or ""
	local style = ABILITY_STYLE[name]
	holder.Visible = player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true and style ~= nil
	if style then
		abilityColor = style.Color
		abilityTitle.Text = style.Title
		abilityTitle.TextColor3 = style.Color
	end
end
