	btnScale.Scale = 1.1
	tween(btnScale, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
end
local wasCooling = false
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	if not holder.Visible then
		return
	end
	if player:GetAttribute("StoneActive") == true then
		setSweep(0)
		keyLabel.Text = "E"
		keyLabel.TextColor3 = THEME.Text
		abilityState.Text = "TAP TO RELEASE"
		abilityState.TextColor3 = THEME.Green
		ring.Color = THEME.Green
		ring.Transparency = 0.1
		wasCooling = false
		return
	end
	local armedName = player:GetAttribute("AbilityArmed") or ""
	if armedName ~= "" and (player:GetAttribute("AbilityArmedEnd") or 0) > Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() then
		setSweep(0)
		keyLabel.Text = "!"
		keyLabel.TextColor3 = abilityColor
		abilityState.Text = "ARMED  •  KICK!"
		abilityState.TextColor3 = abilityColor
		ring.Color = abilityColor
		ring.Transparency = 0.45 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(os.clock() * 8))
		wasCooling = false
		return
	end
	local remain = (player:GetAttribute("AbilityCooldownEnd") or 0) - Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
	if remain > 0 then
		local total = player:GetAttribute("AbilityCooldown") or ABILITY_COOLDOWN
		setSweep(remain / total)
		keyLabel.Text = remain < 1 and string.format("%.1f", remain) or tostring(math.ceil(remain))
		keyLabel.TextColor3 = THEME.Muted
		abilityState.Text = "RECHARGING"
		abilityState.TextColor3 = THEME.Muted
		ring.Color = abilityColor
		ring.Transparency = 0.7
		wasCooling = true
	else
		setSweep(0)
		keyLabel.Text = "E"
		keyLabel.TextColor3 = THEME.Text
		abilityState.Text = "READY"
		abilityState.TextColor3 = abilityColor
		ring.Color = abilityColor
		ring.Transparency = 0.1
		if wasCooling then
			wasCooling = false
			readyPing()
		end
	end
end)
local function pulseButton()
	btnScale.Scale = 0.88
	tween(btnScale, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
end
local marker = new("Frame", {
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
	Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
	Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30),
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	Visible = false,
	Parent = gui,
})
for _, rot in ipairs({ 45, -45 }) do
	new("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3),
		Rotation = rot,
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = marker,
	}, { corner(UDim.new(1, 0)) })
end
local markerToken = 0
local function showHitMarker()
	markerToken += 1
	local token = markerToken
	marker.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 44)
	marker.Visible = true
	tween(marker, 0.15, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30) })
	task.delay(0.22, function()
		if token == markerToken then
			marker.Visible = false
		end
	end)
end
local function popText(text, color)
	local label = new("TextLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 44),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 30),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
		TextSize = 22,
		TextColor3 = color,
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4,
		Text = text,
		Parent = gui,
	})
	tween(label, 0.7, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 14), TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1 })
	Debris:AddItem(label, 0.8)
end
local function floatDamage(part, damage)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Numbers == false then
		return
	end
	if not part or not part.Parent then
		return
	end
	local startOffset = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 2, 2.6, 0)
	local bb = new("BillboardGui", {
		Adornee = part,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 40),
		StudsOffset = startOffset,
		AlwaysOnTop = true,
		LightInfluence = 0,
		Parent = part,
	})
	local label = new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
		TextSize = 22,
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 96, 96),
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3,
		Text = "-" .. tostring(math.floor(damage + 0.5)),
		Parent = bb,
	})
	tween(bb, 0.8, { StudsOffset = startOffset + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0) })
	tween(label, 0.8, { TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1 })
	Debris:AddItem(bb, 0.9)
end
HitFeedbackRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(hits)
	showHitMarker()
	shake(0.18, 0.15)
	for _, hit in ipairs(hits) do
		floatDamage(hit.Part, hit.Damage)
	end
	local total = 0
	for _, hit in ipairs(hits) do
		total += hit.Kicks or 1
	end
	local crit = false
	for _, hit in ipairs(hits) do
		crit = crit or hit.Crit == true
	end
	popText(crit and ("CRITICAL  +%d"):format(total) or total == 1 and "+1 KICK" or ("+%d KICKS"):format(total), crit and Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80) or THEME.Green)
end)
local IMPACT_COLOR = { Ice = ICE_COLOR, Fire = FIRE_ORANGE, FrostFire = FROST_BLUE, Dizzy = DIZZY_YELLOW, Crazy = CRAZY_RED }
local HIT_SOUND, CRIT_SOUND = "rbxassetid://87240804552787", "rbxassetid://134085140066247"
local DEATH_SOUNDS = { "rbxassetid://137887059021698", "rbxassetid://133312610824902", "rbxassetid://138282778837335" }
local tracked = {}
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	for hum, rec in pairs(tracked) do
		local root = hum.RootPart or (hum.Parent and hum.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
		if root then
			rec.pos = root.Position
		end
	end
end)
local function playDeath(rec, model)
	if rec.played or not rec.pos or model:GetAttribute("IsMirror") then
		return
	end
	rec.played = true
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	local far = not cam or (cam.CFrame.Position - rec.pos).Magnitude > 110
	local snd = new("Sound", {
		SoundId = DEATH_SOUNDS[math.random(#DEATH_SOUNDS)],
		Volume = 1,
		RollOffMinDistance = 12,
		RollOffMaxDistance = 100,
	})
	if far or model == player.Character then
		snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
	else
		snd.Parent = new("Part", {
			Anchored = true,
			CanCollide = false,
			CanQuery = false,
			CanTouch = false,
			Transparency = 1,
			Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2),
			Position = rec.pos,
			Parent = Workspace,
		})
	end
	snd:Play()
	Debris:AddItem(snd.Parent == game:GetService("SoundService") and snd or snd.Parent, 4)
end
local function hookDeath(hum)
	if tracked[hum] then
		return
	end
	local rec = { played = false }
	tracked[hum] = rec
	local model = hum.Parent
	hum.Died:Connect(function()
		playDeath(rec, hum.Parent or model)
	end)
	hum.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
		if not parent then
			if hum.Health <= 0 or (rec.pos and rec.pos.Y < Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 120) then
				playDeath(rec, model)
			end
			tracked[hum] = nil
		end
	end)
end
for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
	if d:IsA("Humanoid") then
		hookDeath(d)
	end
end
Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
	if d:IsA("Humanoid") then
		hookDeath(d)
	end
end)
HitFXRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(part, kickName, inStone, crit)
	if not part or not part.Parent then
		return
	end
	local fx = KICK_FX[kickName] or KICK_FX.Normal
	local position = part.Position
	burstAt(position, fx.Impact)
	if IMPACT_COLOR[fx.Impact] then
		novaRing(position, IMPACT_COLOR[fx.Impact], 8)
		burstAt(position + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0), fx.Impact)
	end
	local snd = new("Sound", {
		SoundId = crit and CRIT_SOUND or HIT_SOUND,
		Volume = (crit and 1.1 or 0.9) * (inStone and 0.6 or 1),
		RollOffMinDistance = 10,
		RollOffMaxDistance = 90,
		Parent = part,
	})
	snd:Play()
	Debris:AddItem(snd, 2)
	if crit then
		novaRing(position, Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60), 10)
		burstAt(position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), "Crazy")
	end
	if fx.Impact == "Stone" then
		for _ = 1, 6 do
			spawnChunk(CFrame.new(position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)), STONE_GRAY, Enum.Material.Slate, 0.4, randomVelocity(22, 14))
		end
	elseif fx.Impact == "Ice" or fx.Impact == "FrostFire" then
		for _ = 1, 6 do
			spawnChunk(CFrame.new(position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)), ICE_COLOR, Enum.Material.Ice, 0.35, randomVelocity(22, 14), 0.25)
		end
	end
end)
RagdollRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(active, direction, power)
	local char = player.Character
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hum or not hrp or hum.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end
	if not active then
		hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
		return
	end
	local waited = 0
	while char:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") ~= true and waited < 0.6 do
		waited += task.wait()
	end
	if not char.Parent or hum.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end
	hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
	local velocity = direction * power + Vector3.new(0, power * 0.45, 0)
	for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
		if part:IsA("BasePart") then
			part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = velocity
		end
	end
	hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 14, (math.random() - 0.5) * 8, (math.random() - 0.5) * 14)
	shake(0.8, 0.35)
	punchFov(6, 0.08, 0.4)
end)
local function onStoneChanged()
	local on = player:GetAttribute("StoneActive") == true
	tween(stoneGrade, 0.4, {
		Saturation = on and -0.75 or 0,
		Contrast = on and 0.12 or 0,
		Brightness = on and -0.04 or 0,
		TintColor = on and Color3.fromRGB(205, 210, 225) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
	})
	if on then
		shake(0.5, 0.35)
		punchFov(-6, 0.15, 0.4)
	else
		shake(0.3, 0.25)
	end
end
local CAST_KIND = { IceCast = "Ice", FireCast = "Fire", FrostFireCast = "FrostFire", DizzyCast = "Dizzy" }
local CAST_COLOR = { Ice = ICE_COLOR, Fire = FIRE_ORANGE, FrostFire = FROST_BLUE, Dizzy = DIZZY_YELLOW }
AbilityFXRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(who, state, targets, radius)
	local char = typeof(who) == "Instance" and (who:IsA("Player") and who.Character or who) or nil
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then
		return
	end
	local castKind = CAST_KIND[state]
	if castKind then
		novaRing(hrp.Position, CAST_COLOR[castKind], radius or 6)
		burstAt(hrp.Position, castKind)
		playSfx(state, hrp)
		if who == player then
			shake(0.35, 0.25)
			punchFov(4, 0.1, 0.3)
		end
	elseif state == "StoneOn" then
		burstAt(hrp.Position, "Stone")
		burstAt(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), "Dust")
		playSfx("StoneOn", hrp)
	elseif state == "StoneOff" then
		burstAt(hrp.Position, "Dust")
		playSfx("StoneOff", hrp)
	end
end)
local legFx = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function getLegParts(char)
	local parts = {}
	for _, name in ipairs({ "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot", "Right Leg" }) do
		local part = char:FindFirstChild(name)
		if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
			table.insert(parts, part)
		end
	end
	return parts
end
local function stopLegFx(char)
	local fx = legFx[char]
	if not fx then
		return
	end
	legFx[char] = nil
	fx.alive = false
	for _, tw in ipairs(fx.tweens) do
		tw:Cancel()
	end
	for _, inst in ipairs(fx.instances) do
		if inst.Parent then
			inst:Destroy()
		end
	end
	for part, saved in pairs(fx.saved) do
		if part.Parent then
			part.Color = saved.Color
			part.Material = saved.Material
		end
	end
end
local function addCracks(parts, container)
	for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
		local sx, sy, sz = part.Size.X, part.Size.Y, part.Size.Z
		for i = 1, 4 do
			local len = sy * (0.35 + math.random() * 0.4)
			local y = (math.random() - 0.5) * sy * 0.6
			local onFront = i <= 3
			local crack = new("Part", {
				Name = "Crack",
				Size = onFront and Vector3.new(0.05, len, 0.025) or Vector3.new(0.025, len, 0.05),
				Color = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28),
				Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
				Transparency = 1,
				CanCollide = false,
				CanTouch = false,
				CanQuery = false,
				Massless = true,
			})
			local offset
			if onFront then
				offset = CFrame.new((math.random() - 0.5) * sx * 0.6, y, -sz / 2 - 0.01) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad((math.random() - 0.5) * 70))
			else
				offset = CFrame.new(sx / 2 + 0.01, y, (math.random() - 0.5) * sz * 0.6) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad((math.random() - 0.5) * 70), 0, 0)
			end
			crack.CFrame = part.CFrame * offset
			crack.Parent = part
			new("WeldConstraint", { Part0 = part, Part1 = crack, Parent = crack })
			table.insert(container, crack)
		end
	end
end
local function runLegFx(char, kind)
	stopLegFx(char)
	local parts = getLegParts(char)
	if #parts == 0 then
		return
	end
	local style = LEG_STYLE[kind]
	local fx = { alive = true, saved = {}, instances = {}, tweens = {}, emitters = {} }
	legFx[char] = fx
	for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
		fx.saved[part] = { Color = part.Color, Material = part.Material }
		part.Material = style.Material
		table.insert(fx.tweens, tween(part, 0.12, { Color = style.Color }))
	end
	local share = 1 / #parts
	for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
		if kind == "Stone" then
			local dust = emitter(part, { Color = STONE_GRAY, SizeStart = 0.5, SizeEnd = 1.1, TransStart = 0.55, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 2, LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 0.9, Rate = 18 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = dust, base = 18 * share, boost = 45 * share })
			table.insert(fx.instances, dust)
		elseif kind == "Ice" then
			local frost = emitter(part, { Texture = SPARK, Color = ICE_LIGHT, SizeStart = 0.3, SizeEnd = 0.1, TransStart = 0, TransEnd = 0.6, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 3, Light = 0.6, Accel = Vector3.new(0, -8, 0), LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 0.9, Rate = 30 * share })
			local mist = emitter(part, { Color = ICE_COLOR, SizeStart = 0.6, SizeEnd = 1.4, TransStart = 0.6, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 2, LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 0.9, Rate = 10 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = frost, base = 30 * share, boost = 70 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = mist, base = 10 * share, boost = 20 * share })
			table.insert(fx.instances, frost)
			table.insert(fx.instances, mist)
		elseif kind == "Fire" then
			local flame = emitter(part, { Texture = FIRE_TEX, Color = FIRE_ORANGE, SizeStart = 1.1, SizeEnd = 0.2, TransStart = 0.1, TransEnd = 1, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 3, Light = 0.8, Accel = Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), LifeMin = 0.3, LifeMax = 0.6, Rate = 30 * share })
			local embers = emitter(part, { Texture = SPARK, Color = FIRE_RED, SizeStart = 0.25, SizeEnd = 0.1, TransStart = 0, TransEnd = 0.7, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 4, Light = 0.8, Accel = Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 0.9, Rate = 18 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = flame, base = 30 * share, boost = 60 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = embers, base = 18 * share, boost = 36 * share })
			table.insert(fx.instances, flame)
			table.insert(fx.instances, embers)
		elseif kind == "FrostFire" then
			local flame = emitter(part, { Texture = FIRE_TEX, Color = FROST_BLUE, SizeStart = 1.6, SizeEnd = 0.2, TransStart = 0.1, TransEnd = 1, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 4, Light = 1, Accel = Vector3.new(0, 10, 0), LifeMin = 0.35, LifeMax = 0.7, Rate = 70 * share })
			local frost = emitter(part, { Texture = SPARK, Color = ICE_LIGHT, SizeStart = 0.45, SizeEnd = 0.1, TransStart = 0, TransEnd = 0.6, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 5, Light = 0.8, Accel = Vector3.new(0, -8, 0), LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 1, Rate = 50 * share })
			local embers = emitter(part, { Texture = SPARK, Color = FIRE_ORANGE, SizeStart = 0.35, SizeEnd = 0.1, TransStart = 0, TransEnd = 0.7, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 5, Light = 0.8, Accel = Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 1, Rate = 30 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = flame, base = 70 * share, boost = 130 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = frost, base = 50 * share, boost = 100 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = embers, base = 30 * share, boost = 60 * share })
			table.insert(fx.instances, flame)
			table.insert(fx.instances, frost)
			table.insert(fx.instances, embers)
		elseif kind == "Dizzy" then
			local stars = emitter(part, { Texture = SPARK, Color = DIZZY_YELLOW, SizeStart = 0.6, SizeEnd = 0.2, TransStart = 0, TransEnd = 0.7, SpeedMin = 2, SpeedMax = 7, Light = 1, LifeMin = 0.6, LifeMax = 1.1, Rate = 60 * share })
			local glow = emitter(part, { Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 170), SizeStart = 1, SizeEnd = 2.2, TransStart = 0.6, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 2, Light = 0.6, LifeMin = 0.4, LifeMax = 0.8, Rate = 18 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = stars, base = 60 * share, boost = 120 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = glow, base = 18 * share, boost = 36 * share })
			table.insert(fx.instances, stars)
			table.insert(fx.instances, glow)
		elseif kind == "Crazy" then
			local sparks = emitter(part, { Texture = SPARK, Color = CRAZY_RED, SizeStart = 0.5, SizeEnd = 0.1, TransStart = 0, TransEnd = 0.7, SpeedMin = 3, SpeedMax = 10, Light = 0.9, Accel = Vector3.new(0, -20, 0), LifeMin = 0.4, LifeMax = 0.9, Rate = 80 * share })
			local smoke = emitter(part, { Color = Color3.fromRGB(35, 32, 36), SizeStart = 0.9, SizeEnd = 2.2, TransStart = 0.45, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 3, LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 1, Rate = 30 * share })
			local flame = emitter(part, { Texture = FIRE_TEX, Color = CRAZY_RED, SizeStart = 1.3, SizeEnd = 0.2, TransStart = 0.1, TransEnd = 1, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 3, Light = 0.9, Accel = Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), LifeMin = 0.3, LifeMax = 0.6, Rate = 30 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = sparks, base = 80 * share, boost = 160 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = smoke, base = 30 * share, boost = 60 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = flame, base = 30 * share, boost = 60 * share })
			table.insert(fx.instances, sparks)
			table.insert(fx.instances, smoke)
			table.insert(fx.instances, flame)
		else
			local grains = emitter(part, { Texture = SPARK, Color = SAND_COLOR, SizeStart = 0.22, SizeEnd = 0.12, TransStart = 0, TransEnd = 0.5, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 3, Accel = Vector3.new(0, -50, 0), LifeMin = 0.6, LifeMax = 1, Rate = 45 * share })
			local dust = emitter(part, { Color = SAND_COLOR, SizeStart = 0.5, SizeEnd = 1.2, TransStart = 0.6, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 2, LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 0.9, Rate = 10 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = grains, base = 45 * share, boost = 90 * share })
			table.insert(fx.emitters, { emitter = dust, base = 10 * share, boost = 18 * share })
			table.insert(fx.instances, grains)
			table.insert(fx.instances, dust)
		end
	end
	if kind == "Stone" then
		fx.cracks = {}
		addCracks(parts, fx.cracks)
		for _, crack in ipairs(fx.cracks) do
			table.insert(fx.instances, crack)
		end
	end
	local footPart = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
	local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	task.spawn(function()
		local t0 = os.clock()
		local function alive()
			return fx.alive and legFx[char] == fx and char.Parent ~= nil
		end
		local function waitUntil(t)
			local remain = t - (os.clock() - t0)
			if remain > 0 then
				task.wait(remain)
			end
			return alive()
		end
		local function footPosition()
			if not footPart or not footPart.Parent then
				return nil
			end
			local drop = footPart.Name == "Right Leg" and 1 or 0.2
			return footPart.Position - Vector3.new(0, drop, 0)
		end
		local function forward()
			return hrp and hrp.Parent and hrp.CFrame.LookVector or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
		end
		if not waitUntil(0.2) then
			return
		end
		local footPos = footPosition()
		if kind == "Stone" then
			for _, crack in ipairs(fx.cracks) do
				table.insert(fx.tweens, tween(crack, 0.06, { Transparency = 0.05 }))
			end
		end
		if footPos then
			burstAt(footPos, LEG_IMPACT[kind], forward())
		end
		if not waitUntil(0.3) then
			return
		end
		playSfx(LEG_SOUND[kind], footPart)
		for _, item in ipairs(fx.emitters) do
			item.emitter.Rate = item.boost
		end
		while alive() and os.clock() - t0 < 0.95 do
			if kind == "Stone" then
				local part = parts[math.random(#parts)]
				if part.Parent then
					local out = part.CFrame.RightVector * (0.5 + math.random() * 0.3) - part.CFrame.LookVector * (math.random() * 0.4)
					spawnChunk(part.CFrame + out, STONE_GRAY, Enum.Material.Slate, 0.28, randomVelocity(5, 2) + out * 2)
				end
			elseif kind == "Crazy" or kind == "Dizzy" then
				fx.flip = not fx.flip
				for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
					if p.Parent then
						if kind == "Crazy" then
							p.Color = fx.flip and style.Color or Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
						else
							p.Color = fx.flip and style.Color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
						end
					end
				end
			elseif kind == "Ice" or kind == "FrostFire" then
				local part = parts[math.random(#parts)]
				if part.Parent then
					local out = part.CFrame.RightVector * (0.5 + math.random() * 0.3) - part.CFrame.LookVector * (math.random() * 0.4)
					spawnChunk(part.CFrame + out, ICE_COLOR, Enum.Material.Ice, 0.2, randomVelocity(5, 2) + out * 2, 0.2)
				end
			end
			task.wait(0.05)
		end
		if not alive() then
			return
		end
		for _, item in ipairs(fx.emitters) do
			item.emitter.Enabled = false
		end
		if fx.cracks then
			for _, crack in ipairs(fx.cracks) do
				crack:Destroy()
			end
		end
		for part, saved in pairs(fx.saved) do
			if part.Parent then
				part.Material = saved.Material
				table.insert(fx.tweens, tween(part, 0.2, { Color = saved.Color }))
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.3)
		if legFx[char] == fx then
			stopLegFx(char)
		end
	end)
end
local JOINT_NAMES = {
	RightHip = { "RightHip", "Right Hip" },
	LeftHip = { "LeftHip", "Left Hip" },
	RightKnee = { "RightKnee" }, -- R15 only
	LeftKnee = { "LeftKnee" }, -- R15 only
	Spine = { "Waist", "RootJoint" }, -- R15 Waist / R6 RootJoint
	Neck = { "Neck" },
	RightShoulder = { "RightShoulder", "Right Shoulder" },
	LeftShoulder = { "LeftShoulder", "Left Shoulder" },
}
local POSE_WINDUP = {
	RightHip = { -55, 0, 0 },
	RightKnee = { -75, 0, 0 },
	LeftHip = { 8, 0, 0 },
	LeftKnee = { -12, 0, 0 },
	Spine = { 6, -14, 0 },
	RightShoulder = { 35, 0, 0 },
	LeftShoulder = { -35, 0, 0 },
}
local POSE_STRIKE = {
	RightHip = { 85, 0, 0 },
	RightKnee = { -8, 0, 0 },
	LeftHip = { -5, 0, 0 },
	LeftKnee = { -18, 0, 0 },
	Spine = { -12, 16, 0 },
	RightShoulder = { -45, 0, 0 },
	LeftShoulder = { 45, 0, 0 },
}
local POSE_FOLLOW = {
	RightHip = { 100, 0, 0 },
	RightKnee = { -22, 0, 0 },
	LeftHip = { -8, 0, 0 },
	LeftKnee = { -22, 0, 0 },
	Spine = { -18, 20, 0 },
	RightShoulder = { -55, 0, 0 },
	LeftShoulder = { 55, 0, 0 },
}
local CRAZY_WIND = {
	RightHip = { -75, 0, 20 }, RightKnee = { -120, 0, 0 }, LeftHip = { 20, 0, -15 }, LeftKnee = { -30, 0, 0 },
	Spine = { 14, -55, 18 }, Neck = { 12, 35, -28 }, RightShoulder = { -160, 0, 60 }, LeftShoulder = { 70, 0, -70 },
}
local CRAZY_STRIKE = {
	RightHip = { 115, 0, -10 }, RightKnee = { -2, 0, 0 }, LeftHip = { -20, 0, 15 }, LeftKnee = { -40, 0, 0 },
	Spine = { -22, 65, -20 }, Neck = { -15, -40, 30 }, RightShoulder = { 60, 0, -50 }, LeftShoulder = { -170, 0, 70 },
}
local CRAZY_FOLLOW = {
	RightHip = { 140, 0, -25 }, RightKnee = { -35, 0, 0 }, LeftHip = { -30, 0, 25 }, LeftKnee = { -60, 0, 0 },
	Spine = { -30, 110, -30 }, Neck = { -25, -60, 40 }, RightShoulder = { 80, 0, -70 }, LeftShoulder = { -180, 0, 90 },
}
local CRAZY_SET = { CRAZY_WIND, CRAZY_STRIKE, CRAZY_FOLLOW }
local W = KICK_TIME.Windup
local S = KICK_TIME.Strike
local H = KICK_TIME.Hold
local R = KICK_TIME.Recover
local TOTAL = W + S + H + R
local ZERO = { 0, 0, 0 }
local function restOf(motor)
	local v = motor:GetAttribute("RestC0")
	if not v then
		v = motor.C0
		motor:SetAttribute("RestC0", v)
	end
	return v
end -- original C0 of every motor we touch
local activeKicks = {}
local function sineOut(x)
	return math.sin(x * math.pi / 2)
end
local function quadIn(x)
	return x * x
end
local function quadOut(x)
	return 1 - (1 - x) * (1 - x)
end
local function lerpAngles(a, b, alpha)
	a = a or ZERO
	b = b or ZERO
	return a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * alpha, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * alpha, a[3] + (b[3] - a[3]) * alpha
end
local function samplePose(key, t, set)
	local wind, strike, follow = POSE_WINDUP, POSE_STRIKE, POSE_FOLLOW
	if set then
		wind, strike, follow = set[1], set[2], set[3]
	end
	if t < W then
		return lerpAngles(nil, wind[key], sineOut(t / W))
	elseif t < W + S then
		return lerpAngles(wind[key], strike[key], quadIn((t - W) / S))
	elseif t < W + S + H then
		return lerpAngles(strike[key], follow[key], quadOut((t - W - S) / H))
	elseif t < TOTAL then
		return lerpAngles(follow[key], nil, quadOut((t - W - S - H) / R))
	end
	return 0, 0, 0
end
local function pivotRotate(rest, rx, ry, rz)
	local p = rest.Position
	return CFrame.new(p) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx), math.rad(ry), math.rad(rz)) * (CFrame.new(-p) * rest)
end
local function collectJoints(char)
	local byName = {}
	for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("Motor6D") then
			byName[d.Name] = d
		end
	end
	local joints = {}
	for key, names in pairs(JOINT_NAMES) do
		for _, name in ipairs(names) do
			if byName[name] then
				joints[key] = byName[name]
				break
			end
		end
	end
	return joints
end
local function suppressTracks(char, kick)
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
	if not animator then
		return
	end
	local ok, tracks = pcall(function()
		return animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
	end)
	if not ok then
		return
	end
	for _, track in ipairs(tracks) do
		if kick.tracks[track] == nil then
			kick.tracks[track] = track.WeightTarget
		end
		pcall(function()
			track:AdjustWeight(0, 0.04)
		end)
	end
end
local function restoreTracks(kick)
	for track, weight in pairs(kick.tracks) do
		pcall(function()
			if track.IsPlaying then
				track:AdjustWeight(math.max(weight, 0.01), 0.2)
			end
		end)
	end
	kick.tracks = {}
end
local function finishKick(char, kick)
	restoreTracks(kick)
	for _, motor in pairs(kick.joints) do
		if motor.Parent then
			motor.C0 = restOf(motor)
		end
	end
	if activeKicks[char] == kick then
		activeKicks[char] = nil
		char:SetAttribute("KickAnimating", false)
	end
end
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	local now = os.clock()
	for char, kick in pairs(activeKicks) do
		if not char.Parent then
			activeKicks[char] = nil
		else
			local t = now - kick.t0
			if t >= kick.duration then
				finishKick(char, kick)
			else
				if kick.isLocal and not kick.lunged and t >= W then
					kick.lunged = true
					local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if hrp and not hrp.Anchored then
						hrp:ApplyImpulse(hrp.CFrame.LookVector * hrp.AssemblyMass * LUNGE_POWER)
					end
				end
				suppressTracks(char, kick)
				local amp = kick.shake and kick.shake(t) or 0
				for key, motor in pairs(kick.joints) do
					if motor.Parent then
						local rx, ry, rz = samplePose(key, t, kick.poses)
						if kick.chaos then
							local j = now * 55 + #key
							rx += math.sin(j) * 9
							ry += math.sin(j * 1.3 + 1) * 9
							rz += math.sin(j * 0.7 + 2) * 9
						end
						if amp > 0 and (key == "RightHip" or key == "RightKnee") then
							rx += (math.random() * 2 - 1) * amp
							rz += (math.random() * 2 - 1) * amp * 0.6
						end
						motor.C0 = pivotRotate(restOf(motor), rx, ry, rz)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)
local TRAIL_COLOR = {
	Normal = Color3.fromRGB(235, 238, 245),
	Stone = Color3.fromRGB(150, 154, 165),
	Beach = SAND_COLOR,
	Ice = ICE_COLOR,
	Fire = FIRE_ORANGE,
	FrostFire = FROST_BLUE,
	Dizzy = DIZZY_YELLOW,
	Crazy = CRAZY_RED,
}
local function addKickTrail(char, kickName)
	local foot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
	if not foot then
		return
	end
	local isR6 = foot.Name == "Right Leg"
	local a0 = new("Attachment", { Name = "KickTrailA", Position = Vector3.new(0, isR6 and 1 or 1.1, 0), Parent = foot })
	local a1 = new("Attachment", { Name = "KickTrailB", Position = Vector3.new(0, isR6 and -1 or -0.3, 0), Parent = foot })
	local bright = kickName == "Ice" or kickName == "Fire" or kickName == "FrostFire" or kickName == "Dizzy" or kickName == "Crazy"
	local trail = new("Trail", {
		Attachment0 = a0,
		Attachment1 = a1,
		Lifetime = 0.25,
		MinLength = 0,
		FaceCamera = true,
		LightEmission = bright and 0.9 or 0.4,
		Color = ColorSequence.new(TRAIL_COLOR[kickName] or TRAIL_COLOR.Normal),
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) }),
		Parent = foot,
	})
	task.delay(W + S + H, function()
		trail.Enabled = false
	end)
	Debris:AddItem(trail, 1)
	Debris:AddItem(a0, 1)
	Debris:AddItem(a1, 1)
end
local function playKickOn(char, isLocal, kickName)
	if not char or not char.Parent then
		return
	end
	local joints = collectJoints(char)
	if next(joints) == nil then
		return
	end
	for _, motor in pairs(joints) do
		restOf(motor)
	end
	local fx = KICK_FX[kickName]
	local previous = activeKicks[char]
	if previous then
		restoreTracks(previous)
	end
	char:SetAttribute("KickAnimating", true)
	activeKicks[char] = {
		t0 = os.clock(),
		tracks = {},
		poses = kickName == "Crazy" and CRAZY_SET or nil,
		chaos = kickName == "Crazy",
		joints = joints,
		isLocal = isLocal,
		lunged = false,
		shake = fx and fx.Shake or nil,
		duration = math.max(TOTAL, fx and fx.ShakeEnd or 0),
	}
	local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	task.delay(SWISH_DELAY, playSfx, SWISH_BY_KICK[kickName] or "Swish", hrp)
	addKickTrail(char, kickName)
	if fx and fx.Leg then
		runLegFx(char, fx.Leg)
	end
end
KickVisualRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kicker, kickName)
	if kicker == player or typeof(kicker) ~= "Instance" then
		return
	end
	local char = kicker:IsA("Player") and kicker.Character or kicker -- a Player or a dummy Model
	if char and char.Parent then
		playKickOn(char, false, kickName)
	end
end)
local lastKick = 0
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local TOUCH_CONTROL_MARGIN = 14
local function isOnTouchControl(position)
	local playerGui = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
	local touchGui = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
	local controls = touchGui and touchGui:FindFirstChild("TouchControlFrame")
	if not controls then
		return false
	end
	local inset = GuiService:GetGuiInset()
	local points = { position, position + inset } -- touch coordinates can be with or without the top bar inset
	for _, child in ipairs(controls:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.Visible then
			local pos, size = child.AbsolutePosition, child.AbsoluteSize
			for _, p in ipairs(points) do
				if p.X >= pos.X - TOUCH_CONTROL_MARGIN
					and p.X <= pos.X + size.X + TOUCH_CONTROL_MARGIN
					and p.Y >= pos.Y - TOUCH_CONTROL_MARGIN
					and p.Y <= pos.Y + size.Y + TOUCH_CONTROL_MARGIN
				then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return false
end
local function tryKick()
	if player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") ~= true or player:GetAttribute("StoneActive") == true then
		return
	end
	if not isAlive() or isRagdolled() or isFrozen() or os.clock() - lastKick < KICK_COOLDOWN then
		return
	end
	local target = player:GetMouse().Target
	if target and (target:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector") or (target.Parent and target.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector"))) then
		return
	end
	lastKick = os.clock()
	playKickOn(player.Character, true, player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick") or "Normal")
	punchFov(4, 0.07, 0.25)
	KickRemote:FireServer()
end
local function tryAbility()
	if player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") ~= true or (player:GetAttribute("AbilityName") or "") == "" then
		return
	end
	if not isAlive() or isRagdolled() or isFrozen() then
		return
	end
	if player:GetAttribute("StoneActive") ~= true then
		local remain = (player:GetAttribute("AbilityCooldownEnd") or 0) - Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
		if remain > 0 then
			return
		end
	end
	if (player:GetAttribute("AbilityArmed") or "") ~= "" then
		return -- already armed: kick something
	end
	pulseButton()
	AbilityRemote:FireServer()
end
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
	if processed then
		return
	end
	local t = input.UserInputType
	if t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		if t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and isOnTouchControl(Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)) then
			return
		end
		tryKick()
	elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
		tryAbility()
	end
end)
btn.Activated:Connect(tryAbility)
for _, attr in ipairs({ "ArenaActive", "AbilityName" }) do
	player:GetAttributeChangedSignal(attr):Connect(function()
		refreshAbility()
		refreshArena()
		refreshCard()
	end)
end
for _, attr in ipairs({ "KickDisplay", "KickStrong" }) do
	player:GetAttributeChangedSignal(attr):Connect(refreshCard)
end

player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("EquippedKick"):Connect(function()
	local kickName = player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick")
	local previous = lastEquipped
	lastEquipped = kickName

	local glow = new("Highlight", {
		FillColor = color,
		OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		FillTransparency = 0.45,
		OutlineTransparency = 0.2,
		Parent = char,
	})
	tween(glow, 0.8, { FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1 })
	Debris:AddItem(glow, 0.9)
	punchFov(3, 0.08, 0.3)
end)

	char:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Frozen"):Connect(function()
		if char:GetAttribute("Frozen") == true then
			popText("FROZEN", ICE_COLOR)
			shake(0.3, 0.2)
		end
	end)
end

	watchFrozen(player.Character)
end

local function new(className, props, children)
	local inst = Instance.new(className)
	for key, value in pairs(props or {}) do
		inst[key] = value
	end
	for _, child in ipairs(children or {}) do
		child.Parent = inst
	end
	return inst
end

local function tween(inst, time, goal, style, direction)
	local t = TweenService:Create(
		inst,
		TweenInfo.new(time, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		goal
	)
	t:Play()
	return t
end

local function formatKicks(n)
	if n >= 1e12 then
		return "INF"
	end
	local s = tostring(math.floor(n))
	local out = s:reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse()
	return (out:gsub("^,", ""))
end

local lastCounterValue = nil
local function setCounter(value)
	counterLabel.Text = "Kick: " .. LT .. 'font color="#FFFFFF"' .. GT .. formatKicks(value) .. LT .. "/font" .. GT
	if lastCounterValue and value > lastCounterValue then
		counterScale.Scale = 1.12
		tween(counterScale, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end
	lastCounterValue = value
end

	local status = new("TextLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -6),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 34),
		FontFace = FONT_MED,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = THEME.Muted,
		TextWrapped = true,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		Text = "",
		Parent = panel,
	})

	local order = 0
	local function nextOrder()
		order += 1
		return order
	end

	local pages, tabs = {}, {}
	local function makePage(name)
		local scroll = new("ScrollingFrame", {
			Name = name,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 74),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -114),
			CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
			AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			ScrollBarThickness = 3,
			ScrollBarImageColor3 = THEME.Muted,
			Visible = false,
			Parent = panel,
		}, {
			new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }),
			new("UIPadding", {
				PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 14),
				PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 14),
				PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2),
				PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 14),
			}),
		})
		pages[name] = scroll
		local tab = new("TextButton", {
			Size = UDim2.new(1 / 6, -5, 1, 0),
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 31, 43),
			AutoButtonColor = false,
			FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
			TextSize = 11,
			TextColor3 = THEME.Muted,
			Text = name,
			Parent = tabBar,
		}, { corner(UDim.new(0, 9)) })
		tabs[name] = tab
		return scroll
	end

	local function showPage(name)
		for n, page in pairs(pages) do
			page.Visible = n == name
			tabs[n].BackgroundColor3 = n == name and THEME.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(28, 31, 43)
			tabs[n].TextColor3 = n == name and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or THEME.Muted
		end
		status.Text = ""
	end

	local function sectionLabel(parent, text)
		return new("TextLabel", {
			LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
			FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
			TextSize = 11,
			TextColor3 = THEME.Accent,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			Text = string.upper(text),
			Parent = parent,
		})
	end

	local function noteLabel(parent, text)
		return new("TextLabel", {
			LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			FontFace = FONT_MED,
			TextSize = 11,
			TextColor3 = THEME.Muted,
			TextWrapped = true,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			Text = text,
			Parent = parent,
		})
	end

	local function inputBox(parent, placeholder)
		return new("TextBox", {
			LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 31, 43),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			FontFace = FONT_MED,
			TextSize = 14,
			TextColor3 = THEME.Text,
			PlaceholderText = placeholder,
			PlaceholderColor3 = THEME.Muted,
			Text = "",
			ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			Parent = parent,
		}, {
			corner(UDim.new(0, 8)),
			new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10) }),
		})
	end

	local function actionButton(parent, text, color)
		return new("TextButton", {
			LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
			BackgroundColor3 = color or THEME.Accent,
			AutoButtonColor = true,
			FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
			TextSize = 14,
			TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			Text = text,
			Parent = parent,
		}, { corner(UDim.new(0, 8)) })
	end

	local busy = false
	local function call(remoteName, missingHint, ...)
		if busy then
			return false
		end
		busy = true
		status.Text = "Working..."
		status.TextColor3 = THEME.Muted
		local remote = remotes:FindFirstChild(remoteName)
		local ok, success, message = false, false, missingHint
		if remote then
			ok, success, message = pcall(remote.InvokeServer, remote, ...)
			if not ok then
				success, message = false, "Request failed"
			end
		end
		status.Text = tostring(message or "")
		status.TextColor3 = success and THEME.Green or THEME.Red
		busy = false
		return success == true
	end
	local function hub(...)
		return call("AdminHub", "Missing script: add KickArenaAdmin to ServerScriptService", ...)
	end

	-- EVENT

		globalScope = not globalScope

	local dummyPage = makePage("Dummy")

	local kickLabels = { Normal = "Normal", Stone = "Stone", Beach = "Beach", Ice = "Ice", Fire = "Fire", FrostFire = "Frost Fire", Dizzy = "Dizzy", Crazy = "Crazy", Lightning = "Lightning", Gold = "Gold Stone", Wood = "Wood", Painting = "Painting", SixPack = "Six Pack", Tycoon = "Master Tycoon", God = "God's Kick", Mirror = "Mirror", Smile = "Smile", Devil = "Devil", Wind = "Wind", Spectator = "Spectator", PayKick = "Pay Kick", Joris = "Joris", Error = "Error" }

	local kickToggle = actionButton(dummyPage, "Can kick: OFF", Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 92))
	local abilityToggle = actionButton(dummyPage, "Can use ability: OFF", Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 92))
	sectionLabel(dummyPage, "Actions")
	local spawnButton = actionButton(dummyPage, "Spawn Dummy", Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 100))

	local clearButton = actionButton(dummyPage, "Clear My Dummies", Color3.fromRGB(150, 60, 60))

		call("AdminAction", "Server script is outdated: replace KickArenaServer", ...)
	end

		kickIndex = kickIndex % #kickChoices + 1
		kickPick.Text = "Dummy kick: " .. kickLabels[kickChoices[kickIndex]]

	-- TARGET PICKER (shared by Give and Mails)
	local function buildPicker(parent)
		sectionLabel(parent, "Player (tap to select)")
		local list = new("ScrollingFrame", {
			LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 110),
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 24, 34),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
			AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			ScrollBarThickness = 3,
			Parent = parent,
		}, {
			corner(UDim.new(0, 8)),
			new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.Name }),
			new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6) }),
		})
		local selectedLabel = noteLabel(parent, "Selected: nobody")
		local offlineBox = inputBox(parent, "Or type an offline username")
		local selected = nil
		local rows = {}
		local function paintRows()
			for plr, row in pairs(rows) do
				row.BackgroundColor3 = (selected == plr) and THEME.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(34, 38, 52)
			end
			selectedLabel.Text = "Selected: " .. (selected and selected.DisplayName .. " (@" .. selected.Name .. ")" or "nobody")
		end
		local function addRow(plr)
			if rows[plr] then
				return
			end
			local row = new("TextButton", {
				Name = string.lower(plr.Name),
				Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28),
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 38, 52),
				AutoButtonColor = true,
				FontFace = FONT_MED,
				TextSize = 13,
				TextColor3 = THEME.Text,
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
				Text = "  " .. plr.DisplayName .. "  @" .. plr.Name,
				Parent = list,
			}, { corner(UDim.new(0, 6)) })
			rows[plr] = row
			row.Activated:Connect(function()
				selected = plr
				offlineBox.Text = ""
				paintRows()
			end)
			paintRows()
		end
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			addRow(plr)
		end
		Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(addRow)
		Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
			if rows[plr] then
				rows[plr]:Destroy()
				rows[plr] = nil
			end
			if selected == plr then
				selected = nil
			end
			paintRows()
		end)
		return function()
			local typed = string.gsub(offlineBox.Text, "%s", "")
			if #typed > 0 then
				return typed
			end
			if selected and selected.Parent then
				return { id = selected.UserId }
			end
			return nil
		end
	end
	local function needTarget(getTarget)
		local target = getTarget()
		if not target then
			status.Text = "Pick a player first"
			status.TextColor3 = THEME.Red
		end
		return target
	end
	local function cycler(page, label, values, names, color)
		local index = 1
		local b = actionButton(page, label .. names[values[1]], color or Color3.fromRGB(60, 70, 120))
		b.Activated:Connect(function()
			index = index % #values + 1
			b.Text = label .. names[values[index]]
		end)
		return b, function()
			return values[index]
		end
	end
	local questKicks = { "Dizzy", "Crazy", "FrostFire", "Lightning", "Gold", "Tycoon", "SixPack", "Mirror", "Painting", "Joris", "Error" }
	local questNames = { None = "None", Dizzy = "Dizzy", Crazy = "Crazy", FrostFire = "Frost Fire", Lightning = "Lightning", Gold = "Gold Stone", SixPack = "Six Pack", Mirror = "Mirror", Painting = "Painting", Tycoon = "Master Tycoon", Joris = "Joris", Error = "Error" }

	local useMails = false
	local mailToggle = actionButton(givePage, "Use Mails: OFF", Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 92))
	mailToggle.Activated:Connect(function()
		useMails = not useMails
		mailToggle.Text = "Use Mails: " .. (useMails and "ON" or "OFF")
		mailToggle.BackgroundColor3 = useMails and Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 92)
	end)
	noteLabel(givePage, "ON: the gift arrives in the player's Mails and must be claimed. OFF: delivered instantly (offline players get it when they join).")
	sectionLabel(givePage, "Give Kicks")
	local amountBox = inputBox(givePage, "Amount (e.g. 500) or inf")
	local giveKicksButton = actionButton(givePage, "Give Kicks", Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 100))
	sectionLabel(givePage, "Unlock a kick")
	local _, giftValue = cycler(givePage, "Kick: ", questKicks, questNames)
	local giveKickButton = actionButton(givePage, "Give Kick", Color3.fromRGB(70, 110, 160))
	sectionLabel(givePage, "Delete a kick")
	local _, deleteValue = cycler(givePage, "Kick: ", questKicks, questNames, Color3.fromRGB(110, 60, 60))
	local deleteKickButton = actionButton(givePage, "Delete Kick", Color3.fromRGB(170, 60, 60))
	noteLabel(givePage, "Delete resets the kick's quest progress so it must be earned again. Roblox does not let scripts take a badge out of an inventory.")
	giveKicksButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		local target = needTarget(getGiveTarget)
		if target then
			hub("GiveKicks", target, amountBox.Text, useMails)
		end
	end)
	giveKickButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		local target = needTarget(getGiveTarget)
		if target then
			hub("GiveKick", target, giftValue(), useMails)
		end
	end)
	deleteKickButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		local target = needTarget(getGiveTarget)
		if target then
			hub("DeleteKick", target, deleteValue())
		end
	end)

	local bodyBox = inputBox(mailPage, "Message (max 300)")

	local mailKickList = { "None", "Dizzy", "Crazy", "FrostFire", "Lightning", "Gold", "Tycoon", "SixPack", "Mirror", "Painting", "Joris", "Error" }
	local _, mailKickValue = cycler(mailPage, "Kick gift: ", mailKickList, questNames)

		local kick = mailKickValue()

			title = titleBox.Text,
			body = bodyBox.Text,
			kicks = mailKicksBox.Text,
			kick = kick ~= "None" and kick or "",
		}, { scope = scope, target = target })
	end

		local target = needTarget(getMailTarget)
		if target then
			sendMail("One", target)
		end
	end)

		sendMail("Server")
	end)
	sendGlobal.Activated:Connect(function()
		sendMail("Global")
	end)

	-- BAN
	local banPage = makePage("Ban")
	sectionLabel(banPage, "Ban or unban a player")
	local banName = inputBox(banPage, "Player name (in this server or any)")
	local banTime = inputBox(banPage, "Time: 1 h, 30 m, 2 d, 1 mon, 1 y")
	local banReason = inputBox(banPage, "Reason")
	local banSuggest = noteLabel(banPage, "")
	local banButton = actionButton(banPage, "Ban", Color3.fromRGB(190, 60, 60))
	local unbanButton = actionButton(banPage, "Unban", Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 100))
	noteLabel(banPage, "Leave Time empty for a permanent ban. s sec, m min, h hour, d day, mon month, y year. Indonesian works too: detik, menit, jam, hari, bulan, tahun.")
	banName:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local q = string.lower(banName.Text)
		local names = {}
		if #q > 0 then
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if string.sub(string.lower(p.Name), 1, #q) == q or string.sub(string.lower(p.DisplayName), 1, #q) == q then
					table.insert(names, p.Name)
				end
			end
		end
		banSuggest.Text = #names > 0 and ("In this server: " .. table.concat(names, ", ", 1, math.min(#names, 4))) or ""
	end)
	banButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		hub("Ban", banName.Text, banTime.Text, banReason.Text)
	end)
	unbanButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		hub("Unban", banName.Text)
	end)

	-- WATCH
	local watchPage = makePage("Watch")
	sectionLabel(watchPage, "Spectate (you become hidden)")
	local specToggle = actionButton(watchPage, "Spectate: OFF", Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 92))
	local specRow = new("Frame", { LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = watchPage })
	local specPrev = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 70, 120), FontFace = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Text = "<", Parent = specRow }, { corner(UDim.new(0, 8)) })
	local specNext = new("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 70, 120), FontFace = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Text = ">", Parent = specRow }, { corner(UDim.new(0, 8)) })
	local specName = new("TextLabel", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -112, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, FontFace = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = THEME.Text, TextWrapped = true, Text = "Nobody", Parent = specRow })
	noteLabel(watchPage, "While spectating you are removed from the leaderboard and invisible. Roblox's own player list cannot be edited by scripts.")
	sectionLabel(watchPage, "Find a player in any server")
	local findName = inputBox(watchPage, "Player name")
	local findButton = actionButton(watchPage, "Find Player", Color3.fromRGB(70, 110, 160))
	local joinButton = actionButton(watchPage, "Join Their Server", Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 100))
	local specOn, specIdx = false, 1
	local function specList()
		local list = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local h = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if p ~= player and h and h.Health > 0 then
				table.insert(list, p)
			end
		end
		return list
	end
	local function ownCamera()
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local h = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if cam and h then
			cam.CameraSubject = h
		end
	end
	local function refreshSpec()
		if not specOn then
			return
		end
		local list = specList()
		if #list == 0 then
			specName.Text = "Nobody to watch"
			ownCamera()
			return
		end
		specIdx = ((specIdx - 1) % #list) + 1
		local t = list[specIdx]
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local h = t.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if cam and cam.CameraSubject ~= h then
			cam.CameraSubject = h
		end
		specName.Text = ("%s\n%s"):format(t.DisplayName, tostring(t:GetAttribute("EquippedKick") or "Normal"))
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent do
			task.wait(0.3)
			refreshSpec()
		end
	end)
	specToggle.Activated:Connect(function()
		if hub("Spectate", not specOn) then
			specOn = not specOn
			specToggle.Text = "Spectate: " .. (specOn and "ON" or "OFF")
			specToggle.BackgroundColor3 = specOn and Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 92)
			if specOn then
				refreshSpec()
			else
				specName.Text = "Nobody"
				ownCamera()
			end
		end
	end)
	specPrev.Activated:Connect(function()
		specIdx -= 1
		refreshSpec()
	end)
	specNext.Activated:Connect(function()
		specIdx += 1
		refreshSpec()
	end)
	findButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		hub("Find", findName.Text)
	end)
	joinButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		hub("Join", findName.Text)
	end)

	for name, tab in pairs(tabs) do
		tab.Activated:Connect(function()
			showPage(name)
		end)
	end
	showPage("Event")

	local open = false
	local function setOpen(state)
		open = state
		player:SetAttribute("AdminOpen", state)
		toggle.Text = state and "CLOSE" or "ADMIN"
		if state then
			panel.Visible = true
			panel.GroupTransparency = 1
			panelScale.Scale = 0.92
			tween(panel, 0.2, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
			tween(panelScale, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		else
			tween(panelScale, 0.15, { Scale = 0.92 })
			tween(panel, 0.15, { GroupTransparency = 1 }).Completed:Once(function()
				if not open then
					panel.Visible = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	toggle.Activated:Connect(function()
		setOpen(not open)
	end)
end

		buildAdminPanel()
	end
end

-- KickArenaMotion (LocalScript in StarterPlayerScripts)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local SMOKE = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
local FIRE_TEX = "rbxasset://textures/particles/fire_main.dds"
local ICE_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(170, 220, 255)
local ICE_LIGHT = Color3.fromRGB(225, 244, 255)
local FIRE_ORANGE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 50)
local FIRE_RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 30)
local FROST_BLUE = Color3.fromRGB(90, 190, 255)

local function new(className, props)

local function getHumanoid()
	local char = player.Character

local function isRagdolled()

local function popText(text, color)
	local label = new("TextLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 44),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 30),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
		TextSize = 22,
		TextColor3 = color,
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4,
		Text = text,
		Parent = gui,
	})
	TweenService:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(1.2), { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 4), TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1 }):Play()

end

local function shake() end

local function emitter(parent, o)
	local e = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	e.Texture = o.Texture or SMOKE
	e.Color = ColorSequence.new(o.Color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
	e.Size = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, o.SizeStart or 1),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, o.SizeEnd or 0),
	})
	e.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, o.TransStart or 0),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, o.TransEnd or 1),
	})
	e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(o.LifeMin or 0.4, o.LifeMax or 0.8)
	e.Speed = NumberRange.new(o.SpeedMin or 5, o.SpeedMax or 10)
	e.SpreadAngle = o.Spread or Vector2.new(180, 180)
	e.Acceleration = o.Accel or Vector3.zero
	e.LightEmission = o.Light or 0
	e.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
	e.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-120, 120)
	e.EmissionDirection = o.Direction or Enum.NormalId.Top
	e.Rate = o.Rate or 0
	e.Parent = parent
	return e
end

local function restOf(motor)
	local v = motor:GetAttribute("RestC0")
	if not v then
		v = motor.C0
		motor:SetAttribute("RestC0", v)
	end
	return v
end

local function pivotRotate(rest, rx, ry, rz)
	local p = rest.Position
	return CFrame.new(p) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx), math.rad(ry), math.rad(rz)) * (CFrame.new(-p) * rest)
end

local JOINT_NAMES = {
	RightHip = { "RightHip", "Right Hip" },
	LeftHip = { "LeftHip", "Left Hip" },
	RightKnee = { "RightKnee" },
	LeftKnee = { "LeftKnee" },
	Spine = { "Waist", "RootJoint" },
	Neck = { "Neck" },
	RightShoulder = { "RightShoulder", "Right Shoulder" },
	LeftShoulder = { "LeftShoulder", "Left Shoulder" },
}

local function collectJoints(char)
	local byName = {}
	for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("Motor6D") then
			byName[d.Name] = d
		end
	end
	local joints = {}
	for key, names in pairs(JOINT_NAMES) do
		for _, name in ipairs(names) do
			if byName[name] then
				joints[key] = byName[name]
				break
			end
		end
	end
	return joints
end

local function suppressTracks(char, kick)
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
	if not animator then
		return
	end
	local ok, tracks = pcall(function()
		return animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
	end)
	if not ok then
		return
	end
	for _, track in ipairs(tracks) do
		if kick.tracks[track] == nil then
			kick.tracks[track] = track.WeightTarget
		end
		pcall(function()
			track:AdjustWeight(0, 0.04)
		end)
	end
end

local function restoreTracks(kick)
	for track, weight in pairs(kick.tracks) do
		pcall(function()
			if track.IsPlaying then
				track:AdjustWeight(math.max(weight, 0.01), 0.2)
			end
		end)
	end
	kick.tracks = {}
end

local controlsModule = nil
task.spawn(function()
	local ok, controls = pcall(function()
		local module = player:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("PlayerModule", 15)
		return require(module):GetControls()
	end)
	if ok then
		controlsModule = controls
	end
end)

local function isDizzy()
	local char = player.Character
	return char ~= nil and char:GetAttribute("Dizzy") == true
end

RunService:BindToRenderStep("KickArenaDizzyMove", Enum.RenderPriority.Input.Value + 1, function()
	if not controlsModule or not isDizzy() or isRagdolled() then
		return
	end
	local hum = getHumanoid()
	if not hum then
		return
	end
	local ok, move = pcall(function()
		return controlsModule:GetMoveVector()
	end)
	if ok and typeof(move) == "Vector3" then
		hum:Move(-move, true)
	end
end)

RunService:BindToRenderStep("KickArenaDizzyCam", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function()
	if not isDizzy() then
		return
	end
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if cam then
		local t = os.clock()
		cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.sin(t * 1.7) * 0.02, math.sin(t * 1.3) * 0.015, math.sin(t * 2.1) * 0.08)
	end
end)

			popText("DIZZY! CONTROLS REVERSED", DIZZY_YELLOW)
			shake(0.3, 0.3)
		end
	end)

	watchDizzy(player.Character)
end

local crazyState = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function stopCrazy(char)
	local st = crazyState[char]
	if not st then
		return
	end
	crazyState[char] = nil
	restoreTracks(st)
	for _, motor in pairs(st.joints) do
		if motor.Parent then
			motor.C0 = restOf(motor)
		end
	end
end

local function jit(t, a, b)
	return (math.sin(t * a) + 0.6 * math.sin(t * b + 1.3)) / 1.6
end

local function crazyPose(key, mode, f, t)
	local sin = math.sin
	if mode == "walk" then
		if key == "RightHip" then
			return sin(f) * 55 + sin(f * 2.3) * 12, 0, 8 + sin(f * 1.7) * 10
		elseif key == "LeftHip" then
			return sin(f + 3.8) * 55 + sin(f * 1.9) * 10, 0, -8 - sin(f * 2.1) * 10
		elseif key == "RightKnee" then
			return -(0.5 + 0.5 * sin(f)) * 55, 0, 0
		elseif key == "LeftKnee" then
			return -(0.5 + 0.5 * sin(f + 3.8)) * 55, 0, 0
		elseif key == "RightShoulder" then
			return sin(f * 0.8 + 1) * 120, 0, 25 + sin(f * 1.4) * 35
		elseif key == "LeftShoulder" then
			return sin(f * 1.1 + 2.2) * 120, 0, -25 - sin(f * 1.1) * 35
		elseif key == "Spine" then
			return 8 + sin(f * 2) * 5, sin(f * 0.7) * 22, sin(f * 0.9) * 14
		elseif key == "Neck" then
			return sin(f * 3.1) * 18, sin(f * 1.3) * 25, sin(f * 2.2) * 22
		end
	elseif mode == "air" then
		if key == "RightHip" then
			return -25 + sin(f * 1.7) * 75, 0, 15 + sin(f * 2.3) * 15
		elseif key == "LeftHip" then
			return -25 + sin(f * 1.7 + 3) * 75, 0, -15 - sin(f * 2.1) * 15
		elseif key == "RightKnee" then
			return -(0.5 + 0.5 * sin(f * 1.7)) * 90, 0, 0
		elseif key == "LeftKnee" then
			return -(0.5 + 0.5 * sin(f * 1.7 + 3)) * 90, 0, 0
		elseif key == "RightShoulder" then
			return -150 + sin(f * 2.2) * 40, 0, 45 + sin(f * 3) * 35
		elseif key == "LeftShoulder" then
			return -150 + sin(f * 2.6 + 1) * 40, 0, -45 - sin(f * 2.8) * 35
		elseif key == "Spine" then
			return sin(f) * 12, sin(f * 1.3) * 45, sin(f * 0.9) * 22
		elseif key == "Neck" then
			return sin(f * 3) * 25, sin(f * 1.7) * 35, sin(f * 2.4) * 28
		end
	elseif mode == "sit" then
		if key == "RightHip" then
			return 80 + sin(f * 1.4) * 18, 0, 10 + sin(f * 2) * 10
		elseif key == "LeftHip" then
			return 80 + sin(f * 1.4 + 2.5) * 18, 0, -10 - sin(f * 2.2) * 10
		elseif key == "RightKnee" then
			return -(0.5 + 0.5 * sin(f * 1.4)) * 70, 0, 0
		elseif key == "LeftKnee" then
			return -(0.5 + 0.5 * sin(f * 1.4 + 2.5)) * 70, 0, 0
		elseif key == "RightShoulder" then
			return -110 + sin(f * 2) * 55, 0, 35 + sin(f * 2.7) * 30
		elseif key == "LeftShoulder" then
			return -110 + sin(f * 2.3 + 1) * 55, 0, -35 - sin(f * 2.5) * 30
		elseif key == "Spine" then
			return sin(f * 1.1) * 8, sin(f * 0.8) * 30, sin(f * 1.6) * 18
		elseif key == "Neck" then
			return sin(f * 3.3) * 28, sin(f * 1.1) * 40, sin(f * 2.5) * 30
		end
	else
		if key == "RightShoulder" then
			return jit(t, 2.1, 5.3) * 30, 0, 12 + jit(t, 3.4, 7.1) * 22
		elseif key == "LeftShoulder" then
			return jit(t, 2.7, 6.1) * 30, 0, -12 - jit(t, 3.9, 8.3) * 22
		elseif key == "RightHip" then
			return jit(t, 1.9, 4.7) * 8, 0, 3 + jit(t, 2.5, 6.7) * 5
		elseif key == "LeftHip" then
			return jit(t, 2.3, 5.9) * 8, 0, -3 - jit(t, 3.1, 6.1) * 5
		elseif key == "Spine" then
			return jit(t, 1.7, 4.1) * 7, jit(t, 2.2, 5.7) * 14, jit(t, 2.9, 6.3) * 9
		elseif key == "Neck" then
			return jit(t, 4.1, 9.3) * 20, jit(t, 3.3, 7.7) * 32, jit(t, 5.1, 11.3) * 26
		end
	end
	return 0, 0, 0
end

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local char = plr.Character
		if char then
			local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local active = hum ~= nil
				and hrp ~= nil
				and hum.Health > 0
				and plr:GetAttribute("EquippedKick") == "Crazy"
				and plr:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true
				and char:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") ~= true
				and char:GetAttribute("Frozen") ~= true
				and plr:GetAttribute("StoneActive") ~= true

			if not active then
				stopCrazy(char)
			elseif char:GetAttribute("KickAnimating") ~= true then
				local st = crazyState[char]
				if not st then
					st = { t0 = os.clock(), joints = collectJoints(char), tracks = {} }
					for _, motor in pairs(st.joints) do
						restOf(motor)
					end
					crazyState[char] = st
				end
				suppressTracks(char, st)

				local speed = Vector3.new(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z).Magnitude
				local mode = "idle"
				if hum.Sit then
					mode = "sit"
				elseif hum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then
					mode = "air"
				elseif speed > 2 then
					mode = "walk"
				end
				local t = os.clock() - st.t0
				local f = t * (mode == "walk" and 9 * math.clamp(speed / 16, 0.6, 1.4) or 8)
				for key, motor in pairs(st.joints) do
					if motor.Parent then
						local rx, ry, rz = crazyPose(key, mode, f, t)
						motor.C0 = pivotRotate(restOf(motor), rx, ry, rz)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

local auraState = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function buildAura(hrp, kind)
	local att = new("Attachment", { Name = "KickAura", Position = Vector3.new(0, kind == "Dizzy" and 2.6 or -0.4, 0), Parent = hrp })
	if kind == "Ice" then
		emitter(att, { Texture = SPARK, Color = ICE_LIGHT, SizeStart = 0.4, SizeEnd = 0.1, TransEnd = 0.7, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 2.5, Light = 0.7, Accel = Vector3.new(0, -3, 0), LifeMin = 0.8, LifeMax = 1.4, Rate = 18 })
		emitter(att, { Color = ICE_COLOR, SizeStart = 1.5, SizeEnd = 3, TransStart = 0.75, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 1.5, LifeMin = 0.8, LifeMax = 1.4, Rate = 5 })
	elseif kind == "Fire" then
		emitter(att, { Texture = FIRE_TEX, Color = FIRE_ORANGE, SizeStart = 1.6, SizeEnd = 0.2, TransStart = 0.2, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 2, Light = 0.9, Accel = Vector3.new(0, 7, 0), LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 0.9, Rate = 14 })
		emitter(att, { Texture = SPARK, Color = FIRE_RED, SizeStart = 0.3, SizeEnd = 0.1, TransEnd = 0.7, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 3, Light = 0.9, Accel = Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), LifeMin = 0.6, LifeMax = 1.2, Rate = 10 })
	elseif kind == "FrostFire" then
		emitter(att, { Texture = FIRE_TEX, Color = FROST_BLUE, SizeStart = 1.6, SizeEnd = 0.2, TransStart = 0.2, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 2, Light = 1, Accel = Vector3.new(0, 7, 0), LifeMin = 0.5, LifeMax = 0.9, Rate = 14 })
		emitter(att, { Texture = SPARK, Color = ICE_LIGHT, SizeStart = 0.4, SizeEnd = 0.1, TransEnd = 0.7, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 3, Light = 0.8, Accel = Vector3.new(0, -2, 0), LifeMin = 0.8, LifeMax = 1.4, Rate = 14 })
	elseif kind == "Dizzy" then
		emitter(att, { Texture = SPARK, Color = DIZZY_YELLOW, SizeStart = 0.6, SizeEnd = 0.3, TransEnd = 0.6, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 2.5, Light = 1, LifeMin = 1, LifeMax = 1.6, Rate = 9, Spread = Vector2.new(180, 180) })
	elseif kind == "Crazy" then
		emitter(att, { Texture = SPARK, Color = CRAZY_RED, SizeStart = 0.4, SizeEnd = 0.1, TransEnd = 0.7, SpeedMin = 1, SpeedMax = 9, Light = 0.9, LifeMin = 0.4, LifeMax = 1, Rate = 22 })
		emitter(att, { Color = Color3.fromRGB(35, 32, 36), SizeStart = 1.4, SizeEnd = 3, TransStart = 0.6, SpeedMin = 0.5, SpeedMax = 2, LifeMin = 0.8, LifeMax = 1.4, Rate = 6 })
	end
	return att
end

local AURA_KICKS = { Ice = true, Fire = true, FrostFire = true, Dizzy = true, Crazy = true }

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(0.4)
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local char = plr.Character
			local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hrp and hum then
				local kind = plr:GetAttribute("EquippedKick")
				local want = AURA_KICKS[kind] and plr:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true and hum.Health > 0
				local st = auraState[char]
				if st and (not want or st.kind ~= kind or not st.att.Parent) then
					st.att:Destroy()
					auraState[char] = nil
					st = nil
				end
				if want and not st then
					auraState[char] = { kind = kind, att = buildAura(hrp, kind) }
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

-- KickArenaHud (LocalScript in StarterPlayerScripts): ability buttons, shop, VIP chat tag, fly and ball controls
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local THEME = {
	Panel = Color3.fromRGB(16, 18, 26),
	Text = Color3.fromRGB(245, 247, 252),
	Muted = Color3.fromRGB(150, 158, 178),
	Green = Color3.fromRGB(72, 222, 128),

local function new(className, props, children)
	local inst = Instance.new(className)
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
		inst[k] = v
	end
	for _, c in ipairs(children or {}) do
		c.Parent = inst
	end
	return inst
end

local function tween(inst, time, goal, style, dir)
	local t = TweenService:Create(inst, TweenInfo.new(time, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), goal)
	t:Play()
	return t
end

local function corner(r)
	return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = r })
end

local gui = new("ScreenGui", {
	Name = "KickArenaHud",
	ResetOnSpawn = false,
	DisplayOrder = 6,

local function click()

local buttons = {}

local function makeButton(key)
	local holder = new("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BTN + 40, BTN + 44),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Visible = false,
		Parent = gui,
	})
	local btn = new("TextButton", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BTN, BTN),
		BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Panel,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Text = "",
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		Parent = holder,
	}, { corner(UDim.new(1, 0)) })
	local ring = new("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.Green, Thickness = 3, Transparency = 0.1, Parent = btn })
	local fill = new("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Visible = false,
		Parent = btn,
	}, { corner(UDim.new(1, 0)), new("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90 }) })
	local keyLabel = new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
		TextSize = isTouch and 28 or 24,
		TextColor3 = THEME.Text,
		Text = key,
		ZIndex = 3,
		Parent = btn,
	})
	local title = new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, BTN + 6),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
		FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = THEME.Text,
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.6,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	local state = new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, BTN + 22),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14),
		FontFace = FONT_BOLD,
		TextSize = 11,
		TextColor3 = THEME.Muted,
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.7,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	btn.Activated:Connect(function()
		if ActionRemote then
			ActionRemote:FireServer(key)
		end
	end)
	return { key = key, holder = holder, ring = ring, fill = fill, keyLabel = keyLabel, title = title, state = state }
end

buttons.E = makeButton("E")
buttons.Q = makeButton("Q")

local function styleNow()
	if player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") ~= true then
		return nil
	end
	return STYLE[player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick") or ""]
end

local function paint(b, label, color, stateText, stateColor, fraction, enabled, big)
	b.title.Text = label
	b.title.TextColor3 = color
	b.state.Text = stateText
	b.state.TextColor3 = stateColor
	b.ring.Color = color
	b.ring.Transparency = enabled and 0.1 or 0.7
	local f = math.clamp(fraction, 0, 1)
	b.fill.Visible = f > 0.002
	if f > 0.002 then
		local cut = math.clamp(1 - f, 0.001, 0.998)
		b.fill.UIGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(cut, 1),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(cut + 0.001, 0.45),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.45),
		})
	end
	b.keyLabel.Text = big or b.key
	b.keyLabel.TextTransparency = enabled and 0 or 0.45
end

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	local style = styleNow()
	buttons.E.holder.Visible = style ~= nil
	buttons.Q.holder.Visible = style ~= nil and style.q ~= nil
	if not style then
		return
	end
	local kick = player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick")
	local active = player:GetAttribute("XActive") == true
	local remain = (player:GetAttribute("XCooldownEnd") or 0) - Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
	local total = math.max(player:GetAttribute("XCooldown") or 1, 0.1)
	local stopped = Workspace:GetAttribute("KickTimeStop") == true

	if style.q then
		buttons.E.holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, -58)
		buttons.Q.holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 64)
	else
		buttons.E.holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 0)
	end

	if kick == "Tycoon" then
		if active then
			paint(buttons.E, style.e, style.color, "BUILT", THEME.Muted, 0, false)
			paint(buttons.Q, style.q, Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 100), "TAP TO DESTROY", Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 100), 0, true)
		else
			local cool = remain > 0
			paint(buttons.E, style.e, style.color, cool and "WAIT" or "READY", cool and THEME.Muted or style.color, cool and math.clamp(remain / total, 0, 1) or 0, not cool)
			paint(buttons.Q, style.q, THEME.Muted, "NO TYCOON", THEME.Muted, 0, false)
		end
		return
	end

	if style.toggle then
		if active then
			paint(buttons.E, style.e, style.color, "ON  -  TAP TO STOP", THEME.Green, 0, true, "ON")
		else
			local cool = remain > 0
			paint(buttons.E, style.e, style.color, cool and "WAIT" or "READY", cool and THEME.Muted or style.color, cool and math.clamp(remain / total, 0, 1) or 0, not cool)
		end
		return
	end

	if stopped and kick == "God" then
		paint(buttons.E, style.e, style.color, "TIME STOPPED", style.color, 0, true, "!")
	elseif remain > 0 then
		paint(buttons.E, style.e, style.color, "RECHARGING", THEME.Muted, math.clamp(remain / total, 0, 1), false, remain < 10 and string.format("%.1f", remain) or tostring(math.ceil(remain)))
	else
		paint(buttons.E, style.e, style.color, "READY", style.color, 0, true)
	end
end)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
	if processed or not ActionRemote or not styleNow() then
		return
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
		ActionRemote:FireServer("E")
	elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q and styleNow().q then
		ActionRemote:FireServer("Q")
	end
end)

local shopOpen = false
local function setShop(state)
	shopOpen = state
	if state then
		refreshShop()
		panel.Visible = true
		panel.GroupTransparency = 1
		shopScale.Scale = 0.92
		tween(panel, 0.2, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
		tween(shopScale, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	else
		tween(shopScale, 0.15, { Scale = 0.92 })
		tween(panel, 0.15, { GroupTransparency = 1 }).Completed:Once(function()
			if not shopOpen then
				panel.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end
end
local function shopVisibility()
	local lobby = player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") ~= true and player:GetAttribute("AdminOpen") ~= true
	shopButton.Visible = lobby
	if not lobby and shopOpen then
		setShop(false)
	end
end
player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("ArenaActive"):Connect(shopVisibility)
player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("AdminOpen"):Connect(shopVisibility)
shopVisibility()
shopButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	click()
	tween(shopScaleBtn, 0.08, { Scale = 0.92 }).Completed:Once(function()
		tween(shopScaleBtn, 0.15, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end)
	setShop(not shopOpen)
end)
closeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	setShop(false)
end)

end

-- VIP CHAT TAG
if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
	local SHINE = { "#FFFBD0", "#FFE680", "#FFD21F", "#FFB300", "#FFD21F", "#FFE680", "#FFFBD0" }
	local LETTERS = { "[", "V", "I", "P", "]" }
	local counter = 0
	local function vipTag(shift)
		local out = {}
		for i, ch in ipairs(LETTERS) do
			out[i] = string.format('<font color="%s">%s</font>', SHINE[((i + shift) % #SHINE) + 1], ch)
		end
		return "<b>" .. table.concat(out) .. "</b> "
	end
	TextChatService.OnIncomingMessage = function(message)
		local props = Instance.new("TextChatMessageProperties")
		local src = message.TextSource
		if src then
			local plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(src.UserId)
			if plr and plr:GetAttribute("IsVIP") == true then
				counter += 1
				props.PrefixText = vipTag(counter) .. message.PrefixText
			end
		end
		return props
	end
end

-- CONTROLS
local controls = nil
task.spawn(function()
	local ok, module = pcall(function()
		return require(player:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("PlayerModule"))
	end)
	if ok then
		local ok2, c = pcall(function()
			return module:GetControls()
		end)
		if ok2 then
			controls = c
		end
	end
end)

local function moveVector()
	if controls then
		local ok, v = pcall(function()
			return controls:GetMoveVector()
		end)
		if ok and v then
			return v
		end
	end
	return Vector3.zero
end

-- FLY (Spectator)
local flyParts = nil

local function stopFly()
	if flyParts then
		for _, inst in ipairs(flyParts.items) do
			inst:Destroy()
		end
		local hum = flyParts.hum
		if hum and hum.Parent then
			hum.PlatformStand = false
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
		end
		flyParts = nil
	end
end

local function startFly(char)
	local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not hrp or not hum then
		return
	end
	local att = new("Attachment", { Parent = hrp })
	local lv = new("LinearVelocity", {
		Attachment0 = att,
		MaxForce = math.huge,
		VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero,
		RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World,
		Parent = hrp,
	})
	local ao = new("AlignOrientation", {
		Attachment0 = att,
		Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment,
		MaxTorque = math.huge,
		Responsiveness = 25,
		Parent = hrp,
	})
	hum.PlatformStand = true
	flyParts = { items = { att, lv, ao }, hum = hum, lv = lv, ao = ao, hrp = hrp }
end

-- noclip while flying
RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
	if flyParts and flyParts.hrp.Parent then
		for _, d in ipairs(flyParts.hrp.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide then
				d.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end
end)

-- BALL (Smile)
local slipUntil, nextSlip = 0, os.clock() + 2
UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
	local char = player.Character
	local ball = char and char:FindFirstChild("SmileBall")
	if ball and player:GetAttribute("XActive") == true then
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
		if Workspace:Raycast(ball.Position, Vector3.new(0, -4.4, 0), params) then
			ball:ApplyImpulse(Vector3.new(0, ball.AssemblyMass * 36, 0))
		end
	end
end)

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
	local char = player.Character
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	local kick = player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick")
	local active = player:GetAttribute("XActive") == true and player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true

	if (kick == "Spectator" or kick == "Joris") and active and char then
		if not flyParts or not flyParts.hrp.Parent then
			stopFly()
			startFly(char)
		end
		if flyParts and cam then
			local mv = moveVector()
			local dir = cam.CFrame.RightVector * mv.X + cam.CFrame.LookVector * -mv.Z
			local up = flyParts.hum.Jump and 32 or 0
			local vel = dir * 60 + Vector3.new(0, up + math.sin(os.clock() * 2) * 0.6, 0)
			flyParts.lv.VectorVelocity = vel
			local flat = Vector3.new(cam.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, cam.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
			if flat.Magnitude > 0.05 then
				flyParts.ao.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, flat.Unit) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(math.clamp(vel.Magnitude, 0, 60) * 0.25), 0, 0)
			end
		end
	elseif flyParts then
		stopFly()
	end

	local ball = char and char:FindFirstChild("SmileBall")
	if ball and (kick == "Smile" or player:GetAttribute("ForceSmile") == true) and active and cam then
		local mv = moveVector()
		local dir = cam.CFrame.RightVector * mv.X + cam.CFrame.LookVector * -mv.Z
		dir = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
		local t = os.clock()
		if t > nextSlip then
			slipUntil = t + 0.8
			nextSlip = t + 2 + math.random() * 2.5
			ball:ApplyImpulse(Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 2, 0, (math.random() - 0.5) * 2) * ball.AssemblyMass * 14)
		end
		local acc = t < slipUntil and 14 or 62
		if dir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
			ball:ApplyImpulse(dir.Unit * math.min(dir.Magnitude, 1) * acc * ball.AssemblyMass * dt)
		end
		local v = ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		local flatV = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
		if flatV.Magnitude > 52 then
			ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity = flatV.Unit * 52 + Vector3.new(0, v.Y, 0)
		end
	end
end)

-- KickArenaFX (LocalScript in StarterPlayerScripts): effects and sounds for the new kicks, weather and events
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("KickArenaRemotes")
local FXRemote = remotes:WaitForChild("ExtraFX", 30)
local KickVisualRemote = remotes:WaitForChild("KickVisual")
local HitFXRemote = remotes:WaitForChild("HitFX")

local SPARK = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
local SMOKE = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
local FIRE = "rbxasset://textures/particles/fire_main.dds"

local C = {
