local fxFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
fxFolder.Name = "KickArenaFX"
fxFolder.Parent = Workspace

local function dummyPart(pos, life)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	p.Anchored = true
	p.CanCollide = false
	p.CanQuery = false
	p.CanTouch = false
	p.Transparency = 1
	p.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
	p.Position = pos
	p.Parent = fxFolder
	Debris:AddItem(p, life or 3)
	return p
end

local SFX_ALT = {
	["button.wav"] = { "button.mp3", "switch.mp3", "clickfast.mp3" },
	["collide.wav"] = { "collide.mp3", "hit.mp3", "snap.mp3" },
	["Rocket whoosh 01.wav"] = { "swordlunge.wav", "swordlunge.mp3", "swordslash.mp3" },
	["glassbreak.wav"] = { "glassbreak.mp3", "collide.mp3", "hit.mp3" },
	["swordslash.wav"] = { "swordslash.mp3", "swordlunge.mp3" },
	["clickfast.wav"] = { "clickfast.mp3", "button.mp3" },
	["bass.mp3"] = { "bass.wav", "collide.mp3" },
	["snap.mp3"] = { "snap.wav", "hit.mp3" },
	["electronicpingshort.wav"] = { "electronicpingshort.mp3", "button.mp3" },
}
local sfxGood = {}
local function armSound(s, file)
	local alts = SFX_ALT[file]
	if not alts then
		return
	end
	if sfxGood[file] then
		s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/" .. sfxGood[file]
		return
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(0.35)
		if not s.Parent then
			return
		end
		if s.IsLoaded and s.TimeLength > 0 then
			sfxGood[file] = file
			return
		end
		for _, n in ipairs(alts) do
			s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/" .. n
			s:Play()
			task.wait(0.35)
			if not s.Parent then
				return
			end
			if s.IsLoaded and s.TimeLength > 0 then
				sfxGood[file] = n
				return
			end
		end
	end)
end

local function sfx(file, volume, pitch, at, delay)
	local function play()
		local s = Instance.new("Sound")
		s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/" .. file
		s.Volume = volume or 0.6
		s.PlaybackSpeed = pitch or 1
		s.RollOffMaxDistance = 140
		s.RollOffMinDistance = 12
		if typeof(at) == "Vector3" then
			s.Parent = dummyPart(at, 4)
		elseif typeof(at) == "Instance" then
			s.Parent = at
		else
			s.Parent = fxFolder
		end
		s:Play()
		armSound(s, file)
		Debris:AddItem(s, 4)
	end
	if delay and delay > 0 then
		task.delay(delay, play)
	else
		play()
	end
end

local function burst(pos, colors, count, speed, size, tex, light)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Vfx == false then
		return
	end
	local p = dummyPart(pos, 3)
	local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
	att.Parent = p
	local e = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	e.Texture = tex or SPARK
	local seq = {}
	for i, c in ipairs(colors) do
		table.insert(seq, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((i - 1) / math.max(#colors - 1, 1), c))
	end
	if #seq == 1 then
		table.insert(seq, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, colors[1]))
	end
	e.Color = ColorSequence.new(seq)
	e.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, size or 0.6), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0) })
	e.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
	e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 1.1)
	e.Speed = NumberRange.new(speed * 0.4, speed)
	e.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
	e.Drag = 3
	e.LightEmission = light == nil and 0.8 or light
	e.Rate = 0
	e.Parent = att
	e:Emit(count)
end

local function ring(pos, color, size, time, thick)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Vfx == false then
		return
	end
	local r = Instance.new("Part")
	r.Anchored = true
	r.CanCollide = false
	r.CanQuery = false
	r.CanTouch = false
	r.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
	r.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
	r.Color = color
	r.Transparency = 0.2
	r.Size = Vector3.new(thick or 0.4, 1, 1)
	r.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
	r.Parent = fxFolder
	TweenService:Create(r, TweenInfo.new(time or 0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Size = Vector3.new(thick or 0.4, size, size),
		Transparency = 1,
	}):Play()
	Debris:AddItem(r, (time or 0.5) + 0.1)
end

local function arc(a, b, color, thick, segs, jitter, life)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Vfx == false then
		return
	end
	local pts = { a }
	for i = 1, segs - 1 do
		local t = i / segs
		local p = a:Lerp(b, t) + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * jitter, (math.random() - 0.5) * jitter, (math.random() - 0.5) * jitter)
		table.insert(pts, p)
	end
	table.insert(pts, b)
	for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
		local p0, p1 = pts[i], pts[i + 1]
		local len = (p1 - p0).Magnitude
		if len > 0.05 then
			local seg = Instance.new("Part")
			seg.Anchored = true
			seg.CanCollide = false
			seg.CanQuery = false
			seg.CanTouch = false
			seg.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
			seg.Color = color
			seg.Size = Vector3.new(thick, thick, len)
			seg.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((p0 + p1) / 2, p1)
			seg.Parent = fxFolder
			TweenService:Create(seg, TweenInfo.new(life or 0.2), { Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(thick * 0.3, thick * 0.3, len) }):Play()
			Debris:AddItem(seg, (life or 0.2) + 0.05)
		end
	end
end

local function floatText(pos, text, color, size)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Numbers == false then
		return
	end
	local p = dummyPart(pos, 1.4)
	local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(80, 30)
	gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
	gui.Adornee = p
	gui.Parent = p
	local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	l.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	l.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
	l.TextSize = size or 20
	l.TextColor3 = color
	l.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
	l.Text = text
	l.Parent = gui
	TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = pos + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 3, 5, (math.random() - 0.5) * 3) }):Play()
	TweenService:Create(l, TweenInfo.new(1.1), { TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1 }):Play()
end

local function shake(pos, mag, dur)
	local st = _G.KickArenaSettings
	if st and st.Shake == false then
		return
	end
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	local char = player.Character
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not cam or not hum then
		return
	end
	local d = (cam.CFrame.Position - pos).Magnitude
	if d > 120 then
		return
	end
	local m = mag * (1 - d / 120)
	task.spawn(function()
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while os.clock() - t0 < dur and hum.Parent do
			hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * m, (math.random() - 0.5) * m, 0)
			task.wait()
		end
		if hum.Parent then
			hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero
		end
	end)
end

local flashGrade = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
flashGrade.Name = "KickArenaFlash"
flashGrade.Parent = Lighting
local function flash(amount, time)
	flashGrade.Brightness = amount
	TweenService:Create(flashGrade, TweenInfo.new(time or 0.3), { Brightness = 0 }):Play()
end

local function bodyParts(model)
	local list = {}
	for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and d.Transparency < 0.95 then
			table.insert(list, d)
		end
	end
	return list
end

local function rootOf(model)
	return model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model:FindFirstChild("Torso") or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
end

local function highlight(model, fill, outline, fillT)
	local h = Instance.new("Highlight")
	h.FillColor = fill
	h.OutlineColor = outline
	h.FillTransparency = fillT
	h.OutlineTransparency = 0.1
	h.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
	h.Parent = model
	return h
end

local function sparkleOn(root, color, rate)
	local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
	att.Parent = root
	local e = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	e.Texture = SPARK
	e.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
	e.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.6), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0) })
	e.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
	e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.6, 1.1)
	e.Speed = NumberRange.new(2, 6)
	e.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
	e.LightEmission = 0.9
	e.Rate = rate
	e.Parent = att
	return att
end

	Devil = { c = { C.Red, C.Dark }, n = 30, sp = 26, sz = 0.9, ring = C.Red, snd = { { "bass.mp3", 0.8, 0.5 }, { "snap.mp3", 0.6, 0.5 } } },
	Wind = { c = { C.White, C.Mint }, n = 26, sp = 40, sz = 1, snd = { { "Rocket whoosh 01.wav", 0.6, 1.1 } }, tex = SMOKE },

	Joris = { c = { C.PaleGold, C.White }, n = 34, sp = 36, sz = 0.8, ring = C.PaleGold, shake = 0.5, snd = { { "button.wav", 0.7, 0.8 }, { "collide.wav", 0.5, 1.1 } } },
	PayKick = { c = { C.Red, Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120) }, n = 44, sp = 46, sz = 0.8, ring = C.Red, ring2 = true, shake = 0.9, text = "X" },
}

local SWISH = {
	Lightning = { "clickfast.wav", 0.6, 2.2 },
	Gold = { "swordslash.wav", 0.5, 0.9 },
	Tycoon = { "button.wav", 0.5, 0.9 },
	God = { "Rocket whoosh 01.wav", 0.7, 0.5 },

	Joris = { "swordslash.wav", 0.6, 1.1 },

local TRAIL = {
	Lightning = { C.Blue, C.White },
	Gold = { C.Gold, C.PaleGold },
	Tycoon = { C.Green, C.Gold },
	God = { C.PaleGold, C.White },
	SixPack = { C.Orange, C.Red },
	Wood = { C.Brown, C.Tan },
	Mirror = { C.White, C.Gray },
	Painting = { Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 200), Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 255) },
	Smile = { C.Yellow, C.White },
	Devil = { C.Red, C.Dark },
	Wind = { C.White, C.Mint },
	PayKick = { C.Red, C.Dark },
	Joris = { C.PaleGold, C.White },
	Error = { C.Green, C.Dark },
}

local function hitFx(pos, kind)
	local s = SPEC[kind]
	if not s then
		return
	end
	local colors = s.c
	if s.rainbow then
		colors = {}
		for i = 0, 5 do
			table.insert(colors, Color3.fromHSV(i / 6, 0.85, 1))
		end
	end
	burst(pos, colors, s.n, s.sp, s.sz, s.tex)
	if s.ring then
		ring(pos, s.ring, 14, 0.45)
		if s.ring2 then
			task.delay(0.12, ring, pos, C.White, 26, 0.6)
		end
	end
	for _ = 1, s.arcs or 0 do
		local d = Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.3, math.random() - 0.5)
		d = d.Magnitude > 0.1 and d.Unit or Vector3.yAxis
		arc(pos, pos + d * (3 + math.random() * 3), C.Blue, 0.16, 4, 1.2, 0.18)
	end
	if s.text then
		for _ = 1, 3 do
			floatText(pos + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), s.text, colors[1], 22)
		end
	end
	if s.shake then
		shake(pos, s.shake, 0.25)
	end
end

HitFXRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(targetPart, kickName)
	if typeof(targetPart) == "Instance" and targetPart.Parent and SPEC[kickName] then
		hitFx(targetPart.Position, kickName)
	end
end)

local function footOf(char)
	return char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
end

KickVisualRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kicker, kickName)
	local style = TRAIL[kickName]
	if not style then
		return
	end
	local char = typeof(kicker) == "Instance" and (kicker:IsA("Player") and kicker.Character or kicker) or nil
	if not char then
		return
	end
	local foot = footOf(char)
	local root = rootOf(char)
	if not foot or not root then
		return
	end
	local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
	a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0.7, 0)
	a0.Parent = foot
	local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
	a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -0.7, 0)
	a1.Parent = foot
	local tr = Instance.new("Trail")
	tr.Attachment0 = a0
	tr.Attachment1 = a1
	tr.Lifetime = 0.35
	tr.LightEmission = 0.9
	tr.Color = ColorSequence.new(style[1], style[2])
	tr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
	tr.WidthScale = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1.4), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.2) })
	tr.Parent = foot
	Debris:AddItem(tr, 0.8)
	Debris:AddItem(a0, 0.8)
	Debris:AddItem(a1, 0.8)
	local sw = SWISH[kickName]
	if sw then
		sfx(sw[1], sw[2], sw[3], root, 0.05)
	end
	if kickName == "Lightning" then
		task.delay(0.12, function()
			if foot.Parent then
				arc(foot.Position, foot.Position + root.CFrame.LookVector * 5 + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), C.Blue, 0.2, 5, 1.4, 0.2)
			end
		end)
	end
end)

-- AURAS
local AURA = {
	Lightning = { color = C.Blue, rate = 10 },
	Gold = { color = C.Gold, rate = 14 },
	Tycoon = { color = C.Green, rate = 8 },
	God = { color = C.PaleGold, rate = 22 },
	SixPack = { color = C.Orange, rate = 8 },
	Mirror = { color = C.White, rate = 10 },
	Painting = { color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 220), rate = 16, rainbow = true },
	Devil = { color = C.Red, rate = 12 },
	Wind = { color = C.Mint, rate = 9 },
	Smile = { color = C.Yellow, rate = 6 },
}

local function syncAura(plr)
	local char = plr.Character
	local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then
		return
	end
	local kick = plr:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true and plr:GetAttribute("EquippedKick") or nil
	if kick == "Devil" and plr:GetAttribute("XActive") == true then
		kick = nil
	end
	local want = kick and AURA[kick] and kick or nil
	local att = root:FindFirstChild("KickAuraAtt")
	if att and att:GetAttribute("Kick") ~= want then
		att:Destroy()
		att = nil
	end
	if want and not att then
		local a = AURA[want]
		att = Instance.new("Attachment")
		att.Name = "KickAuraAtt"
		att:SetAttribute("Kick", want)
		att.Parent = root
		local e = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
		e.Texture = want == "Devil" and SMOKE or SPARK
		if a.rainbow then
			local keys = {}
			for i = 0, 6 do
				table.insert(keys, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / 6, Color3.fromHSV(i / 7, 0.8, 1)))
			end
			e.Color = ColorSequence.new(keys)
		else
			e.Color = ColorSequence.new(a.color)
		end
		e.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, want == "Devil" and 1.6 or 0.55), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0) })
		e.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
		e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.4)
		e.Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 3)
		e.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
		e.LightEmission = want == "Devil" and 0.2 or 0.85
		e.Rate = a.rate
		e.Parent = att
	end
end

local arcClock = 0
task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(0.45)
		arcClock += 1
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			syncAura(plr)
			if plr:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true and plr:GetAttribute("EquippedKick") == "Lightning" and plr.Character then
				local parts = bodyParts(plr.Character)
				if #parts >= 2 then
					arc(parts[math.random(#parts)].Position, parts[math.random(#parts)].Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0), C.Blue, 0.1, 3, 1.2, 0.16)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

-- RAIN
local rain = nil
local grade = nil

local function setRain(on)
	if on and not rain then
		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Anchored = true
		part.CanCollide = false
		part.CanQuery = false
		part.CanTouch = false
		part.Transparency = 1
		part.Size = Vector3.new(80, 1, 80)
		part.Parent = fxFolder
		local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
		att.Parent = part
		local e = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
		e.Texture = SMOKE
		e.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(205, 220, 240))
		e.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.35)
		e.Squash = NumberSequence.new(-3)
		e.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.55)
		e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.55, 0.7)
		e.Speed = NumberRange.new(85, 100)
		e.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel
		e.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
		e.Rate = 650
		e.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
		e.ShapeStyle = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume
		e.Parent = part
		local s = Instance.new("Sound")
		s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/Rocket whoosh 01.wav"
		s.Looped = true
		s.Volume = 0.06
		s.PlaybackSpeed = 2.6
		s.Parent = part
		s:Play()
		rain = { part = part, emitter = e, sound = s, t = 0, covered = false }
		grade = grade or Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
		grade.Name = "KickArenaRain"
		grade.Parent = Lighting
		TweenService:Create(grade, TweenInfo.new(2), { Brightness = -0.05, Contrast = 0.08, Saturation = -0.3, TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 198, 228) }):Play()
	elseif not on and rain then
		local r = rain
		rain = nil
		r.emitter.Enabled = false
		r.sound:Stop()
		Debris:AddItem(r.part, 2)
		if grade then
			TweenService:Create(grade, TweenInfo.new(3), { Brightness = 0, Contrast = 0, Saturation = 0, TintColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }):Play()
		end
	end
end

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if rain and cam then
		local focus = cam.CFrame.Position
		rain.part.Position = focus + Vector3.new(0, 42, 0)
		rain.t += dt
		if rain.t > 0.4 then
			rain.t = 0
			local params = RaycastParams.new()
			params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { player.Character, fxFolder }
			local hit = Workspace:Raycast(focus, Vector3.new(0, 70, 0), params)
			rain.covered = hit ~= nil and hit.Instance.CanCollide
			rain.emitter.Enabled = not rain.covered
			rain.sound.Volume = rain.covered and 0.02 or 0.06
		end
	end
end)

Workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal("KickRain"):Connect(function()
	setRain(Workspace:GetAttribute("KickRain") == true)
end)
setRain(Workspace:GetAttribute("KickRain") == true)

-- TIME STOP
local stopGrade = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
stopGrade.Name = "KickArenaTimeStop"
stopGrade.Parent = Lighting
local function refreshStop()
	local on = Workspace:GetAttribute("KickTimeStop") == true and player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true
	TweenService:Create(stopGrade, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {
		Saturation = on and -1 or 0,
		Contrast = on and 0.25 or 0,
		Brightness = on and -0.03 or 0,
	}):Play()
	if on then
		task.spawn(function()
			while Workspace:GetAttribute("KickTimeStop") == true and player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true do
				sfx("clickfast.wav", 0.25, 1.1)
				task.wait(0.5)
			end
		end)
	end
end
Workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal("KickTimeStop"):Connect(refreshStop)
player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("ArenaActive"):Connect(refreshStop)

-- PAINT SCREEN
local screen = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
screen.Name = "KickArenaPaint"
screen.ResetOnSpawn = false
screen.IgnoreGuiInset = true
screen.DisplayOrder = 30
screen.Parent = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local function paintScreen(dur)
	local blobs = {}
	for i = 1, 16 do
		local size = math.random(120, 340)
		local b = Instance.new("Frame")
		b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		b.Position = UDim2.fromScale(math.random(), math.random() * 0.9)
		b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
		b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(math.random(), 0.85, 1)
		b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
		b.BorderSizePixel = 0
		b.Parent = screen
		local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
		c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		c.Parent = b
		table.insert(blobs, b)
		task.delay(i * 0.03, function()
			TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Back), { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size) }):Play()
		end)
	end
	sfx("snap.mp3", 0.8, 0.6)
	sfx("Rocket whoosh 01.wav", 0.4, 2.2)
	for _, b in ipairs(blobs) do
		TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), { Position = b.Position + UDim2.fromOffset(0, 90) }):Play()
		task.delay(math.max(dur - 1.4, 0.5), function()
			TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(1.3), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
		end)
		Debris:AddItem(b, dur + 0.5)
	end
end

-- EVENT HANDLERS
local H = {}

function H.Bolt(pos)
	local top = pos + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 30, 170, (math.random() - 0.5) * 30)
	arc(top, pos, C.Blue, 1.4, 16, 14, 0.4)
	arc(top, pos, C.White, 0.5, 16, 14, 0.4)
	for _ = 1, 3 do
		local mid = top:Lerp(pos, 0.3 + math.random() * 0.5)
		arc(mid, mid + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 50, -25 - math.random() * 25, (math.random() - 0.5) * 50), C.Blue, 0.6, 6, 6, 0.3)
	end
	burst(pos, { C.White, C.Blue }, 50, 45, 0.9)
	ring(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0), C.Blue, 24, 0.5)
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	local d = cam and (cam.CFrame.Position - pos).Magnitude or 0
	if d < 220 then
		flash(0.45 * (1 - d / 220), 0.35)
	end
	shake(pos, 1.5, 0.35)
	sfx("clickfast.wav", 1, 0.55, pos)
	sfx("snap.mp3", 0.9, 0.5, pos)
	sfx("bass.mp3", 1, 0.4, pos, math.min(d / 340, 1.2))
	sfx("collide.wav", 0.8, 0.35, pos, math.min(d / 340, 1.2))
end

function H.Shock(model, dur)
	if typeof(model) ~= "Instance" or not model.Parent then
		return
	end
	local h = highlight(model, Color3.fromRGB(255, 244, 150), C.White, 0.6)
	local parts = bodyParts(model)
	local t0 = os.clock()
	local root = rootOf(model)
	if root then
		sfx("clickfast.wav", 0.8, 1.5, root)
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while os.clock() - t0 < dur and model.Parent do
			h.FillTransparency = math.random() < 0.5 and 0.35 or 0.85
			if #parts >= 2 then
				for _ = 1, 2 do
					arc(parts[math.random(#parts)].Position, parts[math.random(#parts)].Position, C.Blue, 0.12, 3, 3, 0.1)
				end
			end
			if root and math.random() < 0.3 then
				sfx("button.wav", 0.25, 2.2, root)
			end
			task.wait(0.07)
		end
		h:Destroy()
	end)
end

function H.Gold(model, dur)
	if typeof(model) ~= "Instance" or not model.Parent then
		return
	end
	local root = rootOf(model)
	if not root then
		return
	end
	local h = highlight(model, C.Gold, C.PaleGold, 0.75)
	local att = sparkleOn(root, C.PaleGold, 25)
	burst(root.Position, { C.Gold, C.PaleGold }, 40, 26, 0.7)
	ring(root.Position, C.Gold, 16, 0.5)
	sfx("button.wav", 0.8, 1.9, root)
	sfx("glassbreak.wav", 0.3, 2.2, root)
	task.delay(dur, function()
		h:Destroy()
		att:Destroy()
		if root.Parent then
			burst(root.Position, { C.Gold }, 20, 18, 0.5)
			sfx("button.wav", 0.5, 1.4, root)
		end
	end)
end

function H.GodLight(pos)
	local function glow(size, cf, time, delay)
		local p = Instance.new("Part")
		p.Anchored = true
		p.CanCollide = false
		p.CanQuery = false
		p.CanTouch = false
		p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
		p.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		p.Transparency = 0.1
		p.Size = size
		p.CFrame = cf
		p.Parent = fxFolder
		task.delay(delay or 0, function()
			TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(time, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
				Transparency = 1,
				Size = Vector3.new(size.X * 0.15, size.Y * 1.25, size.Z * 0.15),
			}):Play()
		end)
		Debris:AddItem(p, time + (delay or 0) + 0.2)
	end
	glow(Vector3.new(6, 60, 6), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 26, 0)), 0.9)
	for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		for i = 1, 3 do
			local ang = math.rad(side * (20 + i * 16))
			glow(Vector3.new(0.5, 12 - i * 2, 3), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(side * (2 + i * 1.6), 6 + i * 1.5, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, ang), 0.8, 0.05 * i)
		end
	end
	ring(pos, C.White, 26, 0.8, 0.5)
	burst(pos + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), { C.White, C.PaleGold }, 40, 40, 0.9)
	sfx("Rocket whoosh 01.wav", 0.9, 1.4, pos)
	shake(pos, 0.7, 0.4)
end

local PAINT_SONGS = { "rbxassetid://1847853099", "rbxassetid://1846368080" } -- party song, replace the ids if you want another track
function H.PaintDisco(dur)
	local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
	cc.Name = "PaintDisco"
	cc.Saturation = 0.6
	cc.Parent = Lighting
	local bloom = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
	bloom.Intensity = 0.7
	bloom.Size = 30
	bloom.Threshold = 0.8
	bloom.Parent = Lighting
	local t0 = os.clock()
	local song = Instance.new("Sound")
	song.Name = "PaintPartySong"
	song.Looped = true
	song.Volume = 0.7
	song.SoundId = PAINT_SONGS[1]
	song.Parent = SoundService
	song:Play()
	task.delay(4, function()
		if song.Parent and not song.IsLoaded and PAINT_SONGS[2] then
			song.SoundId = PAINT_SONGS[2]
			song:Play()
		end
	end)
	local conn
	conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local t = os.clock() - t0
		if t >= dur or not cc.Parent then
			conn:Disconnect()
			cc:Destroy()
			bloom:Destroy()
			TweenService:Create(song, TweenInfo.new(1), { Volume = 0 }):Play()
			Debris:AddItem(song, 1.2)
			return
		end
		local pulse = (math.sin(t * 9) + 1) / 2
		cc.TintColor = Color3.fromHSV((t * 0.9) % 1, 0.35 + 0.25 * pulse, 1)
		cc.Brightness = 0.04 + 0.08 * pulse
		cc.Contrast = 0.12
		bloom.Intensity = 0.5 + 0.8 * pulse
	end)
	sfx("bass.mp3", 0.7, 1.2, nil)
end

function H.Wind(origin, dir)
	for i = 1, 16 do
		local side = Vector3.new(-dir.Z, 0, dir.X) * ((math.random() - 0.5) * 16)
		local start = origin + Vector3.new(0, 0.5 + math.random() * 5, 0) + side + dir * 2
		local streak = Instance.new("Part")
		streak.Anchored = true
		streak.CanCollide = false
		streak.CanQuery = false
		streak.CanTouch = false
		streak.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
		streak.Color = i % 2 == 0 and C.White or C.Mint
		streak.Transparency = 0.45
		streak.Size = Vector3.new(0.12, 0.12, 6 + math.random() * 5)
		streak.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(start, start + dir)
		streak.Parent = fxFolder
		TweenService:Create(streak, TweenInfo.new(0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
			CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(start + dir * 55, start + dir * 56),
			Transparency = 1,
		}):Play()
		Debris:AddItem(streak, 0.8)
	end
	burst(origin + dir * 3 + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), { C.White, C.Mint }, 60, 60, 2.2, SMOKE, 0.3)
	ring(origin + Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0), C.Mint, 18, 0.4)
	sfx("Rocket whoosh 01.wav", 0.9, 0.8, origin)
	sfx("bass.mp3", 0.5, 1.7, origin)
	shake(origin, 0.5, 0.3)
end

		return Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(Instance.new("HumanoidDescription"), Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
	end)

	name.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
	name.TextSize = 13
	name.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	name.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
	name.Text = "Mirror Guard " .. index
	name.Parent = gui
	local back = Instance.new("Frame")
	back.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22)
	back.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 7)
	back.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 12)
	back.BorderSizePixel = 0
	back.Parent = gui
	local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
	bar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
	bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	bar.Parent = back
	hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(hp)
		bar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(hp / hum.MaxHealth, 0, 1), 1)
	end)
	return model, hum
end

local function award(player, t)
	if t.awarded then
		return
	end
	t.awarded = true
	local saved = false
	for _ = 1, 5 do
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			questStore:UpdateAsync("u_" .. player.UserId, function(old)
				old = type(old) == "table" and old or { Unlocked = {}, HitBy = {}, Hits = 0, Kills = 0 }
				old.Unlocked = old.Unlocked or {}
				old.Unlocked.Mirror = true
				return old
			end)
		end)
		if ok then
			saved = true
			break
		end
		warn("[KickArenaTrial] Could not save the quest: " .. tostring(err))
		task.wait(2)
	end
	for _ = 1, 5 do
		local ok, res = pcall(function()
			return BadgeService:AwardBadge(player.UserId, BADGE_ID)
		end)
		if ok then
			break
		end
		warn("[KickArenaTrial] Badge award failed: " .. tostring(res))
		task.wait(2)
	end
	return saved
end

local function finish(player, t)
	t.done = true
	award(player, t)
	send(player, "Complete", t.back and t.back ~= 0)
	task.wait(6)
	if player.Parent and t.back and t.back ~= 0 then
		pcall(function()
			TeleportService:TeleportAsync(t.back, { player })
		end)
	end
end

local function countKill(player, t, model)
	if model:GetAttribute("Counted") or model:GetAttribute("LastHitBy") ~= player.UserId then
		return
	end
	model:SetAttribute("Counted", true)
	t.kills += 1
	send(player, "Progress", t.kills, NEEDED)
	if t.kills >= NEEDED and not t.done then
		t.done = true
		task.spawn(finish, player, t)
	end
end

local function runNpc(player, t, index)
	local model, hum = makeNpc(index)
	local char = player.Character
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local angle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
	local base = hrp and hrp.Position or Vector3.new(0, 3, 40)
	local pos = Vector3.new(math.clamp(base.X + math.cos(angle) * 45, -70, 70), 4, math.clamp(base.Z + math.sin(angle) * 45, -70, 70))
	model:PivotTo(CFrame.new(pos))
	model.Parent = npcs
	t.npc = model
	local dead = false
	hum.Died:Connect(function()
		dead = true
		countKill(player, t, model)
		task.delay(2, function()
			model:Destroy()
		end)
	end)
	local lastAttack = 0
	while not dead and model.Parent and player.Parent do
		task.wait(0.15)
		local c = player.Character
		local ph = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local pr = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local root = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if ph and pr and root and ph.Health > 0 then
			local off = pr.Position - root.Position
			local flat = Vector3.new(off.X, 0, off.Z)
			if flat.Magnitude > 5.5 then
				hum:MoveTo(pr.Position)
			else
				hum:MoveTo(root.Position)
				if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
					root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, root.Position + flat.Unit)
				end
				if os.clock() - lastAttack > 1.3 then
					lastAttack = os.clock()
					t.dodgeId = (t.dodgeId or 0) + 1
					local id = t.dodgeId
					t.dodged = false
					t.dodgeOpen = os.clock() + 1.25
					send(player, "Dodge", id)
					task.wait(1)
					t.dodgeOpen = nil
					local c2 = player.Character
					local ph2 = c2 and c2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					local pr2 = c2 and c2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if t.dodged then
						send(player, "Dodged")
					elseif ph2 and pr2 and ph2.Health > 0 and model.Parent and (Vector3.new(pr2.Position.X - root.Position.X, 0, pr2.Position.Z - root.Position.Z)).Magnitude < 9 then
						ph2:TakeDamage(10 + index * 3)
						local dir = flat.Magnitude > 0.1 and flat.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
						pr2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dir * 55 + Vector3.new(0, 24, 0)
						send(player, "Hurt", root.Position)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if not dead and not model.Parent then
		countKill(player, t, model)
	end
	if model.Parent and not dead then
		model:Destroy()
	end
	return dead
end

local function startTrial(player)
	local t = trials[player]
	if not t or t.started then
		return
	end
	t.started = true
	send(player, "Progress", 0, NEEDED)
	task.wait(2)
	while player.Parent and t.kills < NEEDED do
		runNpc(player, t, math.min(t.kills + 1, NEEDED))
		task.wait(1)
	end
	if player.Parent and t.kills >= NEEDED and not t.done then
		t.done = true
		finish(player, t)
	end
end

DodgeRemote.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, id)
	local t = trials[player]
	local hrp = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if t and hrp and t.dodgeOpen and id == t.dodgeId and not t.dodged then
		t.dodged = true
		local side = hrp.CFrame.RightVector * (math.random() < 0.5 and 1 or -1)
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = side * 48 + Vector3.new(0, 14, 0)
	end
end)

KickRemote.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player)
	local t = trials[player]
	local char = player.Character
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not t or not t.valid or not hum or not hrp or hum.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end
	if os.clock() - t.lastKick < KICK_COOLDOWN then
		return
	end
	t.lastKick = os.clock()
	for _, npc in ipairs(npcs:GetChildren()) do
		local nh = npc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local nr = npc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if nh and nr and nh.Health > 0 then
			local off = nr.Position - hrp.Position
			local flat = Vector3.new(off.X, 0, off.Z)
			if flat.Magnitude <= KICK_RANGE and flat.Magnitude > 0.05 and hrp.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(flat.Unit) > 0.2 then
				npc:SetAttribute("LastHitBy", player.UserId)
				nh:TakeDamage(KICK_DAMAGE)
				nr.AssemblyLinearVelocity = flat.Unit * 38 + Vector3.new(0, 16, 0)
				send(player, "Hit", nr.Position, KICK_DAMAGE)
			end
		end
	end
end)

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
	local join = player:GetJoinData()
	local data = join and join.TeleportData
	trials[player] = {
		kills = 0,
		lastKick = 0,
		done = false,
		back = (type(data) == "table" and data.back) or MAIN_PLACE_ID,
		valid = type(data) == "table" and data.quest == "Mirror",
		warned = type(data) == "table" and data.quest == "Joris",
	}
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		local t = trials[player]
		if t and t.valid then
			local hum = player.Character and player.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
			if hum then
				hum.Died:Connect(function()
					if t.done or t.failed then
						return
					end
					t.deaths = (t.deaths or 0) + 1
					send(player, "Lives", math.max(0, 2 - t.deaths))
					if t.deaths >= 2 then
						t.failed = true
						task.delay(4, function()
							if player.Parent and t.back and t.back ~= 0 then
								pcall(function()
									TeleportService:TeleportAsync(t.back, { player })
								end)
							end
						end)
					end
				end)
			end
			task.spawn(startTrial, player)
		elseif t and not t.warned then
			task.wait(0.3)
			local c = player.Character
			if c then
				c:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, 8005, 0))
			end
		end
	end)
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	local t = trials[player]
	if t and t.npc then
		t.npc:Destroy()
	end
	trials[player] = nil
end)

local lastKick = 0
local locked = false

local function motorOf(char, name)
	local m = char:FindFirstChild(name, true)
	return m and m:IsA("Motor6D") and m or nil
end

local function pose(list, info)
	for _, e in ipairs(list) do
		if e.m and e.m.Parent then
			TweenService:Create(e.m, info, { C0 = e.base * e.rot }):Play()
		end
	end
end

local function slash(char)
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then
		return
	end
	local ring = new("Part", {
		Anchored = true,
		CanCollide = false,
		CanQuery = false,
		CanTouch = false,
		Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder,
		Size = Vector3.new(0.15, 3, 3),
		Material = Enum.Material.Neon,
		Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Transparency = 0.15,
		CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -0.6, -3.2) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(90)),
		Parent = Workspace,
	})
	TweenService:Create(ring, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Size = Vector3.new(0.15, 9, 9),
		Transparency = 1,
	}):Play()
	Debris:AddItem(ring, 0.4)
end

local function swing()
	local char = player.Character
	if not char then
		return
	end
	local function entry(name)
		local m = motorOf(char, name)
		return { m = m, base = m and m.C0 or CFrame.new(), rot = CFrame.new() }
	end
	local hip, knee, waist = entry("RightHip"), entry("RightKnee"), entry("Waist")
	local lArm, rArm = entry("LeftShoulder"), entry("RightShoulder")
	local all = { hip, knee, waist, lArm, rArm }
	local function set(e, x, y, z)
		e.rot = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(x or 0), math.rad(y or 0), math.rad(z or 0))
	end
	set(hip, -30)
	set(knee, 55)
	set(waist, 10)
	set(lArm, 30, 0, 20)
	set(rArm, -30, 0, -20)
	pose(all, TweenInfo.new(0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out))
	task.delay(0.08, function()
		set(hip, 100)
		set(knee, -5)
		set(waist, -14)
		set(lArm, -50, 0, 25)
		set(rArm, 50, 0, -25)
		pose(all, TweenInfo.new(0.07, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out))
		slash(char)
		sound("swordslash.wav", 0.7, 1.15)
	end)
	task.delay(0.26, function()
		for _, e in ipairs(all) do
			e.rot = CFrame.new()
		end
		pose(all, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out))
	end)
end

local function kick()
	if locked or os.clock() - lastKick < COOLDOWN then
		return
	end
	lastKick = os.clock()
	KickRemote:FireServer()
	swing()
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
	if processed then
		return
	end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F then
		kick()
	end
end)

-- KickArenaAdmin (Script in ServerScriptService): owner tools. Events (server or global), gifts for online and offline players
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local DataStoreService = game:GetService("DataStoreService")
local MessagingService = game:GetService("MessagingService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local MemoryStoreService = game:GetService("MemoryStoreService")
local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")

local OWNER_ID = 8469510776
local INF_VALUE = 1e12
local TOPIC = "KickArenaEvents_v1"
local MAX_QUEUED_GIFTS = 25

local giftStore = DataStoreService:GetDataStore("KickArenaGifts_v1")
local questStore = DataStoreService:GetDataStore("KickArenaQuests_v2")

local Core
repeat
	Core = _G.KickArenaCore
	if not Core then
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until Core

local Ext
repeat
	Ext = _G.KickArenaExt
	if not (Ext and Ext.ready) then
		Ext = nil
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until Ext

local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("KickArenaRemotes")
local MailApi
repeat
	MailApi = _G.KickArenaMail
	if not MailApi then
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until MailApi

local Hub = Instance.new("RemoteFunction")
Hub.Name = "AdminHub"
Hub.Parent = remotes

local EVENTS = { PaintTime = true, Rain = true, Mirror = true, MaxTycoon = true, TimeStop = true, AllSmile = true, BossPortal = true, ErrorEvent = true }

local function runEvent(name)
	if name == "PaintTime" then
		return Ext.paintTime(60)
	elseif name == "Rain" then
		return Ext.startRain(30)
	elseif name == "MaxTycoon" then
		local n = Ext.maxAllTycoons()
		return n > 0, n > 0 and (n .. " tycoon(s) maxed") or "No tycoons in this server"
	elseif name == "TimeStop" then
		if Workspace:GetAttribute("KickTimeStop") then
			return false, "A time stop is already running"
		end
		local adminPlayer = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(OWNER_ID)
		if adminPlayer and Ext.godTimeStop(adminPlayer) then
			return true, "Time stop started (you are not frozen)"
		end
		return false, "You must be alive in the arena"
	elseif name == "BossPortal" then
		local adminPlayer = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(OWNER_ID)
		local api = _G.KickArenaJoris
		if not adminPlayer or not api then
			return false, "Portal system is not ready"
		end
		return api.adminPortal(adminPlayer)
	elseif name == "ErrorEvent" then
		local api = _G.KickArenaError
		if not api then
			return false, "Error system is not ready"
		end
		return api.startEvent(60)
	elseif name == "AllSmile" then
		local n = Ext.smileAll(60)
		return n > 0, n > 0 and ("Smile ball for " .. n .. " players/dummies, 60 sec") or "Nobody is in the arena"
	elseif name == "Mirror" then
		local alive = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if Core.getAlive(p) then
				table.insert(alive, p)
			end
		end
		if #alive == 0 then
			return false, "No player is alive in this server"
		end
		return Ext.spawnMirrorProp(alive[math.random(#alive)])
	end
	return false, "Unknown event"
end

pcall(function()
	MessagingService:SubscribeAsync(TOPIC, function(message)
		local data = message.Data
		if type(data) == "table" and EVENTS[data.event] then
			runEvent(data.event)
		end
	end)
end)

local function parseAmount(text)
	text = string.lower((string.gsub(text, "%s", "")))
	if text == "inf" or text == "infinite" then
		return INF_VALUE
	end
	local n = tonumber(text)
	if not n or n ~= n then
		return nil
	end
	return math.clamp(math.floor(n), -INF_VALUE, INF_VALUE)
end

local function applyGift(player, gift)
	if gift.kind == "Kicks" then
		local kv = Core.getKicksValue(player)
		if not kv then
			return false
		end
		kv.Value = math.clamp(kv.Value + gift.amount, 0, INF_VALUE)
		if gift.amount > 0 then
			Core.notify(player, ("Gift received: +%s Kicks"):format(gift.amount >= INF_VALUE and "infinite" or Core.formatNumber(gift.amount)), true)
		end
		return true
	elseif gift.kind == "Unlock" then
		local cfg = Core.KICKS[gift.kick]
		if not cfg or not cfg.Badge then
			return false
		end
		Core.unlockKick(player, gift.kick)
		Core.notify(player, ("Gift received: %s unlocked"):format(cfg.Display), true)
		return true
	end
	return false
end

local function queueGift(userId, gift)
	local queued = false
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		giftStore:UpdateAsync("g_" .. userId, function(old)
			old = type(old) == "table" and old or {}
			if #old >= MAX_QUEUED_GIFTS then
				return nil
			end
			table.insert(old, gift)
			queued = true
			return old
		end)
	end)
	if not ok then
		return false, "Could not save the gift: " .. tostring(err)
	end
	if not queued then
		return false, "That player already has too many pending gifts"
	end
	return true
end

local function claimGifts(player)
	local key = "g_" .. player.UserId
	local ok, list = pcall(giftStore.RemoveAsync, giftStore, key)
	if not ok or type(list) ~= "table" or #list == 0 then
		return
	end
	for _ = 1, 60 do
		if Core.questData[player] and Core.getKicksValue(player) then
			break
		end
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
	for _, gift in ipairs(list) do
		if player.Parent and type(gift) == "table" then
			applyGift(player, gift)
		else
			queueGift(player.UserId, gift)
		end
	end
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
	task.spawn(claimGifts, player)
end)
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	task.spawn(claimGifts, p)
end

local function resolveTarget(target)
	if type(target) == "table" and type(target.id) == "number" then
		return target.id, nil
	end
	if type(target) == "string" and #target >= 3 and #target <= 20 and target:match("^[%w_]+$") then
		local ok, id = pcall(Players.GetUserIdFromNameAsync, Players, target)
		if ok and id then
			return id, target
		end
		return nil, "Player not found"
	end
	return nil, "Pick a player or type a valid username"
end

local function give(gift, target)
	local userId, err = resolveTarget(target)
	if not userId then
		return false, err
	end
	local online = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
	if online then
		if applyGift(online, gift) then
			return true, "Sent to " .. online.Name
		end
		return false, online.Name .. " is not ready yet, try again"
	end
	local ok, message = queueGift(userId, gift)
	if not ok then
		return false, message
	end
	return true, "Offline player: the gift is waiting and arrives when they join"
end

local function giveMail(target, gift)
	local userId, err = resolveTarget(target)
	if not userId then
		return false, err
	end
	local text = gift.kind == "Kicks" and (gift.amount >= INF_VALUE and "Infinite Kicks" or Core.formatNumber(gift.amount) .. " Kicks") or (Core.KICKS[gift.kick].Display .. " unlock")
	local mail, merr = MailApi.make("A gift for you", "The admin team sent you a gift: " .. text .. ". Press Claim to receive it.", gift, OWNER_ID)
	if not mail then
		return false, merr
	end
	return MailApi.sendTo(userId, mail)
end

local function resetKick(data, kick)
	data.Unlocked = data.Unlocked or {}
	data.Unlocked[kick] = nil
	if kick == "Crazy" then
		data.Hits = 0
	elseif kick == "Dizzy" then
		data.HitBy = {}
	elseif kick == "SixPack" then
		data.Kills = 0
	end
end

local function deleteKick(target, kick)
	local cfg = type(kick) == "string" and Core.KICKS[kick]
	if not cfg or not cfg.Badge then
		return false, "Only quest kicks can be deleted"
	end
	local userId, err = resolveTarget(target)
	if not userId then
		return false, err
	end
	local online = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
	if online then
		local data = Core.questData[online]
		if not data then
			return false, online.Name .. " is not ready yet, try again"
		end
		resetKick(data, kick)
		if online:GetAttribute("EquippedKick") == kick then
			online:SetAttribute("EquippedKick", "Normal")
		end
		task.spawn(Core.saveQuests, online)
		Core.notify(online, cfg.Display .. " was removed from your account. Complete its quest again to get it back", false)
		return true, cfg.Display .. " removed from " .. online.Name
	end
	local found = false
	local ok, e = pcall(function()
		questStore:UpdateAsync("u_" .. userId, function(old)
			if type(old) ~= "table" then
				return nil
			end
			found = true
			resetKick(old, kick)
			return old
		end)
	end)
	if not ok then
		return false, "Could not update the data: " .. tostring(e)
	end
	if not found then
		return false, "No saved data for that player"
	end
	return true, cfg.Display .. " removed from the offline player"
end

-- MODERATION: ban, unban, watch, find players in other servers
local UNITS = {
	s = 1, sec = 1, secs = 1, second = 1, seconds = 1, detik = 1,
	m = 60, min = 60, mins = 60, minute = 60, minutes = 60, menit = 60,
	h = 3600, hr = 3600, hour = 3600, hours = 3600, jam = 3600,
	d = 86400, day = 86400, days = 86400, hari = 86400,
	mon = 2592000, month = 2592000, months = 2592000, bulan = 2592000,
	y = 31536000, yr = 31536000, year = 31536000, years = 31536000, tahun = 31536000,
}
local function parseDuration(text)
	text = string.lower((string.gsub(text, "^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")))
	if text == "" then
		return -1
	end
	local total = 0
	for num, unit in string.gmatch(text, "(%d+)%s*(%a+)") do
		local mult = UNITS[unit]
		if not mult then
			return nil
		end
		total += tonumber(num) * mult
	end
	local rest = string.gsub(string.gsub(text, "(%d+)%s*(%a+)", ""), "[%s,]+", "")
	if rest ~= "" or total <= 0 then
		return nil
	end
	return math.min(total, 31536000 * 100)
end
local function durationText(secs)
	if secs < 0 then
		return "permanently"
	end
	local parts = {}
	for _, u in ipairs({ { "y", 31536000 }, { "mon", 2592000 }, { "d", 86400 }, { "h", 3600 }, { "m", 60 }, { "s", 1 } }) do
		if secs >= u[2] then
			table.insert(parts, math.floor(secs / u[2]) .. u[1])
			secs = secs % u[2]
		end
	end
	return "for " .. table.concat(parts, " ")
end
local function findUser(name)
	if type(name) ~= "string" then
		return nil, "Enter a player name"
	end
	name = string.lower((string.gsub(name, "^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")))
	if #name < 2 or #name > 40 then
		return nil, "Enter a player name"
	end
	local match
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local n, d = string.lower(p.Name), string.lower(p.DisplayName)
		if n == name or d == name then
			return p.UserId, p.Name
		end
		if string.sub(n, 1, #name) == name or string.sub(d, 1, #name) == name then
			match = match or p
		end
	end
	if match then
		return match.UserId, match.Name
	end
	local ok, id = pcall(Players.GetUserIdFromNameAsync, Players, name)
	if ok and id then
		return id, name
	end
	return nil, "Player not found"
end

local presence
pcall(function()
	presence = MemoryStoreService:GetHashMap("KickArenaPresence_v1")
end)
task.spawn(function()
	while presence and game.PrivateServerId == "" do
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			pcall(presence.SetAsync, presence, tostring(p.UserId), { j = game.JobId, p = game.PlaceId }, 80)
		end
		task.wait(30)
	end
end)

local hidden = {}
local function applyHidden(player, st)
	local char = player.Character
	if not char then
		return
	end
	for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
		if (d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Decal")) and st.tr[d] == nil then
			st.tr[d] = d.Transparency
			d.Transparency = 1
		elseif d:IsA("BillboardGui") and st.gui[d] == nil then
			st.gui[d] = d.Enabled
			d.Enabled = false
		end
	end
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if hum then
		hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
	end
	if hrp then
		hrp.Anchored = true
		hrp.CanQuery, hrp.CanTouch = false, false
	end
	local ff = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField")
	if not ff then
		ff = Instance.new("ForceField")
		ff.Visible = false
		ff.Parent = char
	end
end
local function setHidden(player, on)
	local st = hidden[player]
	if on and not st then
		st = { tr = {}, gui = {} }
		hidden[player] = st
		local ls = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
		if ls then
			st.ls = ls
			ls.Parent = ServerStorage
		end
		player:SetAttribute("Hidden", true)
		player:SetAttribute("NoKick", true)
		player:SetAttribute("KickImmune", true)
		applyHidden(player, st)
		st.conn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
			task.wait(0.6)
			if hidden[player] then
				applyHidden(player, hidden[player])
			end
		end)
	elseif not on and st then
		hidden[player] = nil
		st.conn:Disconnect()
		if st.ls and st.ls.Parent == ServerStorage then
			st.ls.Parent = player
		end
		player:SetAttribute("Hidden", nil)
		player:SetAttribute("NoKick", nil)
		player:SetAttribute("KickImmune", nil)
		for d, v in pairs(st.tr) do
			if d.Parent then
				d.Transparency = v
			end
		end
		for d, v in pairs(st.gui) do
			if d.Parent then
				d.Enabled = v
			end
		end
		local char = player.Character
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if hum then
			hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.Viewer
		end
		if hrp then
			hrp.Anchored = false
			hrp.CanQuery, hrp.CanTouch = true, true
		end
		local ff = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField")
		if ff then
			ff:Destroy()
		end
	end
	return hidden[player] ~= nil
end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
	hidden[p] = nil
end)

local function lookup(name)
	local id, who = findUser(name)
	if not id then
		return nil, who
	end
	local online = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(id)
	if online then
		return id, who, "here"
	end
	if not presence then
		return id, who, nil
	end
	local ok, value = pcall(presence.GetAsync, presence, tostring(id))
	if ok and type(value) == "table" and value.j then
		return id, who, value
	end
	return id, who, nil
end

local last = {}
Hub.OnServerInvoke = function(player, action, a, b, c)
	if player.UserId ~= OWNER_ID then
		return false, "Not allowed"
	end
	local now = os.clock()
	if last[player] and now - last[player] < 0.4 then
		return false, "Slow down"
	end
	last[player] = now
	if action == "Event" then
		if type(a) ~= "string" or not EVENTS[a] then
			return false, "Unknown event"
		end
		if b == "Global" and a ~= "TimeStop" and a ~= "BossPortal" then
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				MessagingService:PublishAsync(TOPIC, { event = a })
			end)
			if ok then
				return true, "Event sent to every server"
			end
			local done, message = runEvent(a)
			return done, "Global send failed (" .. tostring(err) .. "). Local result: " .. tostring(message)
		end
		return runEvent(a)
	elseif action == "GiveKicks" then
		if type(b) ~= "string" or #b > 30 then
			return false, "Invalid amount"
		end
		local amount = parseAmount(b)
		if not amount or amount == 0 then
			return false, "Enter a number (like 500) or inf"
		end
		local gift = { kind = "Kicks", amount = amount }
		if c == true then
			return giveMail(a, gift)
		end
		return give(gift, a)
	elseif action == "GiveKick" then
		local cfg = type(b) == "string" and Core.KICKS[b]
		if not cfg or not cfg.Badge then
			return false, "That kick cannot be gifted"
		end
		local gift = { kind = "Unlock", kick = b }
		if c == true then
			return giveMail(a, gift)
		end
		return give(gift, a)
	elseif action == "DeleteKick" then
		return deleteKick(a, b)
	elseif action == "Ban" then
		local id, who = findUser(a)
		if not id then
			return false, who
		end
		if id == OWNER_ID then
			return false, "You cannot ban the owner"
		end
		local dur = parseDuration(type(b) == "string" and b or "")
		if not dur then
			return false, "Time format: 1 h, 30 m, 2 d, 1 mon, 1 y. Empty = permanent"
		end
		local reason = type(c) == "string" and string.sub(c, 1, 300) or ""
		if reason == "" then
			reason = "No reason given"
		end
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			Players:BanAsync({
				UserIds = { id },
				Duration = dur,
				DisplayReason = reason,
				PrivateReason = "Banned from the admin panel",
				ExcludeAltAccounts = false,
				ApplyToUniverse = true,
			})
		end)
		if not ok then
			return false, "Ban failed: " .. tostring(err)
		end
		return true, ("%s banned %s"):format(who, durationText(dur))
	elseif action == "Unban" then
		local id, who = findUser(a)
		if not id then
			return false, who
		end
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			Players:UnbanAsync({ UserIds = { id }, ApplyToUniverse = true })
		end)
		if not ok then
			return false, "Unban failed: " .. tostring(err)
		end
		return true, who .. " was unbanned"
	elseif action == "Spectate" then
		local on = setHidden(player, a == true)
		return true, on and "Spectating. You are hidden from the game" or "Spectate off. You are visible again"
	elseif action == "Find" then
		local id, who, where = lookup(a)
		if not id then
			return false, who
		end
		if where == "here" then
			return true, who .. " is in this server"
		elseif where then
			return true, who .. " is in another server. Press Join Server"
		end
		return false, who .. " is not online in any server"
	elseif action == "Join" then
		local id, who, where = lookup(a)
		if not id then
			return false, who
		end
		if where == "here" then
			return false, who .. " is already in this server"
		elseif not where then
			return false, who .. " is not online in any server"
		end
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(where.p, where.j, player)
		end)
		if not ok then
			return false, "Teleport failed: " .. tostring(err)
		end
		return true, "Joining " .. who
	elseif action == "SendMail" then
		if type(a) ~= "table" or type(b) ~= "table" then
			return false, "Invalid mail"
		end
		local title, body = a.title, a.body
		if type(title) ~= "string" or type(body) ~= "string" or #title < 1 or #title > 60 or #body > 300 then
			return false, "Write a title (max 60) and a message (max 300)"
		end
		local gift = nil
		if type(a.kicks) == "string" and #a.kicks > 0 then
			local amount = parseAmount(a.kicks)
			if not amount or amount == 0 then
				return false, "Gift amount must be a number or inf"
			end
			gift = { kind = "Kicks", amount = amount }
		elseif type(a.kick) == "string" and a.kick ~= "" then
			local cfg = Core.KICKS[a.kick]
			if not cfg or not cfg.Badge then
				return false, "That kick cannot be gifted"
			end
			gift = { kind = "Unlock", kick = a.kick }
		end
		local mail, err = MailApi.make(title, body, gift, OWNER_ID)
		if not mail then
			return false, err
		end
		if b.scope == "Global" then
			return MailApi.sendGlobal(mail)
		elseif b.scope == "Server" then
			return MailApi.sendServer(mail)
		end
		local userId, terr = resolveTarget(b.target)
		if not userId then
			return false, terr
		end
		return MailApi.sendTo(userId, mail)
	end
	return false, "Unknown action"
end

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	last[player] = nil
end)

-- KickArenaMail (Script in ServerScriptService): mailbox with claimable gifts, global mails, saved settings, "Infinite" leaderboard text
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local DataStoreService = game:GetService("DataStoreService")
local MessagingService = game:GetService("MessagingService")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local INF_VALUE = 1e12
local LEADERBOARD_TEXT = true -- shows "Infinite" on the leaderboard (set false if your own save script reads leaderstats.Kicks by name)
local MAX_MAILS = 40
local GLOBAL_KEEP = 30
local GLOBAL_MAX_AGE = 30 * 86400
local TOPIC_TO = "KickArenaMailTo_v1"
local TOPIC_ALL = "KickArenaMailAll_v1"

local mailStore = DataStoreService:GetDataStore("KickArenaMail_v1")
local settingsStore = DataStoreService:GetDataStore("KickArenaSettings_v1")

local Core
repeat
	Core = _G.KickArenaCore
	if not Core then
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until Core

local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("KickArenaRemotes")
local MailSync = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
MailSync.Name = "MailSync"
MailSync.Parent = remotes
local MailAction = Instance.new("RemoteFunction")
MailAction.Name = "MailAction"
MailAction.Parent = remotes
local SettingsSave = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
SettingsSave.Name = "SettingsSave"
SettingsSave.Parent = remotes

local boxes = {}

local function newId()
	return ("%d_%d"):format(os.time(), math.random(100000, 999999))
end

local function clean(mail)
	if type(mail) ~= "table" or type(mail.id) ~= "string" then
		return nil
	end
	local gift = nil
	if type(mail.gift) == "table" then
		if mail.gift.kind == "Kicks" and type(mail.gift.amount) == "number" then
			gift = { kind = "Kicks", amount = math.clamp(math.floor(mail.gift.amount), -INF_VALUE, INF_VALUE) }
		elseif mail.gift.kind == "Unlock" and type(mail.gift.kick) == "string" and Core.KICKS[mail.gift.kick] then
			gift = { kind = "Unlock", kick = mail.gift.kick }
		end
	end
	return {
		id = mail.id,
		title = tostring(mail.title or "Mail"):sub(1, 60),
		body = tostring(mail.body or ""):sub(1, 300),
		gift = gift,
		t = tonumber(mail.t) or os.time(),
		read = mail.read == true,
		claimed = mail.claimed == true,
	}
end

local function hasMail(box, id)
	for _, m in ipairs(box.list) do
		if m.id == id then
			return m
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function push(player, kind, payload)
	MailSync:FireClient(player, kind, payload)
end

local function addToBox(player, mail, silent)
	local box = boxes[player]
	mail = clean(mail)
	if not box or not mail or hasMail(box, mail.id) then
		return false
	end
	table.insert(box.list, 1, mail)
	while #box.list > MAX_MAILS do
		local dropIndex = #box.list
		for i = #box.list, 1, -1 do
			if box.list[i].claimed or not box.list[i].gift then
				dropIndex = i
				break
			end
		end
		table.remove(box.list, dropIndex)
	end
	box.dirty = true
	push(player, "Add", mail)
	if not silent then
		Core.notify(player, "New mail: " .. mail.title, true)
	end
	return true
end

local function saveBox(player, uid)
	local box = boxes[player]
	if not box or not box.loaded or not box.dirty then
		return
	end
	box.dirty = false
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		mailStore:UpdateAsync("m_" .. uid, function(old)
			old = type(old) == "table" and old or {}
			local byId, out = {}, {}
			for _, m in ipairs(box.list) do
				byId[m.id] = true
				table.insert(out, m)
			end
			for _, m in ipairs(old.m or {}) do
				if type(m) == "table" and not byId[m.id] and not box.removed[m.id] then
					table.insert(out, m)
				end
			end
			while #out > MAX_MAILS do
				table.remove(out)
			end
			return { m = out, g = box.gseen }
		end)
	end)
	if not ok then
		box.dirty = true
		warn("[KickArenaMail] Save failed: " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

local function loadBox(player)
	local box = { list = {}, removed = {}, loaded = false, dirty = false, gseen = 0 }
	boxes[player] = box
	local data
	for _ = 1, 3 do
		local ok, result = pcall(mailStore.GetAsync, mailStore, "m_" .. player.UserId)
		if ok then
			data = result or {}
			break
		end
		task.wait(2)
	end
	if not data then
		return
	end
	for _, m in ipairs(data.m or {}) do
		local c = clean(m)
		if c then
			table.insert(box.list, c)
		end
	end
	box.gseen = tonumber(data.g) or 0
	box.loaded = true
	local okG, globals = pcall(mailStore.GetAsync, mailStore, "GLOBAL")
	if okG and type(globals) == "table" then
		table.sort(globals, function(a, b)
			return (a.t or 0) < (b.t or 0)
		end)
		for _, m in ipairs(globals) do
			if type(m) == "table" and (m.t or 0) > box.gseen and os.time() - (m.t or 0) < GLOBAL_MAX_AGE then
				addToBox(player, m, true)
				box.gseen = math.max(box.gseen, m.t or 0)
			end
		end
	end
	push(player, "Full", box.list)
end

local function filterText(text, fromUserId)
	if text == "" then
		return ""
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local filtered = TextService:FilterStringAsync(text, fromUserId, Enum.TextFilterContext.PublicChat)
		return filtered:GetNonChatStringForBroadcastAsync()
	end)
	if ok and result then
		return result
	end
	if RunService:IsStudio() then
		return text
	end
	return nil
end

local function applyGift(player, gift)
	if gift.kind == "Kicks" then
		local kv = Core.getKicksValue(player)
		if not kv then
			return false, "Your stats are still loading"
		end
		kv.Value = math.clamp(kv.Value + gift.amount, 0, INF_VALUE)
		return true, gift.amount >= INF_VALUE and "Infinite Kicks claimed!" or ("+%s Kicks claimed!"):format(Core.formatNumber(gift.amount))
	elseif gift.kind == "Unlock" then
		local cfg = Core.KICKS[gift.kick]
		if not cfg or not cfg.Badge then
			return false, "This gift is no longer valid"
		end
		if not Core.questData[player] then
			return false, "Your data is still loading"
		end
		Core.unlockKick(player, gift.kick)
		return true, cfg.Display .. " unlocked!"
	end
	return false, "Unknown gift"
end

MailAction.OnServerInvoke = function(player, action, id)
	local box = boxes[player]
	if not box or not box.loaded then
		return false, "Mailbox is loading"
	end
	if action == "ClaimAll" then
		local count = 0
		for _, m in ipairs(box.list) do
			if m.gift and not m.claimed then
				local ok = applyGift(player, m.gift)
				if ok then
					m.claimed, m.read = true, true
					count += 1
					push(player, "Update", m)
				end
			end
		end
		box.dirty = true
		return count > 0, count > 0 and ("Claimed %d gift%s"):format(count, count == 1 and "" or "s") or "Nothing to claim"
	end
	if type(id) ~= "string" then
		return false, "Invalid mail"
	end
	local mail = hasMail(box, id)
	if not mail then
		return false, "Mail not found"
	end
	if action == "Read" then
		mail.read = true
		box.dirty = true
		return true, ""
	elseif action == "Claim" then
		if not mail.gift or mail.claimed then
			return false, "Nothing to claim"
		end
		local ok, message = applyGift(player, mail.gift)
		if ok then
			mail.claimed, mail.read = true, true
			box.dirty = true
			push(player, "Update", mail)
		end
		return ok, message
	elseif action == "Delete" then
		if mail.gift and not mail.claimed then
			return false, "Claim the gift first"
		end
		for i, m in ipairs(box.list) do
			if m.id == id then
				table.remove(box.list, i)
				break
			end
		end
		box.removed[id] = true
		box.dirty = true
		push(player, "Remove", id)
		return true, ""
	end
	return false, "Unknown action"
end

local function appendStore(uid, mail)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		mailStore:UpdateAsync("m_" .. uid, function(old)
			old = type(old) == "table" and old or { m = {}, g = 0 }
			old.m = old.m or {}
			table.insert(old.m, 1, mail)
			while #old.m > MAX_MAILS do
				table.remove(old.m)
			end
			return old
		end)
	end)
	return ok, err
end

pcall(function()
	MessagingService:SubscribeAsync(TOPIC_TO, function(message)
		local d = message.Data
		if type(d) == "table" and type(d.uid) == "number" then
			local p = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(d.uid)
			if p then
				addToBox(p, d.mail)
			end
		end
	end)
	MessagingService:SubscribeAsync(TOPIC_ALL, function(message)
		local d = message.Data
		if type(d) == "table" and type(d.mail) == "table" then
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				addToBox(p, d.mail)
				local box = boxes[p]
				if box then
					box.gseen = math.max(box.gseen, d.mail.t or 0)
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end)

local API = {}

function API.make(title, body, gift, fromUserId)
	local t = filterText(title, fromUserId)
	local b = filterText(body, fromUserId)
	if not t or not b then
		return nil, "Text could not be filtered, try again"
	end
	return { id = newId(), title = t, body = b, gift = gift, t = os.time(), read = false, claimed = false }
end

function API.sendTo(userId, mail)
	local p = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
	if p then
		if addToBox(p, mail) then
			return true, "Mail sent to " .. p.Name
		end
		return false, "Mailbox is not ready yet"
	end
	local ok, err = appendStore(userId, mail)
	if not ok then
		return false, "Could not save the mail: " .. tostring(err)
	end
	pcall(function()
		MessagingService:PublishAsync(TOPIC_TO, { uid = userId, mail = mail })
	end)
	return true, "Offline player: the mail is waiting in their inbox"
end

function API.sendServer(mail)
	local count = 0
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if addToBox(p, mail) then
			count += 1
		end
	end
	return true, ("Mail sent to %d player%s in this server"):format(count, count == 1 and "" or "s")
end

function API.sendGlobal(mail)
	pcall(function()
		mailStore:UpdateAsync("GLOBAL", function(old)
			old = type(old) == "table" and old or {}
			table.insert(old, mail)
			while #old > GLOBAL_KEEP do
				table.remove(old, 1)
			end
			return old
		end)
	end)
	local ok = pcall(function()
		MessagingService:PublishAsync(TOPIC_ALL, { mail = mail })
	end)
	if not ok then
		API.sendServer(mail)
		return true, "Saved for everyone. Live delivery failed, so only this server got it now"
	end
	return true, "Mail sent to every server. Players who join later get it too"
end

_G.KickArenaMail = API

-- SETTINGS (saved per player, applied on the client)
local SETTING_KEYS = {
	Sfx = "number",
	Music = "number",
	Vfx = "boolean",
	Shake = "boolean",
	Numbers = "boolean",
	Labels = "boolean",
	Toasts = "boolean",
}
local lastSave = {}

local function loadSettings(player)
	local ok, data = pcall(settingsStore.GetAsync, settingsStore, "s_" .. player.UserId)
	local out = {}
	if ok and type(data) == "table" then
		for key, kind in pairs(SETTING_KEYS) do
			if typeof(data[key]) == kind then
				out[key] = data[key]
			end
		end
	end
	player:SetAttribute("KickSettings", HttpService:JSONEncode(out))
end

SettingsSave.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, data)
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return
	end
	local now = os.clock()
	if lastSave[player] and now - lastSave[player] < 1 then
		return
	end
	lastSave[player] = now
	local clean = {}
	for key, kind in pairs(SETTING_KEYS) do
		local v = data[key]
		if typeof(v) == kind then
			clean[key] = kind == "number" and math.clamp(v, 0, 1) or v
		end
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(settingsStore.SetAsync, settingsStore, "s_" .. player.UserId, clean)
	end)
end)

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
	task.spawn(loadSettings, player)
end)
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	task.spawn(loadSettings, p)
end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	lastSave[player] = nil
end)

-- INFINITE KICKS ON THE LEADERBOARD
local function watchInfinite(player)
	if not LEADERBOARD_TEXT then
		return
	end
	local kv
	for _ = 1, 60 do
		kv = Core.getKicksValue(player)
		if kv then
			break
		end
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
	local ls = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
	if not kv or not ls then
		return
	end
	local label = nil
	local function update()
		if not player.Parent then
			return
		end
		if kv.Value >= INF_VALUE then
			if not label then
				kv.Name = "KicksValue"
				label = Instance.new("StringValue")
				label.Name = "Kicks"
				label.Value = "Infinite"
				label.Parent = ls
				kv.Parent = player
			end
		elseif label then
			label:Destroy()
			label = nil
			kv.Parent = ls
			kv.Name = "Kicks"
		end
	end
	kv:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(update)
	update()
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
	task.spawn(watchInfinite, player)
end)
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	task.spawn(watchInfinite, p)
end

local saveToken = 0
local function save()
	saveToken += 1
	local token = saveToken
	task.delay(0.9, function()
		if token == saveToken then

			end
		end
	end)

local function loadSaved()
	local raw = player:GetAttribute("KickSettings")
	if raw == nil then
		for _ = 1, 40 do
			task.wait(0.25)
			raw = player:GetAttribute("KickSettings")
			if raw ~= nil then
				break
			end
		end
	end
	if type(raw) == "string" then
		local ok, data = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, raw)
		if ok and type(data) == "table" then
			for k, default in pairs(DEFAULTS) do
				if typeof(data[k]) == typeof(default) then
					S[k] = data[k]
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

-- PANEL HELPER
local function makePanel(name, width, height, title)
	local scale = new("UIScale", { Scale = 0.92 })
	local panel = new("CanvasGroup", {
		Name = name,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height),
		BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Panel,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.03,
		GroupTransparency = 1,
		Active = true,
		Visible = false,
		ZIndex = 5,
		Parent = gui,
	}, {
		corner(UDim.new(0, 16)),
		new("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Transparency = 0.86, Thickness = 1 }),
		new("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(width, height) }),
		scale,
	})
	new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 46),
		FontFace = BOLD,
		TextSize = 17,
		TextColor3 = THEME.Text,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		Text = title,
		Parent = panel,
	})
	local close = new("TextButton", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 9),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28),
		BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Row,
		AutoButtonColor = true,
		FontFace = BOLD,
		TextSize = 14,
		TextColor3 = THEME.Text,
		Text = "X",
		Parent = panel,
	}, { corner(UDim.new(1, 0)) })
	local state = { open = false }
	function state.set(on)
		state.open = on
		if on then
			panel.Visible = true
			panel.GroupTransparency = 1
			scale.Scale = 0.92
			TweenService:Create(panel, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { GroupTransparency = 0 }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
		else
			TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Scale = 0.92 }):Play()
			local t = TweenService:Create(panel, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { GroupTransparency = 1 })
			t.Completed:Once(function()
				if not state.open then
					panel.Visible = false
				end
			end)
			t:Play()
		end
	end
	close.Activated:Connect(function()
		state.set(false)
	end)
	state.panel = panel
	return state
end

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)

local KICK_NAMES = {
	Dizzy = "Dizzy Kick", Crazy = "Crazy Kick", FrostFire = "Frost Fire Kick", Lightning = "Lightning Kick",
	Gold = "Gold Stone Kick", SixPack = "Six Pack Kick", Mirror = "Mirror Kick", Painting = "Painting Kick",
}
local mails = {}

local mailRemote = remotes:WaitForChild("MailAction", 20)
local statusText = new("TextLabel", {
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
	Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -6),
	Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 18),
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	FontFace = MED,
	TextSize = 12,
	TextColor3 = THEME.Muted,

	ZIndex = 6,
	Parent = mailsPanel.panel,
})

local function say(text, good)
	statusText.Text = text or ""
	statusText.TextColor3 = good and THEME.Green or THEME.Red
	task.delay(3, function()
		if statusText.Text == text then
			statusText.Text = ""
		end
	end)
end

local function call(action, id)
	if not mailRemote then
		return false
	end
	local ok, success, message = pcall(mailRemote.InvokeServer, mailRemote, action, id)
	if not ok then
		say("Request failed", false)
		return false
	end
	if message and message ~= "" then
		say(message, success)
	end
	return success
end

local function giftText(gift)
	if gift.kind == "Kicks" then
		if gift.amount >= 1e12 then
			return "Infinite Kicks"
		end
		return (gift.amount >= 0 and "+" or "") .. tostring(gift.amount):reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse():gsub("^,", "") .. " Kicks"
	end
	return KICK_NAMES[gift.kick] or gift.kick
end

local function ago(t)
	local d = math.max(os.time() - t, 0)
	if d < 60 then
		return "just now"
	elseif d < 3600 then
		return math.floor(d / 60) .. " min ago"
	elseif d < 86400 then
		return math.floor(d / 3600) .. " h ago"
	end
	return math.floor(d / 86400) .. " d ago"
end

local function updateBadge()
	local count = 0
	for _, m in ipairs(mails) do
		if (m.gift and not m.claimed) or (not m.gift and not m.read) then
			count += 1
		end
	end
	badge.Visible = count > 0
	badge.Text = count > 9 and "9+" or tostring(count)
	empty.Visible = #mails == 0
end

local function render()
	for _, c in pairs(cards) do
		c:Destroy()
	end
	cards = {}
	for i, m in ipairs(mails) do
		local unclaimed = m.gift and not m.claimed
		local card = new("Frame", {
			LayoutOrder = i,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Row,
			Parent = mList,
		}, {
			corner(UDim.new(0, 12)),
			new("UIStroke", { Color = unclaimed and THEME.Green or Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Transparency = unclaimed and 0.5 or 0.9, Thickness = 1 }),
			new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10) }),
			new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 5), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }),
		})
		cards[m.id] = card
		local function line(text, size, color, font, order)
			return new("TextLabel", {
				LayoutOrder = order,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
				AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
				FontFace = font,
				TextSize = size,
				TextColor3 = color,
				TextWrapped = true,
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
				Text = text,
				Parent = card,
			})
		end
		line(m.title, 14, THEME.Text, BOLD, 1)
		if m.body ~= "" then
			line(m.body, 12, Color3.fromRGB(200, 205, 220), MED, 2)
		end
		if m.gift then
			line("Gift: " .. giftText(m.gift) .. (m.claimed and "  (claimed)" or ""), 12, m.claimed and THEME.Muted or THEME.Green, BOLD, 3)
		end
		line(ago(m.t), 11, THEME.Muted, MED, 4)
		local button = new("TextButton", {
			LayoutOrder = 5,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32),
			BackgroundColor3 = unclaimed and Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(60, 64, 82),
			AutoButtonColor = true,
			FontFace = BOLD,
			TextSize = 13,
			TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			Text = unclaimed and "Claim" or "Delete",
			Parent = card,
		}, { corner(UDim.new(0, 8)) })
		button.Activated:Connect(function()
			call(unclaimed and "Claim" or "Delete", m.id)
		end)
	end
	updateBadge()
end

local function find(id)
	for i, m in ipairs(mails) do
		if m.id == id then
			return i, m
		end
	end
end

local MailSync = remotes:WaitForChild("MailSync", 20)
if MailSync then
	MailSync.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind, payload)
		if kind == "Full" then
			mails = payload
		elseif kind == "Add" then
			if not find(payload.id) then
				table.insert(mails, 1, payload)
			end
		elseif kind == "Update" then
			local i = find(payload.id)
			if i then
				mails[i] = payload
			end
		elseif kind == "Remove" then
			local i = find(payload)
			if i then
				table.remove(mails, i)
			end
		end
		render()
	end)
end

claimAll.Activated:Connect(function()
	call("ClaimAll")
end)

mailsButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	local show = not mailsPanel.open
	mailsPanel.set(show)
	if show then
		for _, m in ipairs(mails) do
			if not m.gift and not m.read then
				m.read = true
				task.spawn(call, "Read", m.id)
			end
		end
		updateBadge()
	end
end)

-- KickArenaJoris (Script in ServerScriptService of the MAIN place): End Of Fear ritual and the "Meet the owner" badge
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local BadgeService = game:GetService("BadgeService")
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local OWNER_ID = 8469510776
local MEET_BADGE = 2813350243153463
local MEET_DISTANCE = 45
local MIRROR_PLACE_ID = 129316061020062 -- the mirror place also hosts End Of Fear
local TYCOON_RADIUS = 18
local CENTER_RADIUS = 8
local DEBUG = true -- prints why the ritual did not start (server log only)

local Core
repeat
	Core = _G.KickArenaCore
	if not Core then
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until Core
local Ext
repeat
	Ext = _G.KickArenaExt
	if not (Ext and Ext.ready and Ext.tycoonOf) then
		Ext = nil
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until Ext

local function hrpOf(player)
	local _, _, hrp = Core.getAlive(player)
	return hrp
end

local function flatDist(a, b)
	return Vector3.new(a.X - b.X, 0, a.Z - b.Z).Magnitude
end

-- MEET THE OWNER
local met = {}
task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(2)
		local owner = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(OWNER_ID)
		local oroot = owner and hrpOf(owner)
		if oroot then
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if p ~= owner and not met[p.UserId] then
					local root = hrpOf(p)
					if root and (root.Position - oroot.Position).Magnitude <= MEET_DISTANCE then
						met[p.UserId] = true
						task.spawn(function()
							local has = false
							pcall(function()
								has = BadgeService:UserHasBadgeAsync(p.UserId, MEET_BADGE)
							end)
							if not has then
								for _ = 1, 4 do
									if pcall(function()
										BadgeService:AwardBadge(p.UserId, MEET_BADGE)
									end) then
										break
									end
									task.wait(2)
								end
								Core.notify(p, "You met the owner! Badge earned", true)
							end
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
	met[p.UserId] = nil
end)

-- END OF FEAR RITUAL
local busy = {}

local function maxed(st)
	for _, up in ipairs(Ext.UPGRADES) do
		if not st.bought[up.id] then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

local function inTycoon(pos, center)
	return flatDist(pos, center) <= TYCOON_RADIUS and math.abs(pos.Y - center.Y) <= 14
end

local lastLog = {}
local function why(owner, text)
	if DEBUG and Workspace:GetAttribute("KickTimeStop") == true and os.clock() - (lastLog[owner] or 0) > 3 then
		lastLog[owner] = os.clock()
		print("[KickArenaJoris] ritual blocked for " .. owner.Name .. ": " .. text)
	end
end

local function check(owner)
	local st = Ext.tycoonOf(owner)
	if not st or st.dead then
		return nil
	end
	if not maxed(st) then
		why(owner, "tycoon is not fully upgraded")
		return nil
	end
	local center = st.cf.Position
	local ohrp = hrpOf(owner)
	if not ohrp or flatDist(ohrp.Position, center) > CENTER_RADIUS or math.abs(ohrp.Position.Y - center.Y) > 14 then
		why(owner, "owner is not standing in the middle of the tycoon")
		return nil
	end
	local god, smile
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local root = p ~= owner and hrpOf(p)
		if root and p:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") == true and inTycoon(root.Position, center) then
			local kick = p:GetAttribute("EquippedKick")
			if kick == "God" and not god then
				god = p
			elseif kick == "Smile" and not smile then
				smile = p
			end
		end
	end
	local dummies = 0
	local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Dummies")
	if folder then
		for _, m in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
			local r = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if r and inTycoon(r.Position, center) then
				dummies += 1
			end
		end
	end
	if not god or Workspace:GetAttribute("KickTimeStop") ~= true or Ext.timeStopper() ~= god then
		why(owner, "no God Hand player with an active time stop inside the tycoon")
		return nil
	end
	local needed = smile and 0 or 1
	if dummies < needed then
		why(owner, "no Smile player or dummy inside the tycoon")
		return nil
	end
	return { owner = owner, god = god, smile = smile, center = center, dummies = needed }
end

local function openPortal(group)
	local owner = group.owner
	busy[owner] = true
	local members, allowed = {}, {}
	for _, p in ipairs({ owner, group.god, group.smile }) do
		if p then
			table.insert(members, p)
			allowed[p] = true
		end
	end
	local godRoot = group.god and select(3, Core.getAlive(group.god))
	local okCode, code = pcall(function()
		return TeleportService:ReserveServer(MIRROR_PLACE_ID)
	end)
	if not group.public and not godRoot then
		task.delay(10, function()
			busy[owner] = nil
		end)
		return
	end
	if not okCode then
		warn("[KickArenaJoris] ReserveServer failed, using a normal server: " .. tostring(code))
		code = nil
	end
	local flatDir, ground
	if group.public then
		flatDir, ground = group.dir, group.pos
	else
		flatDir = Vector3.new(group.center.X - godRoot.Position.X, 0, group.center.Z - godRoot.Position.Z)
		flatDir = flatDir.Magnitude > 0.5 and flatDir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
		ground = Vector3.new(godRoot.Position.X, group.center.Y, godRoot.Position.Z)
	end

	local cf = CFrame.lookAt(origin, origin + flatDir)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "EndOfFearPortal"
	model.Parent = Workspace
	local function part(name, size, pcf, color, material, extra)
		local p = Instance.new("Part")
		p.Name = name
		p.Anchored = true
		p.CanCollide = false
		p.CanQuery = false
		p.Size = size
		p.CFrame = pcf
		p.Color = color
		p.Material = material
		for k, v in pairs(extra or {}) do
			p[k] = v
		end
		p.Parent = model
		return p
	end

		Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, CanTouch = true, Transparency = 0.08,
	})
	local inner = part("PortalCore", Vector3.new(0.7, 9, 9), cf * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0), Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235), Enum.Material.Neon, {
		Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Transparency = 0.7,
	})
	local segs = {}
	for i = 1, 40 do
		segs[i] = part("Ring", Vector3.new(1, 2.2, 1), cf, i % 2 == 0 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 80), Enum.Material.Neon)
	end
