	sparks.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
	sparks.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.8, 1.6)
	sparks.Speed = NumberRange.new(2, 7)
	sparks.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
	sparks.Rate = 60
	sparks.LightEmission = 1
	sparks.Parent = att
	local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
	light.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 200)
	light.Range = 40
	light.Brightness = 3
	light.Parent = disc

	gui.Parent = disc

		if not p then
			return
		end
		if not allowed[p] and not group.public then
			if os.clock() - (warnedAt[p] or 0) > 4 then
				warnedAt[p] = os.clock()
				Core.notify(p, "Only the chosen can enter this portal", false)
			end
			return
		end

			return
		end
		entered[p] = true
		Ext.FX:FireAllClients("RitualGo", hit.Position)
		local options = Instance.new("TeleportOptions")
		if code then
			options.ReservedServerAccessCode = code
		end

		local sent, err = pcall(function()
			TeleportService:TeleportAsync(MIRROR_PLACE_ID, { p }, options)
		end)
		if not sent then
			entered[p] = nil
			warn("[KickArenaJoris] Teleport failed: " .. tostring(err))
			Core.notify(p, "The dimension is closed right now. Try again", false)
		end
	end)

	while os.clock() - t0 < (group.public and 90 or 26) and model.Parent do
		local t = os.clock() - t0
		local pulse = 1 + math.sin(t * 5) * 0.04
		for i, seg in ipairs(segs) do
			local a = i / #segs * math.pi * 2 + t * 0.9
			seg.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(math.cos(a) * 7.3 * pulse, math.sin(a) * 7.3 * pulse, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, a + math.rad(90))
		end
		inner.Transparency = 0.6 + math.sin(t * 3) * 0.15
		task.wait(0.05)
	end

	task.delay(20, function()
		busy[owner] = nil
	end)

task.spawn(function()

							busy[group.owner] = nil
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

_G.KickArenaJoris = {
	adminPortal = function(player)
		local _, _, hrp = Core.getAlive(player)
		if not hrp then
			return false, "You must be alive"
		end
		if busy[player] then
			return false, "A portal is already open"
		end
		local look = Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
		look = look.Magnitude > 0.1 and look.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
		busy[player] = true
		local st, err = Ext.demoTycoon(player)
		if not st then
			busy[player] = nil
			return false, err or "Could not build the tycoon here"
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			local center = st.cf.Position
			-- the Smile in the middle
			local ball = Instance.new("Part")
			ball.Name = "RitualSmile"
			ball.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
			ball.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
			ball.Anchored = true
			ball.CanCollide = false

			bg.Parent = ball

			face.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			face.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
			face.TextSize = 30
			face.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 30, 0)
			face.Text = ":)"
			face.Parent = bg
			Ext.FX:FireAllClients("Build", center)
			task.wait(2)
			Ext.FX:FireAllClients("TimeStop", true, center)
			task.wait(3)
			Ext.FX:FireAllClients("TimeStop", false)
			local ground = Vector3.new(center.X, center.Y, center.Z)
			openPortal({ owner = player, public = true, pos = ground, dir = -look })
			ball:Destroy()
			Ext.removeTycoon(player, true)
		end)
		return true, "Ritual started. The portal opens in a moment for 90 sec"
	end,

local function mk(parent, name, size, cf, color, material, extra)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	p.Name = name
	p.Anchored = true
	p.CanCollide = false
	p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.Size = size
	p.CFrame = cf
	p.Color = color
	p.Material = material or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	for k, v in pairs(extra or {}) do
		p[k] = v
	end
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

local function flat(v)
	return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
end

local function charOf(player)
	local c = player.Character
	local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local r = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if h and r and h.Health > 0 then
		return c, h, r
	end
end

local function hrpOf(p)
	local _, _, r = charOf(p)
	return r
end

local function frozen()
	return os.clock() < S.frozenUntil
end

local function bwait(t)
	local elapsed = 0
	while elapsed < t do
		local dt = task.wait(0.05)
		if not frozen() then
			elapsed += dt
		end
	end
end

local function alive()
	local list = {}
	for p, st in pairs(S.players) do
		if p.Parent and not st.eliminated then
			table.insert(list, p)
		end
	end
	return list
end

-- BOSS BODY
local BLACK = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12)
local WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 238)
local function bp(name, size, off, color, material, pivot)
	local p = mk(folder, name, size, B.cf * off, color, material or Enum.Material.Slate, { CanQuery = true })
	table.insert(B.parts, { part = p, off = off, pivot = pivot })
	return p
end

local function place()
	for _, e in ipairs(B.parts) do
		if e.pivot then
			local P = CFrame.new(e.pivot)
			e.part.CFrame = B.cf * P * CFrame.Angles(B.arm, 0, 0) * P:Inverse() * e.off
		else
			e.part.CFrame = B.cf * e.off
		end
	end
end

local function setEyes(angry)
	local color = angry and Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
	for _, e in ipairs(B.parts) do
		if e.part.Name == "Eye" then
			e.part.Color = color
		end
	end
end

local function buildBoss()
	B.model = Instance.new("Model")
	B.model.Name = "TheFear"
	B.model.Parent = Workspace
	local keep = folder
	folder = B.model
	for _, x in ipairs({ -3.2, 3.2 }) do
		bp("Leg", Vector3.new(4.2, 12, 4.2), CFrame.new(x, 6, 0), BLACK)
		bp("Foot", Vector3.new(5, 1.4, 7), CFrame.new(x, 0.7, -1), WHITE, Enum.Material.Marble)
	end
	bp("Torso", Vector3.new(13, 13, 7), CFrame.new(0, 18.5, 0), BLACK)
	for i = 0, 3 do
		bp("Rib", Vector3.new(10.5 - i * 0.8, 0.8, 0.5), CFrame.new(0, 13.8 + i * 3, -3.8), WHITE, Enum.Material.Marble)
	end
	bp("Head", Vector3.new(8, 8.4, 8), CFrame.new(0, 29, 0), WHITE, Enum.Material.Marble)
	bp("Horn", Vector3.new(1.2, 5, 1.2), CFrame.new(-3, 34.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(14)), BLACK)
	bp("Horn", Vector3.new(1.2, 5, 1.2), CFrame.new(3, 34.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(-14)), BLACK)
	for _, x in ipairs({ -1.9, 1.9 }) do
		bp("Eye", Vector3.new(1.8, 1.4, 0.6), CFrame.new(x, 29.6, -4.1), WHITE, Enum.Material.Neon)
		bp("Tear", Vector3.new(0.5, 3.6, 0.4), CFrame.new(x, 27, -4.1), BLACK)
	end
	bp("Mouth", Vector3.new(4.4, 0.7, 0.5), CFrame.new(0, 25.8, -4.1), BLACK)
	for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		local sp = Vector3.new(side * 8.6, 25, 0)
		bp("Arm", Vector3.new(3.6, 22, 3.6), CFrame.new(side * 8.6, 14, 0), BLACK, nil, sp)
		bp("Hand", Vector3.new(5.4, 5.4, 5.4), CFrame.new(side * 8.6, 3, 0), WHITE, Enum.Material.Marble, sp)
	end
	folder = keep
	setEyes(false)
	place()
end

local function anim(dur, f)
	local t0 = os.clock()
	local lastT = t0
	while true do
		local nowT = os.clock()
		if frozen() then
			t0 += nowT - lastT
		end
		lastT = nowT
		local a = math.min((nowT - t0) / dur, 1)
		f(a)
		if a >= 1 then
			break
		end
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
end

local function bossPos()
	return B.cf.Position
end

-- PLAYERS
local function eliminate(player, reason)
	local st = S.players[player]
	if not st or st.eliminated then
		return
	end
	st.eliminated = true
	st.lives = 0
	player:SetAttribute("Lives", 0)
	player:SetAttribute("Eliminated", true)
	player:SetAttribute("CanLosing", false)
	fx("Eliminated", player.DisplayName, reason or "")
	if REVIVE_PRODUCT_ID ~= 0 and not S.finished and not st.offered then
		st.offered = true
		st.offerUntil = os.clock() + 25
		FXRemote:FireClient(player, "ReviveOffer", REVIVE_PRODUCT_ID)
	end
	local c, _, hrp = charOf(player)
	if c and hrp then
		c:PivotTo(CFrame.new(math.random(-12, 12), 154, math.random(-12, 12)))
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end
end

local function hurt(player, from, power)
	local st = S.players[player]
	if not st or st.eliminated or player:GetAttribute("JorisForm") == true then
		return
	end
	if os.clock() < (st.inv or 0) then
		return
	end
	local c0, _, hrp = charOf(player)
	if not hrp then
		return
	end
	st.inv = os.clock() + 1.8
	local hum = c0 and c0:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
		hum:TakeDamage(34)
	end
	local dir = flat(hrp.Position - from)
	dir = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
	FXRemote:FireClient(player, "Push", dir * (power or 70) + Vector3.new(0, 38, 0))
	FXRemote:FireClient(player, "Hurt", st.lives)
end

-- a life is only lost when the character actually dies
local function watchDeath(player, char)
	local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
	if not hum then
		return
	end
	hum.Died:Connect(function()
		local st = S.players[player]
		if not st or st.eliminated or S.finished or player:GetAttribute("JorisForm") == true then
			return
		end
		st.lives -= 1
		player:SetAttribute("Lives", st.lives)
		FXRemote:FireClient(player, "Hurt", st.lives)
		if st.lives <= 0 then
			eliminate(player, "fell to the fear")
		end
	end)
end

local leaving = {}
LeaveRemote.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player)
	if leaving[player] or not S.back and MAIN_PLACE_ID == 0 then
		return
	end
	leaving[player] = true
	local ok = pcall(function()
		TeleportService:TeleportAsync(S.back or MAIN_PLACE_ID, { player })
	end)
	if not ok then
		leaving[player] = nil
	end
end)

local function leaveAll(delay)
	fx("Return", delay)
	task.delay(delay, function()
		local list = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if not leaving[p] then
				table.insert(list, p)
			end
		end
		local back = S.back or MAIN_PLACE_ID
		if #list > 0 and back ~= 0 then
			pcall(function()
				TeleportService:TeleportAsync(back, list)
			end)
		end
	end)
end

local function defeat()
	if S.finished then
		return
	end
	S.finished = true
	setPhase("defeat")
	banner("The fear remains")
	fx("Defeat")
	leaveAll(7)
end

local function checkAllDead()
	if not S.finished and #alive() == 0 then
		for _, st in pairs(S.players) do
			if st.offerUntil and os.clock() < st.offerUntil then
				return
			end
		end
		defeat()
	end
end

-- ATTACKS
local function groundHit(p)
	return p.Y < 5.8
end

local function blockedByCrystal(from, to)
	local hit = Workspace:Raycast(from, to - from, crystalParams)
	return hit ~= nil
end

local function wave(speed, color)
	local center = flat(bossPos())
	local segs = {}
	for i = 1, 44 do
		segs[i] = mk(folder, "Wave", Vector3.new(4, 3, 1.4), CFrame.new(center), color, Enum.Material.Neon, { CanQuery = false })
	end
	local r = 4
	local t0 = os.clock()
	local last = t0
	while r < R and not S.finished do
		local now = os.clock()
		local isFrozen = frozen()
		if not isFrozen then
			r += speed * (now - last)
		end
		last = now
		for i, s in ipairs(segs) do
			local a = i / #segs * math.pi * 2
			s.Size = Vector3.new(2 * math.pi * r / #segs + 1, 3, 1.4)
			s.CFrame = CFrame.new(center + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * r, 1.6, math.sin(a) * r)) * CFrame.Angles(0, -a + math.rad(90), 0)
		end
		for _, p in ipairs(alive()) do
			local _, _, hrp = charOf(p)
			if hrp and not isFrozen then
				local d = flat(hrp.Position - center).Magnitude
				if math.abs(d - r) < 2.8 and groundHit(hrp.Position) and not blockedByCrystal(center + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, 2, hrp.Position.Z)) then
					hurt(p, center, 75)
				end
			end
		end
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
	for _, s in ipairs(segs) do
		s:Destroy()
	end
end

local function slam(rage)
	local base = B.cf
	fx("SlamWarn", bossPos())
	anim(0.9, function(a)
		B.arm = math.rad(170) * a
		B.cf = base * CFrame.new(0, 2 * a, 0)
		place()
	end)
	bwait(0.2)
	anim(0.14, function(a)
		B.arm = math.rad(170 - 160 * a)
		B.cf = base * CFrame.new(0, 2 - 3 * a, 0)
		place()
	end)
	fx("Slam", bossPos(), rage)
	local color = rage and Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 40) or Color3.fromRGB(250, 250, 250)
	task.spawn(wave, rage and 52 or 38, color)
	if rage then
		task.delay(0.7, function()
			if not S.finished then
				wave(52, color)
			end
		end)
	end
	anim(0.5, function(a)
		B.arm = math.rad(10) * (1 - a)
		B.cf = base * CFrame.new(0, -1 + a, 0)
		place()
	end)
	B.cf = base
	B.arm = 0
	place()
end

local function beams(rage)
	local list = {}
	local targets = alive()
	local count = rage and 5 or 3
	for i = 1, count do
		local dir
		local t = targets[((i - 1) % math.max(#targets, 1)) + 1]
		local hrp = t and hrpOf(t)
		if hrp and i <= #targets then
			dir = flat(hrp.Position - bossPos())
		end
		if not dir or dir.Magnitude < 1 then
			local a = math.random() * math.pi * 2
			dir = Vector3.new(math.cos(a), 0, math.sin(a))
		end
		dir = dir.Unit
		local origin = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
		local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, dir * R, crystalParams)
		local len = hit and (hit.Position - origin).Magnitude or R
		local mid = origin + dir * (len / 2)
		local warn = mk(folder, "BeamWarn", Vector3.new(1, 0.2, len), CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(mid.X, 0.3, mid.Z), Vector3.new(mid.X, 0.3, mid.Z) + dir), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Enum.Material.Neon, { Transparency = 0.5, CanQuery = false })
		table.insert(list, { dir = dir, len = len, mid = mid, warn = warn })
	end
	fx("BeamWarn")
	bwait(1.1)
	if S.finished then
		for _, b in ipairs(list) do
			b.warn:Destroy()
		end
		return
	end
	fx("Beam")
	local color = rage and Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 20) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	for _, b in ipairs(list) do
		b.warn.Size = Vector3.new(4.5, 4, b.len)
		b.warn.Transparency = 0.05
		b.warn.Color = color
		b.warn.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(b.mid.X, 2, b.mid.Z), Vector3.new(b.mid.X, 2, b.mid.Z) + b.dir)
	end
	local t0 = os.clock()
	local lastT = t0
	while os.clock() - t0 < 0.7 do
		local nowT = os.clock()
		local isFrozen = frozen()
		if isFrozen then
			t0 += nowT - lastT
		end
		lastT = nowT
		for _, p in ipairs(isFrozen and {} or alive()) do
			local _, _, hrp = charOf(p)
			if hrp and hrp.Position.Y < 7.5 then
				for _, b in ipairs(list) do
					local rel = flat(hrp.Position)
					local along = rel:Dot(b.dir)
					local perp = (rel - b.dir * along).Magnitude
					if along > -1 and along < b.len + 1 and perp < 3.2 then
						hurt(p, hrp.Position - (rel - b.dir * along), 65)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
	for _, b in ipairs(list) do
		b.warn:Destroy()
	end
end

local function sweep(rage)
	local targets = alive()
	local t = targets[math.random(math.max(#targets, 1))]
	local hrp = t and hrpOf(t)
	local dir = hrp and flat(hrp.Position - bossPos()) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
	if dir.Magnitude < 1 then
		dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
	end
	local ang0 = math.atan2(dir.Z, dir.X)
	local half = math.rad(55)
	local len = 34
	local function barCF(a)
		local d = Vector3.new(math.cos(a), 0, math.sin(a))
		return CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(d.X * len / 2, 2.2, d.Z * len / 2), Vector3.new(d.X * len / 2, 2.2, d.Z * len / 2) + d), d
	end
	local warn = mk(folder, "SweepWarn", Vector3.new(1.2, 0.3, len), barCF(ang0 - half), Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Enum.Material.Neon, { Transparency = 0.45, CanQuery = false })
	local warn2 = mk(folder, "SweepWarn", Vector3.new(1.2, 0.3, len), barCF(ang0 + half), Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Enum.Material.Neon, { Transparency = 0.45, CanQuery = false })
	fx("SlamWarn", bossPos())
	bwait(rage and 0.9 or 1.2)
	warn2:Destroy()
	local bar = mk(folder, "Sweep", Vector3.new(4, 4, len), barCF(ang0 - half), rage and Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 20) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Enum.Material.Neon, { CanQuery = false })
	fx("Beam")
	local t0 = os.clock()
	local lastT = t0
	local dur = 0.5
	while os.clock() - t0 < dur and not S.finished do
		local nowT = os.clock()
		local isFrozen = frozen()
		if isFrozen then
			t0 += nowT - lastT
		end
		lastT = nowT
		local a = ang0 - half + (2 * half) * ((nowT - t0) / dur)
		local cf, d = barCF(a)
		bar.CFrame = cf
		warn.CFrame = cf
		for _, p in ipairs(isFrozen and {} or alive()) do
			local h = hrpOf(p)
			if h and h.Position.Y < 7.5 then
				local rel = flat(h.Position)
				local along = rel:Dot(d)
				local perp = (rel - d * along).Magnitude
				if along > 0 and along < len and perp < 3.6 then
					hurt(p, h.Position - (rel - d * along), 85)
				end
			end
		end
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
	bar:Destroy()
	warn:Destroy()
end

-- SHADOW NPCS
local function killShadow(m)
	local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if h and h.Health > 0 then
		h.Health = 0
		fx("BossHit", m:GetPivot().Position)
		task.delay(1, function()
			m:Destroy()
		end)
	end
end

local function spawnShadows(n)
	for i = 1, n do
		if #npcFolder:GetChildren() >= 7 then
			return
		end
		local ok, m = pcall(function()
			return Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(Instance.new("HumanoidDescription"), Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6)
		end)
		if ok and m then
			m.Name = "Shadow"
			for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("BasePart") then
					d.Color = Color3.fromRGB(4, 4, 4)
					d.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
				elseif d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Accessory") or d:IsA("Shirt") or d:IsA("Pants") then
					d:Destroy()
				end
			end
			local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			h.MaxHealth = 1
			h.Health = 1
			h.WalkSpeed = 15
			h.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
			local a = math.random() * math.pi * 2
			m:PivotTo(CFrame.new(math.cos(a) * 60, 4, math.sin(a) * 60))
			m.Parent = npcFolder
			fx("ShadowSpawn", m:GetPivot().Position)
			task.spawn(function()
				local cd = 0
				while m.Parent and h.Health > 0 and not S.finished do
					local best, bd = nil, 1e9
					local root = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					for _, p in ipairs(alive()) do
						local _, _, hrp = charOf(p)
						if hrp and root then
							local d = (hrp.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
							if d < bd then
								best, bd = p, d
							end
						end
					end
					if frozen() then
						if root then
							h:MoveTo(root.Position)
						end
					elseif best then
						local _, _, hrp = charOf(best)
						if hrp then
							h:MoveTo(hrp.Position)
							if bd < 5 and os.clock() > cd then
								cd = os.clock() + 1.6
								hurt(best, root.Position, 60)
							end
						end
					end
					task.wait(0.2)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end

-- JORIS KICK
local function saveJoris(player)
	for _ = 1, 4 do
		local ok = pcall(function()
			questStore:UpdateAsync("u_" .. player.UserId, function(old)
				old = type(old) == "table" and old or { Unlocked = {}, HitBy = {}, Hits = 0, Kills = 0 }
				old.Unlocked = old.Unlocked or {}
				old.Unlocked.Joris = true
				return old
			end)
		end)
		if ok then
			return
		end
		task.wait(2)
	end
end

local function awardBadge(player)
	for _ = 1, 5 do
		local ok = pcall(function()
			BadgeService:AwardBadge(player.UserId, BADGE_ID)
		end)
		if ok then
			return
		end
		task.wait(2)
	end
end

local function bossDie()
	if S.finished then
		return
	end
	S.finished = true
	setPhase("victory")
	Workspace:SetAttribute("BossFallen", false)
	fx("BossDeath", bossPos())
	banner("The fear is gone")
	for _, m in ipairs(npcFolder:GetChildren()) do
		killShadow(m)
	end
	for _, e in ipairs(B.parts) do
		e.part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
		e.part.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(e.part, TweenInfo.new(2.2), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
	end
	if S.winner and S.winner.Parent then
		task.spawn(awardBadge, S.winner)
		FXRemote:FireClient(S.winner, "Banner", "Badge earned: Joris")
	end
	leaveAll(9)
end

local function formEnd(player, saved)
	player:SetAttribute("JorisForm", false)
	local c = player.Character
	if c then
		for d, v in pairs(saved.store) do
			if d.Parent then
				d.Color = v[1]
				d.Material = v[2]
			end
		end
	end
	for _, o in ipairs(saved.items) do
		o:Destroy()
	end
end

local abilityCd = {}
AbilityRemote.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player)
	if not active or S.finished or player ~= S.winner or player:GetAttribute("CanLosing") ~= true then
		return
	end
	if player:GetAttribute("JorisForm") == true or os.clock() < (abilityCd[player] or 0) then
		return
	end
	local c, _, hrp = charOf(player)
	if not c then
		return
	end
	abilityCd[player] = os.clock() + FORM_TIME + 10
	player:SetAttribute("JorisForm", true)
	local saved = { store = {}, items = {} }
	for _, d in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
			saved.store[d] = { d.Color, d.Material }
			d.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 90)
			d.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
		end
	end
	local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
	light.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120)
	light.Range = 24
	light.Brightness = 3
	light.Parent = hrp
	table.insert(saved.items, light)
	local orbs = {}
	for i = 1, 6 do
		local o = mk(folder, "Orb", Vector3.new(2.4, 2.4, 2.4), hrp.CFrame, Color3.fromRGB(255, 226, 130), Enum.Material.Neon, { Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball, CanQuery = false })
		orbs[i] = o
		table.insert(saved.items, o)
	end
	fx("FormOn", hrp.Position, player.UserId)
	task.spawn(function()
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while os.clock() - t0 < FORM_TIME and not S.finished and c.Parent and hrp.Parent do
			local t = os.clock() - t0
			for i, o in ipairs(orbs) do
				local a = t * 3 + i * math.pi * 2 / 6
				o.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * 6.5, math.sin(t * 2 + i) * 1.3, math.sin(a) * 6.5))
			end
			for _, m in ipairs(npcFolder:GetChildren()) do
				local r = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if r then
					for _, o in ipairs(orbs) do
						if (r.Position - o.Position).Magnitude < 4 then
							killShadow(m)
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
			for _, e in ipairs(B.parts) do
				if (e.part.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude < e.part.Size.Magnitude / 2 + 7 then
					bossDie()
					break
				end
			end
			task.wait(0.05)
		end
		formEnd(player, saved)
	end)
end)

local TS_BASE = 6
StopRemote.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player)
	local st = S.players[player]
	if not active or S.finished or not st or st.eliminated or st.role ~= "God" then
		return
	end
	if Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() < (player:GetAttribute("TSReadyAt") or 0) or frozen() then
		return
	end
	local dur = TS_BASE + ((S.tsBonus or Workspace:GetAttribute("DimTS") == true) and 6 or 0)
	S.frozenUntil = os.clock() + dur
	player:SetAttribute("TSReadyAt", Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + dur + 30)
	Workspace:SetAttribute("BossTimeStop", Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + dur)
	fx("TimeStop", dur, bossPos())
end)

-- BOSS FLOW
local function faint(dur)
	local base = B.cf
	fx("FaintStart")
	anim(1.1, function(a)
		B.arm = math.rad(20) * a
		B.cf = base * CFrame.new(0, 4.6 * a, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90) * a, 0, 0)
		place()
	end)
	setEyes(false)
	S.fallen = true
	Workspace:SetAttribute("BossFallen", true)
	fx("Faint", bossPos())
	banner("It fell. Strike now")
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while os.clock() - t0 < dur and S.hp > 20 and not S.finished and #alive() > 0 do
		bwait(0.1)
	end
	S.fallen = false
	Workspace:SetAttribute("BossFallen", false)
	if S.finished then
		return
	end
	anim(1.1, function(a)
		B.arm = math.rad(20) * (1 - a)
		B.cf = base * CFrame.new(0, 4.6 * (1 - a), 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90) * (1 - a), 0, 0)
		place()
	end)
	B.cf = base
	B.arm = 0
	place()
	setEyes(S.rage)
end

local function lastStand()
	setPhase("last")
	setEyes(true)
	fx("Rage")
	local n = #alive()
	if n > 1 then
		banner("Only one can take the Kick. Defeat your friends")
		fx("LastStand")
		local nextAtk = os.clock() + 4
		while #alive() > 1 and not S.finished do
			if os.clock() > nextAtk then
				if math.random() < 0.5 then
					slam(true)
				else
					beams(true)
				end
				nextAtk = os.clock() + 5
			end
			task.wait(0.3)
		end
	end
	local winner = alive()[1]
	if not winner then
		return defeat()
	end
	S.winner = winner
	winner:SetAttribute("CanLosing", true)
	fx("JorisGranted", winner.UserId, winner.DisplayName)
	task.spawn(saveJoris, winner)
	setPhase("final")
	banner("Use LOSING and touch the fear")
	local nextNpc = os.clock() + 5
	while not S.finished do
		if #alive() == 0 then
			return defeat()
		end
		if os.clock() > nextNpc then
			spawnShadows(2)
			nextNpc = os.clock() + 14
		end
		local roll = math.random()
		if roll < 0.4 then
			slam(true)
		elseif roll < 0.7 then
			sweep(true)
		else
			beams(true)
		end
		task.wait(1)
	end
end

local function runBoss()
	setPhase("fight")
	while not S.finished do
		if S.hp <= 20 then
			break
		end
		local rage = S.hp <= 85
		if rage and not S.rage then
			S.rage = true
			setEyes(true)
			fx("Rage")
			banner("It is furious")
			bwait(1.5)
		end
		if S.hp <= 50 and not S.shadows then
			S.shadows = true
			banner("Shadows awaken")
		end
		if S.shadows then
			spawnShadows(3)
		end
		local plan = rage and { "slam", "sweep", "beams", "sweep", "slam" } or { "slam", "sweep" }
		for _, a in ipairs(plan) do
			if S.finished or S.hp <= 20 or #alive() == 0 then
				break
			end
			if a == "slam" then
				slam(rage)
			elseif a == "sweep" then
				sweep(rage)
			else
				beams(rage)
			end
			bwait(rage and 0.8 or 1.4)
		end
		if S.finished or #alive() == 0 then
			break
		end
		faint((rage and 9 or 11) + ((S.faintBonus or Workspace:GetAttribute("DimBF") == true) and 6 or 0))
	end
	if S.finished then
		return
	end
	if #alive() == 0 then
		return defeat()
	end
	lastStand()
end

local function bodyNear(origin, reach)
	for _, p in ipairs(Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(origin, 6.5 + (reach or 0))) do
		if p:IsDescendantOf(B.model) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local lastKick = {}
KickRemote.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player)
	local st = S.players[player]
	if not active or S.finished or not st or st.eliminated then
		return
	end
	local t = os.clock()
	if lastKick[player] and t - lastKick[player] < KICK_COOLDOWN then
		return
	end
	lastKick[player] = t
	local c, _, hrp = charOf(player)
	if not c or player:GetAttribute("JorisForm") == true then
		return
	end
	local reach = player:GetAttribute("DimReach") or 0
	local origin = hrp.Position + hrp.CFrame.LookVector * (3.4 + reach * 0.4)
	if Workspace:GetAttribute("BossPhase") == "last" then
		for other, ost in pairs(S.players) do
			if other ~= player and not ost.eliminated then
				local _, _, oh = charOf(other)
				if oh and (oh.Position - origin).Magnitude < 6.5 + reach then
					FXRemote:FireClient(other, "Push", flat(oh.Position - hrp.Position).Unit * 90 + Vector3.new(0, 30, 0))
					fx("BossHit", oh.Position)
					eliminate(other, "kicked by " .. player.DisplayName)
					return
				end
			end
		end
	end
	for _, m in ipairs(npcFolder:GetChildren()) do
		local r = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if r and (r.Position - origin).Magnitude < 7 + reach then
			killShadow(m)
			return
		end
	end
	if bodyNear(origin, reach) then
		if S.fallen and S.hp > 20 then
			S.hp -= 1
			Workspace:SetAttribute("BossHP", S.hp)
			fx("BossHit", origin)
		else
			fx("Block", origin)
		end
	end
end)

-- SETUP
local function placePlayer(player, index)
	local c = player.Character
	local st = S.players[player]
	if not c or not st then
		return
	end
	local hrp = c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
	local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not hrp then
		return
	end
	if st.eliminated then
		c:PivotTo(CFrame.new(math.random(-12, 12), 154, math.random(-12, 12)))
	else
		c:PivotTo(spawnCF(st.index, math.max(S.expected, st.index)))
	end
	if hum then
		hum.WalkSpeed = player:GetAttribute("DimSpeed") == true and 28 or 16
	end
end

ReviveRemote.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, kind)
	local st = S.players[player]
	if not st then
		return
	end
	if kind == "decline" then
		st.offerUntil = 0
	elseif kind == "pending" and st.eliminated then
		st.offerUntil = os.clock() + 60
	end
end)

local function revive(player)
	local st = S.players[player]
	if not st or not st.eliminated or S.finished then
		return false
	end
	st.eliminated = false
	st.offered = false
	st.offerUntil = nil
	st.lives = LIVES
	st.inv = os.clock() + 3
	player:SetAttribute("Lives", LIVES)
	player:SetAttribute("Eliminated", false)
	task.spawn(placePlayer, player)
	fx("Revived", player.DisplayName)
	return true
end

MarketplaceService.ProcessReceipt = function(info)
	local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(info.PlayerId)
	if not player then
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end
	if info.ProductId == REVIVE_PRODUCT_ID and REVIVE_PRODUCT_ID ~= 0 and revive(player) then
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
	end
	return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
end

local function activate()
	if active then
		return
	end
	active = true
	Workspace:SetAttribute("EndOfFear", true)
	local arena = Workspace:FindFirstChild("TrialArena")
	if arena then
		arena:Destroy()
	end
	local guards = Workspace:FindFirstChild("TrialGuards")
	if guards then
		guards:Destroy()
	end
	buildMap()
	buildBoss()
	Workspace:SetAttribute("BossHP", BOSS_HP)
	Workspace:SetAttribute("BossFallen", false)
	setPhase("intro")
	task.spawn(function()
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while #Players:GetPlayers() < S.expected and os.clock() - t0 < 25 do
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
		task.wait(3)
		for p, st in pairs(S.players) do
			if p.Parent then
				placePlayer(p)
			end
		end
		banner("END OF FEAR")
		fx("Intro")
		B.cf = CFrame.new(0, -36, 0)
		place()
		anim(4, function(a)
			B.cf = CFrame.new(0, -36 * (1 - a), 0)
			place()
		end)
		task.wait(1.5)
		if #Players:GetPlayers() == 0 then
			return
		end
		runBoss()
	end)
end

local order = 0
local function onPlayer(player)
	local join = player:GetJoinData()
	local data = join and join.TeleportData
	if type(data) ~= "table" or data.quest ~= "Joris" then
		return
	end
	order += 1
	S.back = S.back or data.back
	S.expected = math.max(S.expected, tonumber(data.count) or 1)
	S.tsBonus = S.tsBonus or data.ts == true
	S.faintBonus = S.faintBonus or data.bf == true
	S.players[player] = { lives = LIVES, eliminated = false, index = order, role = data.role }
	player:SetAttribute("Role", data.role or "Tycoon")
	player:SetAttribute("TSReadyAt", 0)
	player:SetAttribute("Lives", LIVES)
	player:SetAttribute("Eliminated", false)
	player:SetAttribute("CanLosing", false)
	player:SetAttribute("JorisForm", false)
	activate()
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
		task.spawn(watchDeath, player, char)
		if S.finished then
			return
		end
		task.wait(0.4)
		placePlayer(player)
	end)
	if player.Character then
		task.spawn(watchDeath, player, player.Character)
		task.spawn(placePlayer, player)
	end
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(onPlayer)
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	task.spawn(onPlayer, p)
end

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	local st = S.players[player]
	if st then
		st.eliminated = true
		S.players[player] = nil
		abilityCd[player] = nil
		lastKick[player] = nil
		if S.winner == player and not S.finished then
			S.winner = nil
		end
		task.defer(checkAllDead)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(1)
		if active and not S.finished then
			Workspace:SetAttribute("BossHP", S.hp)
			checkAllDead()
		end
	end
end)

	local i = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props) do
		i[k] = v
	end
	for _, c in ipairs(children or {}) do
		c.Parent = i
	end

local sfxGood = {}
local function armSound(s, file)
	local alts = SFX_ALT[file]
	if not alts then
		return
	end
	if sfxGood[file] then
		s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/" .. sfxGood[file]
		return
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(0.35)
		if not s.Parent then
			return
		end
		if s.IsLoaded and s.TimeLength > 0 then
			sfxGood[file] = file
			return
		end
		for _, n in ipairs(alts) do
			s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/" .. n
			s:Play()
			task.wait(0.35)
			if not s.Parent then
				return
			end
			if s.IsLoaded and s.TimeLength > 0 then
				sfxGood[file] = n
				return
			end
		end
	end)
end

local function snd(file, vol, pitch)
	local s = Instance.new("Sound")
	s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/" .. file
	s.Volume = vol
	s.PlaybackSpeed = pitch
	s.Parent = SoundService
	s:Play()
	armSound(s, file)
	Debris:AddItem(s, 4)
end

leaveBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
	if os.clock() < confirmUntil then
		leaveBtn.Text = "RETURNING..."
		LeaveRemote:FireServer()

		confirmUntil = os.clock() + 3
		leaveBtn.Text = "TAP AGAIN"
		task.delay(3, function()
			if os.clock() >= confirmUntil and leaveBtn.Text == "TAP AGAIN" then
				leaveBtn.Text = "LEAVE"
			end
		end)
	end
end)

local on = false
local stopBtn = new("TextButton", {
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, -272), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 92), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 215, 255),
	AutoButtonColor = false, FontFace = BOLD, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = INK, Text = "TIME STOP", Visible = false, Parent = gui,
}, {
	new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }),
	new("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Thickness = 3 }),
})
stopBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
	StopRemote:FireServer()
end)
task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(0.25)
		local show = on and player:GetAttribute("Role") == "God" and player:GetAttribute("Eliminated") ~= true
		stopBtn.Visible = show
		if show then
			local left = (player:GetAttribute("TSReadyAt") or 0) - Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
			if left > 0 then
				stopBtn.Text = "TIME STOP\n" .. math.ceil(left) .. "s"
				stopBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 100, 120)
			else
				stopBtn.Text = "TIME STOP"
				stopBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 215, 255)
			end
		end
	end
end)

local function flash(color, alpha, time)
	flashFrame.BackgroundColor3 = color
	flashFrame.BackgroundTransparency = alpha
	TweenService:Create(flashFrame, TweenInfo.new(time or 0.4), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
end

local bannerToken = 0
local function banner(text, color)
	bannerToken += 1
	local token = bannerToken
	bannerLabel.Text = text
	bannerLabel.TextColor3 = color or WHITE
	bannerLabel.TextTransparency = 0
	bannerLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
	task.delay(2.4, function()
		if token == bannerToken then
			TweenService:Create(bannerLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.8), { TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1 }):Play()
		end
	end)
end

-- camera shake
local shakeMag, shakeEnd = 0, 0
local function shake(mag, dur)
	shakeMag = math.max(shakeMag, mag)
	shakeEnd = math.max(shakeEnd, os.clock() + dur)
end
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	local char = player.Character
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not hum then
		return
	end
	local left = shakeEnd - os.clock()
	if left > 0 then
		local m = shakeMag * math.min(left, 1)
		hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * m, (math.random() - 0.5) * m, 0)
	else
		shakeMag = 0
		hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero
	end
end)

local function burst(pos, color, n, dist)
	for _ = 1, n do
		local p = new("Part", {
			Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Color = color,
			Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), Position = pos, Parent = Workspace,
		})
		local d = Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() * 0.8, math.random() - 0.5).Unit * dist * (0.5 + math.random())
		TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = pos + d, Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1) }):Play()
		Debris:AddItem(p, 0.8)
	end
end

-- atmosphere and music
local cc, bloom, drone, drone2, song
on = false
local function enable()
	if on then
		return
	end
	on = true
	gui.Enabled = true
	cc = new("ColorCorrectionEffect", { Name = "FearCC", Saturation = -0.85, Contrast = 0.08, Brightness = 0.06, Parent = Lighting })
	bloom = new("BloomEffect", { Name = "FearBloom", Intensity = 0.25, Size = 24, Threshold = 1.2, Parent = Lighting })
	drone = new("Sound", { SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/bass.mp3", Looped = true, Volume = 0.55, PlaybackSpeed = 0.4, Parent = SoundService })
	drone2 = new("Sound", { SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/bass.mp3", Looped = true, Volume = 0, PlaybackSpeed = 0.58, Parent = SoundService })
	drone:Play()
	drone2:Play()
	armSound(drone, "bass.mp3")
	armSound(drone2, "bass.mp3")
	if BOSS_SONG ~= "" then
		song = new("Sound", { SoundId = BOSS_SONG, Looped = true, Volume = 0.6, Parent = SoundService })
		song:Play()
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		local bassLine = { 0.5, 0, 0, 0.5, 0, 0.6, 0, 0.5, 0, 0.75, 0, 0.6, 0, 0.45, 0, 0.5 }
		local lead = { 1, 0, 1.2, 0, 1.5, 0, 1.2, 0, 1, 0, 1.2, 0, 1.8, 0, 1.5, 0 }
		local step = 0
		while on do
			local hp = Workspace:GetAttribute("BossHP") or 100
			local phase = Workspace:GetAttribute("BossPhase") or "intro"
			local bpm = phase == "intro" and 92 or (hp <= 20 and 150 or (hp <= 85 and 128 or 110))
			local fainted = Workspace:GetAttribute("BossFallen") == true
			step = step % 16 + 1
			if step == 1 or step == 5 or step == 9 or step == 13 or (hp <= 85 and step == 11) then
				snd("collide.wav", 0.5, 0.33)
			end
			if step == 5 or step == 13 then
				snd("snap.mp3", 0.5, 0.9)
			end
			if step % 2 == 0 and not fainted then
				snd("clickfast.wav", 0.22, 2.4)
			end
			if bassLine[step] > 0 then
				snd("bass.mp3", 0.55, bassLine[step])
			end
			if hp <= 85 and lead[step] > 0 and not fainted then
				snd("electronicpingshort.wav", 0.22, lead[step])
			end
			task.wait(60 / bpm / 2)
		end
	end)
	task.spawn(function()
		while on do
			local hp = Workspace:GetAttribute("BossHP") or 100
			snd("collide.wav", 0.4, 0.32)
			task.wait(0.16)
			snd("collide.wav", 0.28, 0.28)
			task.wait(0.45 + hp / 100 * 0.9)
		end
	end)
end

local function refresh()
	if Workspace:GetAttribute("EndOfFear") == true then
		enable()
	end
	if not on then
		return
	end
	local hp = Workspace:GetAttribute("BossHP") or 100
	local phase = Workspace:GetAttribute("BossPhase") or "intro"
	local rage = hp <= 85
	pct.Text = math.floor(hp + 0.5) .. "%"
	TweenService:Create(fill, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(hp / 100, 0, 1), 1), BackgroundColor3 = rage and GOLD or WHITE }):Play()
	if rage then
		TweenService:Create(drone2, TweenInfo.new(1), { Volume = 0.45 }):Play()
	end
	local lives = player:GetAttribute("Lives") or 3
	for i, d in ipairs(dots) do
		d.BackgroundColor3 = i <= lives and WHITE or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 44)
	end
	local out = player:GetAttribute("Eliminated") == true
	outLabel.Visible = out
	local fallen = Workspace:GetAttribute("BossFallen") == true
	abilityBtn.Visible = player:GetAttribute("CanLosing") == true and player:GetAttribute("JorisForm") ~= true and not out
	if out then
		hint.Text = "Wait for the others"
	elseif phase == "last" then
		hint.Text = "Only one can leave. Kick your friends"
	elseif phase == "final" then
		hint.Text = player:GetAttribute("CanLosing") == true and "Press LOSING, fly and touch the fear" or "Survive. The chosen one must reach the fear"
	elseif phase == "victory" then
		hint.Text = "The fear is gone"
	elseif fallen then
		hint.Text = "Tap the screen to kick the fear"
	else
		hint.Text = "Dodge the neon light. Jump the waves. Hide behind crystals"
	end
end
Workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal("EndOfFear"):Connect(refresh)
Workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal("BossHP"):Connect(refresh)
Workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal("BossPhase"):Connect(refresh)
Workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal("BossFallen"):Connect(refresh)
for _, a in ipairs({ "Lives", "Eliminated", "CanLosing", "JorisForm" }) do
	player:GetAttributeChangedSignal(a):Connect(refresh)
end
refresh()

-- spectate friends when out
local specBar = new("Frame", {
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -90), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 40), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false, Parent = gui,
})
local specPrev = new("TextButton", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 40), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 76, 96), TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = WHITE, Text = "<", Parent = specBar })
local specNext = new("TextButton", { Position = UDim2.new(1, -44, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 40), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 76, 96), TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = WHITE, Text = ">", Parent = specBar })
local specName = new("TextLabel", {
	Position = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -96, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, FontFace = BOLD, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = WHITE, Text = "", Parent = specBar,
})
local specIdx = 1
local function specList()
	local list = {}
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local h = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if p ~= player and h and h.Health > 0 and p:GetAttribute("Role") ~= nil and p:GetAttribute("Eliminated") ~= true then
			table.insert(list, p)
		end
	end
	return list
end
local function ownCam()
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	local h = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if cam and h then
		cam.CameraSubject = h
	end
end
specPrev.Activated:Connect(function()
	specIdx -= 1
end)
specNext.Activated:Connect(function()
	specIdx += 1
end)
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	local out = on and player:GetAttribute("Eliminated") == true
	specBar.Visible = out
	if not out then
		if specBar:GetAttribute("Was") then
			specBar:SetAttribute("Was", nil)
			ownCam()
		end
		return
	end
	specBar:SetAttribute("Was", true)
	local list = specList()
	if #list == 0 then
		specName.Text = "Nobody left"
		ownCam()
		return
	end
	specIdx = ((specIdx - 1) % #list) + 1
	local target = list[specIdx]
	specName.Text = target.DisplayName
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	local h = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if cam and h and cam.CameraSubject ~= h then
		cam.CameraSubject = h
	end
end)

-- input
local lastKick = 0
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
	if not on then
		return
	end
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
		AbilityRemote:FireServer()
		return
	end
	if processed or player:GetAttribute("Eliminated") == true or player:GetAttribute("JorisForm") == true then
		return
	end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F then
		if os.clock() - lastKick > 0.45 then
			lastKick = os.clock()
			KickRemote:FireServer()
		end
	end
end)
abilityBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
	AbilityRemote:FireServer()
end)

-- gold form flight
local flyParts = nil
local function stopFly()
	if flyParts then
		for _, i in ipairs(flyParts.items) do
			i:Destroy()
		end
		if flyParts.hum.Parent then
			flyParts.hum.PlatformStand = false
			flyParts.hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
		end
		flyParts = nil
	end
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	local char = player.Character
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if on and player:GetAttribute("JorisForm") == true and hum and hrp and cam then
		if not flyParts or not flyParts.lv.Parent then
			stopFly()
			local att = new("Attachment", { Parent = hrp })
			local lv = new("LinearVelocity", { Attachment0 = att, MaxForce = math.huge, VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero, RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World, Parent = hrp })
			local ao = new("AlignOrientation", { Attachment0 = att, Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment, MaxTorque = math.huge, Responsiveness = 25, Parent = hrp })
			hum.PlatformStand = true
			flyParts = { items = { att, lv, ao }, hum = hum, lv = lv, ao = ao }
		end
		local mv = hum.MoveDirection
		local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
		local vel = Vector3.zero
		if mv.Magnitude > 0.1 then
			local flatLook = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
			vel = (flatLook.Magnitude > 0.05 and mv:Dot(flatLook.Unit) > 0.2) and look * 70 or mv * 70
		elseif hum.Jump then
			vel = Vector3.new(0, 40, 0)
		end
		flyParts.lv.VectorVelocity = vel + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(os.clock() * 3) * 0.8, 0)
		local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
		if flat.Magnitude > 0.05 then
			flyParts.ao.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, flat.Unit)
		end
	elseif flyParts then
		stopFly()
	end
end)

-- server events
local H = {}
function H.Banner(text)
	banner(text)
end
function H.Intro()
	snd("bass.mp3", 1, 0.25)
	shake(0.8, 3)
end
function H.SlamWarn()
	snd("Rocket whoosh 01.wav", 0.8, 0.4)
end
function H.Slam(pos, rage)
	shake(rage and 1.5 or 1.1, 0.8)
	snd("collide.wav", 1, 0.45)
	snd("bass.mp3", 0.9, 0.45)
	burst(pos + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), rage and GOLD or WHITE, 26, 22)
	flash(rage and GOLD or WHITE, 0.82, 0.3)
end
function H.BeamWarn()
	snd("clickfast.wav", 0.7, 1.5)
end
function H.Beam()
	snd("Rocket whoosh 01.wav", 0.9, 1.8)
	snd("snap.mp3", 0.8, 1.2)
	shake(0.5, 0.5)
end
function H.BossHit(pos)
	burst(pos, WHITE, 12, 8)
	snd("snap.mp3", 0.8, 0.7)
	snd("collide.wav", 0.6, 1.1)
end
function H.Block(pos)
	snd("button.wav", 0.5, 0.4)
end
function H.FaintStart()
	snd("bass.mp3", 1, 0.3)
	shake(0.6, 1.1)
end
function H.Faint(pos)
	snd("collide.wav", 1, 0.3)
	shake(1.6, 0.9)
	burst(pos, WHITE, 30, 24)
end
function H.Rage()
	snd("bass.mp3", 1, 0.25)
	snd("swordslash.wav", 0.8, 0.4)
	shake(1.2, 1.4)
	flash(GOLD, 0.7, 0.8)
end
function H.LastStand()
	snd("glassbreak.wav", 0.6, 0.5)
end
function H.Hurt()
	flash(Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40), 0.55, 0.5)
	snd("collide.wav", 0.9, 0.6)
	shake(0.9, 0.4)
end
function H.Push(v)
	local hrp = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if hrp then
		hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v
	end
end
local reviveGui
function H.ReviveOffer(productId)
	if reviveGui then
		return
	end
	reviveGui = new("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.45), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 150), BackgroundColor3 = INK,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.05, Active = true, ZIndex = 30, Parent = gui,
	}, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12) }), new("UIStroke", { Color = GOLD, Thickness = 2 }) })
	new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 10), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 24), FontFace = BOLD, TextSize = 18,
		TextColor3 = GOLD, Text = "You are out of lives", ZIndex = 31, Parent = reviveGui,
	})
	new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 38), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 36), FontFace = MED, TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = WHITE, TextWrapped = true, Text = "Revive now and fight with 3 lives again", ZIndex = 31, Parent = reviveGui,
	})
	local function close(decline)
		if decline then
			ReviveRemote:FireServer("decline")
		end
		if reviveGui then
			reviveGui:Destroy()
			reviveGui = nil
		end
	end
	local yes = new("TextButton", {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 92), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 40), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 100),
		TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = WHITE, Text = "REVIVE", ZIndex = 31, Parent = reviveGui,
	})
	local no = new("TextButton", {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(164, 92), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 40), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 76, 96),
		TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = WHITE, Text = "SPECTATE", ZIndex = 31, Parent = reviveGui,
	})
	yes.Activated:Connect(function()
		ReviveRemote:FireServer("pending")
		pcall(function()
			MarketplaceService:PromptProductPurchase(player, productId)
		end)
		close(false)
	end)
	no.Activated:Connect(function()
		close(true)
	end)
end
MarketplaceService.PromptProductPurchaseFinished:Connect(function(userId, _, purchased)
	if userId == player.UserId and not purchased then
		ReviveRemote:FireServer("decline")
	end
end)

end

end

	snd("button.wav", 1, 1.6)

end
function H.BossDeath(pos)
	for i = 1, 4 do
		task.delay(i * 0.25, function()
			burst(pos + Vector3.new(math.random(-6, 6), math.random(2, 28), math.random(-6, 6)), WHITE, 26, 26)
			snd("glassbreak.wav", 1, 0.6 + i * 0.12)
		end)
	end
	snd("bass.mp3", 1, 0.2)
	shake(2, 2.4)
	flash(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0, 2.5)
end
function H.TimeStop(dur, pos)
	banner("Time stop. The fear cannot move", Color3.fromRGB(190, 215, 255))
	flash(Color3.fromRGB(190, 215, 255), 0.5, 0.8)
	snd("clickfast.wav", 1, 0.5)
	snd("bass.mp3", 0.8, 0.35)
	if cc then
		local old = cc.TintColor
		cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(190, 215, 255)
		cc.Saturation = -0.6
		task.delay(dur, function()
			cc.TintColor = old
			cc.Saturation = -1
			snd("button.wav", 0.8, 0.6)
		end)
	end
end
function H.Return(seconds)
	returnLabel.Visible = true
	for i = math.floor(seconds), 1, -1 do
		returnLabel.Text = "Returning to KickArena in " .. i
		task.wait(1)
	end
	returnLabel.Text = "Returning to KickArena..."
end
function H.Defeat()
	flash(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.1, 2)
	snd("bass.mp3", 1, 0.2)
end

		local ok, err = pcall(f, ...)
		if not ok then
			warn("[EndOfFear] " .. tostring(kind) .. ": " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end

17. KickArenaPrivateScript di ServerScriptService (game utama) · 4,752 karakter · 179 baris
-- KickArenaPrivate (Script in ServerScriptService, main place): private server owner tools (dummy, kick, /kick command)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local OWNER = game.PrivateServerOwnerId
if game.PrivateServerId == "" or OWNER == 0 then
	return -- only runs inside a private server that has an owner
end
Workspace:SetAttribute("PrivateOwnerId", OWNER)

local Core
repeat
	Core = _G.KickArenaCore
	if not (Core and Core.spawnDummyFor) then
		Core = nil
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until Core

local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("KickArenaRemotes")
local Hub = Instance.new("RemoteFunction")
Hub.Name = "PrivateHub"
Hub.Parent = remotes

local function myDummy()
	for _, m in ipairs(Core.dummyFolder():GetChildren()) do
		if m:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId") == OWNER then
			return m
		end
	end
end

local function anchorDummy(m)
	for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and not d.Anchored then
			d.Anchored = true
		end
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(1)
		local m = myDummy()
		if m then
			anchorDummy(m)
		end
	end
end)

local function resolve(text)
	text = string.lower(text)
	local found
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if string.lower(p.Name) == text or string.lower(p.DisplayName) == text then
			return p
		end
		if string.sub(string.lower(p.Name), 1, #text) == text or string.sub(string.lower(p.DisplayName), 1, #text) == text then
			if found then
				return nil, "More than one player matches that name"
			end
			found = p
		end
	end
	return found, found == nil and "Player not found" or nil
end

local function kickPlayer(owner, target, reason)
	if not target then
		return false, "Player not found"
	end
	if target == owner then
		return false, "You cannot kick yourself"
	end
	reason = string.sub(tostring(reason or ""), 1, 100)
	local name = target.DisplayName
	target:Kick("You were removed from this private server by its owner." .. (reason ~= "" and ("\nReason: " .. reason) or ""))
	return true, name .. " was kicked"
end

local lastCall = {}
Hub.OnServerInvoke = function(player, action, a, b)
	if player.UserId ~= OWNER then
		return false, "Only the private server owner can do that"
	end
	local now = os.clock()
	if lastCall[player] and now - lastCall[player] < 0.35 then
		return false, "Slow down"
	end
	lastCall[player] = now
	if action == "SpawnDummy" then
		if myDummy() then
			return false, "Only one dummy is allowed. Despawn it first"
		end
		local ok, msg = Core.spawnDummyFor(player)
		if ok then
			local m = myDummy()
			if m then
				anchorDummy(m)
			end
			return true, "Dummy spawned (anchored)"
		end
		return false, msg
	elseif action == "DespawnDummy" then
		if not myDummy() then
			return false, "You have no dummy"
		end
		Core.clearDummiesFor(player)
		return true, "Dummy removed"
	elseif action == "Kick" then
		if type(a) ~= "number" then
			return false, "Pick a player"
		end
		return kickPlayer(player, Players:GetPlayerByUserId(a), type(b) == "string" and b or "")
	elseif action == "List" then
		local list = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= player then
				table.insert(list, { id = p.UserId, name = p.Name, display = p.DisplayName })
			end
		end
		return true, list
	end
	return false, "Unknown action"
end

-- /kick username reason
local seen = {}
local function handleChat(player, text)
	if not player or player.UserId ~= OWNER or type(text) ~= "string" then
		return
	end
	local cmd, rest = text:match("^/(%a+)%s*(.*)$")
	if cmd ~= "kick" then
		return
	end
	if seen[player] and os.clock() - seen[player] < 0.6 then
		return
	end
	seen[player] = os.clock()
	local name, reason = rest:match("^(%S+)%s*(.*)$")
	if not name then
		Core.notify(player, "Use: /kick username reason", false)
		return
	end
	local target, err = resolve(name)
	if not target then
		Core.notify(player, err or "Player not found", false)
		return
	end
	local ok, msg = kickPlayer(player, target, reason)
	Core.notify(player, msg, ok)
end

local function hookPlayer(p)
	p.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
		handleChat(p, msg)
	end)
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookPlayer)
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	hookPlayer(p)
end
if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
	local cmd = Instance.new("TextChatCommand")
	cmd.Name = "KickCommand"
	cmd.PrimaryAlias = "/kick"
	cmd.Parent = TextChatService
	cmd.Triggered:Connect(function(source, text)
		handleChat(Players:GetPlayerByUserId(source.UserId), text)
	end)
end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
	lastCall[p] = nil
	seen[p] = nil
end)

local hub
local function call(...)
	hub = hub or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("KickArenaRemotes"):FindFirstChild("PrivateHub")
	if not hub then
		return false, "Private tools are not ready"
	end
	local ok, a, b = pcall(hub.InvokeServer, hub, ...)
	if not ok then
		return false, "Request failed"
	end
	return a, b
end

	status.TextColor3 = ok and Color3.fromRGB(120, 230, 150) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 130)

local function refreshList()
	local ok, data = call("List")
	if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then
		return
	end
	for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
		if c:IsA("Frame") then
			c:Destroy()
		end
	end
	if #data == 0 then
		new("TextLabel", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, FontFace = MED, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = MUTED, Text = "No other players here", Parent = list })
	end
	for _, e in ipairs(data) do
		local row = new("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), BackgroundColor3 = CARD, Parent = list }, { corner(8) })
		new("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -78, 1, 0), FontFace = MED, TextSize = 12,
			TextColor3 = WHITE, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Text = e.display, Parent = row,
		})
		local kick = new("TextButton", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 22), BackgroundColor3 = RED,
			FontFace = BOLD, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = WHITE, Text = "Kick", Parent = row,
		}, { corner(7) })
		kick.Activated:Connect(function()
			say(call("Kick", e.id, reason.Text))
			task.wait(0.4)
			refreshList()
		end)
	end
end

-- KickArenaDimTycoon (Script in ServerScriptService of the MIRROR place): the End Of Fear upgrade tycoon
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local UPGRADES = {
	{ id = "sp", label = "2x Speed", price = 50 },
	{ id = "kp", label = "Stronger Kick", price = 100 },
	{ id = "d1", label = "+1 Dropper", price = 150 },
	{ id = "auto", label = "Auto Collect + 1 Dropper", price = 200 },
	{ id = "m2", label = "2x Money", price = 235 },
	{ id = "ts", label = "Longer Time Stop", price = 250 },
	{ id = "bf", label = "Longer Boss Faint", price = 300 },
	{ id = "god", label = "5x Strong Kick", price = 1000 },
}
local COIN = 5
local TICK = 1.4
local GOLD = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 70)

local states = {}

local function mk(parent, name, size, cf, color, material)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	p.Name = name
	p.Anchored = true
	p.Size = size
	p.CFrame = cf
	p.Color = color
	p.Material = material or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

local function label(parent, offset, text, size)
	local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, size or 50)
	gui.StudsOffset = offset
	gui.MaxDistance = 70
	gui.Parent = parent
	local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	t.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	t.TextSize = 14
	t.TextWrapped = true
	t.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	t.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
	t.Text = text
	t.Parent = gui
	return t
end

local function newState(p)
	local st = { cash = 0, stored = 0, bought = {}, droppers = 1, mult = 1, auto = false }
	states[p] = st
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	gui.Name = "DimTycoonGui"
	gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	gui.DisplayOrder = 5
	local box = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 46)
	box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -60)
	box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 20)
	box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
	box.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	box.TextSize = 14
	box.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	box.Parent = gui
	local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
	c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
	c.Parent = box
	local msg = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	msg.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(280, 24)
	msg.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 1, -130)
	msg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	msg.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	msg.TextSize = 15
	msg.TextColor3 = GOLD
	msg.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
	msg.Text = ""
	msg.Parent = gui
	gui.Parent = p:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
	st.box, st.msg = box, msg
	return st
end

local function say(st, text)
	st.msg.Text = text
	local mine = text
	task.delay(2.5, function()
		if st.msg.Text == mine then
			st.msg.Text = ""
		end
	end)
end

local function show(st)
	st.box.Text = ("CASH  $%d\nMachine  $%d"):format(st.cash, st.stored)
end

local function apply(p, st)
	local b = st.bought
	p:SetAttribute("DimSpeed", b.sp == true)
	p:SetAttribute("DimReach", (b.kp and 2 or 0) + (b.god and 4 or 0))
	local hum = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hum and b.sp then
		hum.WalkSpeed = 28
	end
	if b.ts then
		Workspace:SetAttribute("DimTS", true)
	end
	if b.bf then
		Workspace:SetAttribute("DimBF", true)
	end
end

local function buildFor(p, st, center, look)
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = "Tycoon_" .. p.Name
	folder.Parent = Workspace
	st.folder = folder
	local cf = CFrame.lookAt(center, center + look)
	local function at(x, y, z)
		return cf * CFrame.new(x, y, z)
	end
	local machine = mk(folder, "CashMachine", Vector3.new(6, 1, 6), at(0, 0.6, 9), Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 46), Enum.Material.Metal)
	mk(folder, "MachineBand", Vector3.new(6.2, 0.3, 6.2), at(0, 1.2, 9), GOLD, Enum.Material.Neon)
	label(machine, Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), p.DisplayName .. "'s CASH MACHINE\nCollect your money", 44)
	local prompt = Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
	prompt.ActionText = "Collect"
	prompt.ObjectText = "Cash Machine"
	prompt.HoldDuration = 0
	prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 9
	prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
	prompt.Parent = machine
	prompt.Triggered:Connect(function(who)
		if who ~= p then
			return
		end
		if st.stored > 0 then
			st.cash += st.stored
			say(st, "+$" .. st.stored)
			st.stored = 0
			show(st)
		end
	end)
	for i, up in ipairs(UPGRADES) do
		local x = (i - 4.5) * 6
		local pad = mk(folder, "Pad_" .. up.id, Vector3.new(5, 0.8, 5), at(x, 0.5, 0), Color3.fromRGB(110, 114, 130), Enum.Material.Metal)
		mk(folder, "PadBand_" .. up.id, Vector3.new(5.2, 0.2, 5.2), at(x, 1, 0), GOLD, Enum.Material.Neon)
		label(pad, Vector3.new(0, 3.4, 0), up.label .. "\n$" .. up.price, 48)
		local pr = Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
		pr.ActionText = "Buy $" .. up.price
		pr.ObjectText = up.label
		pr.HoldDuration = 0.3
		pr.MaxActivationDistance = 8
		pr.RequiresLineOfSight = false
		pr.Parent = pad
		pr.Triggered:Connect(function(who)
			if who ~= p then
				return
			end
			if st.bought[up.id] then
				say(st, "Already owned")
			elseif st.cash < up.price then
				say(st, ("Need $%d more"):format(up.price - st.cash))
			else
				st.cash -= up.price
				st.bought[up.id] = true
				if up.id == "m2" then
					st.mult = 2
				elseif up.id == "auto" then
					st.auto = true
					st.droppers += 1
				elseif up.id == "d1" then
					st.droppers += 1
				end
				apply(p, st)
				say(st, "Purchased: " .. up.label)
				show(st)
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function giveTool(p)
	local st = states[p]
	if not st or st.placed or not p.Character then
		return
	end
	if p.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Place Tycoon") or p.Character:FindFirstChild("Place Tycoon") then
		return
	end
	local tool = Instance.new("Tool")
	tool.Name = "Place Tycoon"
	tool.ToolTip = "Tap to place your tycoon"
	tool.RequiresHandle = false
	tool.CanBeDropped = false
	tool.Parent = p.Backpack
	tool.Activated:Connect(function()
		if st.placed then
			return
		end
		local c = p.Character
		local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp then
			return
		end
		local look = Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
		look = look.Magnitude > 0.1 and look.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
		local center = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z) + look * 14
		if center.Magnitude > 48 then
			center = center.Unit * 48
		end
		st.placed = true
		tool:Destroy()
		buildFor(p, st, center, look)
		say(st, "Tycoon placed")
	end)
	local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hum then
		hum:EquipTool(tool)
	end
	say(st, "Tap to place your tycoon")
end

local function join(p)
	if states[p] or Workspace:GetAttribute("EndOfFear") ~= true then
		return
	end
	local st = newState(p)
	show(st)
	p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(1)
		if states[p] then
			apply(p, states[p])
			giveTool(p)
		end
	end)
	if p.Character then
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(1)
			giveTool(p)
		end)
	end
end

repeat
	task.wait(0.5)
until Workspace:GetAttribute("EndOfFear") == true
task.wait(1)
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	task.spawn(join, p)
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(join)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
	local st = states[p]
	if st and st.folder then
		st.folder:Destroy()
	end
	states[p] = nil
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(TICK)
		for p, st in pairs(states) do
			if not st.placed then
				continue
			end
			local gain = COIN * st.mult * st.droppers
			if st.auto then
				st.cash += gain
			else
				st.stored = math.min(st.stored + gain, 2000)
			end
			if st.box and st.box.Parent then
				show(st)
			end
		end
	end
end)
20. KickArenaCodesScript di ServerScriptService (game utama) · 2,134 karakter · 81 baris
-- KickArenaCodes (Script in ServerScriptService, main place): redeem codes for Kicks
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local DataStoreService = game:GetService("DataStoreService")

local CODES = {
	["release ka"] = 100,
	["75 visit"] = 50,
	["sorry for bug"] = 219,
}

local Core
repeat
	Core = _G.KickArenaCore
	if not (Core and Core.getKicksValue) then
		Core = nil
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
until Core

local store = DataStoreService:GetDataStore("KickArenaCodes_v1")
local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("KickArenaRemotes")
local Redeem = Instance.new("RemoteFunction")
Redeem.Name = "CodeRedeem"
Redeem.Parent = remotes

local busy = {}

Redeem.OnServerInvoke = function(player, raw)
	if busy[player] or type(raw) ~= "string" then
		return false, "Please wait a moment"
	end
	if game.PrivateServerId ~= "" then
		return false, "Codes can't be redeemed in a private server"
	end
	local code = string.lower(raw):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""):gsub("%s+", " ")
	if #code == 0 or #code > 40 then
		return false, "Enter a code"
	end
	local reward = CODES[code]
	if not reward then
		return false, "Invalid code"
	end
	local kv = Core.getKicksValue(player)
	if not kv then
		return false, "Your data is still loading. Try again"
	end
	busy[player] = true
	local key = "u_" .. player.UserId
	local already = false
	local ok = pcall(function()
		store:UpdateAsync(key, function(old)
			old = type(old) == "table" and old or { used = {} }
			old.used = type(old.used) == "table" and old.used or {}
			if old.used[code] then
				already = true
				return nil
			end
			old.used[code] = true
			return old
		end)
	end)
	busy[player] = nil
	if not ok then
		return false, "Could not reach the server. Try again later"
	end
	if already then
		return false, "You already redeemed this code"
	end
	if not player.Parent then
		return false
	end
	kv.Value += reward
	Core.notify(player, ("Code redeemed! +%d Kicks"):format(reward), true)
	return true, ("Code redeemed! +%d Kicks"):format(reward)
end

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
	busy[p] = nil
end)

local function submit()
	if working then
		return
	end
	local text = box.Text
	if #text == 0 then
		status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(248, 81, 81)
		status.Text = "Enter a code"
		return
	end
	working = true
	status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 196, 215)
	status.Text = "Checking..."
	local ok, success, message = pcall(function()
		return Redeem:InvokeServer(text)
	end)
	working = false
	if not ok then
		success, message = false, "Something went wrong. Try again"
	end
	status.TextColor3 = success and Color3.fromRGB(72, 222, 128) or Color3.fromRGB(248, 81, 81)
	status.Text = message or ""
	if success then
		box.Text = ""
	end
end
redeemBtn.Activated:Connect(submit)
box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
	if enter then
		submit()
	end
end)
codesBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
	panel.Visible = not panel.Visible
	status.Text = ""
end)
closeBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
	panel.Visible = false
end)

	local inLobby = player:GetAttribute("ArenaActive") ~= true and player:GetAttribute("AdminOpen") ~= true
	codesBtn.Visible = inLobby
	if not inLobby then
		panel.Visible = false
	end
end

player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("AdminOpen"):Connect(refresh)

-- KickArenaButtons (LocalScript in StarterPlayerScripts, put a copy in BOTH places): blocky pixel style for every button
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local MAX_WIDTH = 128

local function style(b)
	if b:GetAttribute("Blocky") then
		return
	end
	local corner = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
	if corner and corner.CornerRadius.Scale >= 0.5 and b.Size.X.Offset >= 50 then
		return -- big round ability button keeps its ring
	end
	b:SetAttribute("Blocky", true)
	b.Font = Enum.Font.Arcade
	b.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
	b.BorderSizePixel = 0
	if b.Parent and b.Parent:IsA("ScreenGui") and b.Size.X.Scale == 0 and b.Size.X.Offset > MAX_WIDTH then
		b.Size = UDim2.new(0, MAX_WIDTH, b.Size.Y.Scale, b.Size.Y.Offset)
	end
	for _, c in ipairs(b:GetChildren()) do
		if c:IsA("UICorner") then
			c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
		elseif c:IsA("UIGradient") then
			c:Destroy()
		elseif c:IsA("UIStroke") then
			c.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
			c.Thickness = 2
			c.Transparency = 0
			c.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
		end
	end
	if not b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") then
		local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
		st.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		st.Thickness = 2
		st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
		st.Parent = b
	end
	local base = b.BackgroundColor3
	local function strip(name, pos, size, color)
		local f = Instance.new("Frame")
		f.Name = name
		f.Position = pos
		f.Size = size
		f.BackgroundColor3 = color
		f.BorderSizePixel = 0
		f.Active = false
		f.ZIndex = b.ZIndex
		f.Parent = b
	end
	local light, dark = base:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.35), base:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.45)
	strip("BevelTop", UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), light)
	strip("BevelLeft", UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0), light)
	strip("BevelBottom", UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -2), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), dark)
	strip("BevelRight", UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0), dark)
end

local function watch(inst)
	if inst:IsA("TextButton") then
		task.defer(style, inst)
	end
end

for _, d in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do
	watch(d)
end
playerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(watch)

-- KickArenaError (Script in ServerScriptService, main place): Error Kick, the hidden glitch quest and the Error event
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local DataStoreService = game:GetService("DataStoreService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local CAP = 5 -- how many players in total can ever earn the Error Kick by the quest
local CHANCE = 0.10
local HIT_WINDOW = 3.5 -- seconds after a God's Kick hit to use the Stone ability
local WAIT_LIMIT = 90
local PLAY_LIMIT = 600
local START_PHRASE = "(error kick) function start bug"

local Core, Ext
repeat
	Core, Ext = _G.KickArenaCore, _G.KickArenaExt
	if not (Core and Ext and Ext.ready and Core.KICKS.Error) then
		Core, Ext = nil, nil
		task.wait(0.3)
	end
until Core

local store = DataStoreService:GetDataStore("KickArenaError_v1")
local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("KickArenaRemotes")
local Submit = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
Submit.Name = "ErrorSubmit"
Submit.Parent = remotes
local FX = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
FX.Name = "ErrorFX"
FX.Parent = remotes
local Ability = remotes:WaitForChild("Ability")

local capCount = 0
local eventOn = false
local godHitAt = {}
local games = {}
local slotUsed = {}

local function readCap()
	local ok, v = pcall(store.GetAsync, store, "count")
	if ok then
		capCount = tonumber(v) or 0
	end
end
task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		readCap()
		task.wait(25)
	end
end)

local function capOpen()
	return eventOn or capCount < CAP
end

local function claimSlot()
	if eventOn then
		return true
	end
	local granted = false
	local ok = pcall(function()
		store:UpdateAsync("count", function(old)
			old = tonumber(old) or 0
			capCount = old
			if old >= CAP then
				return nil
			end
			granted = true
			return old + 1
		end)
	end)
	if ok and granted then
		capCount += 1
		return true
	end
	return false
end

-- THE KICK: every hit throws the target away
local function launch(model, dir)
	local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local root = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end
	FX:FireAllClients("Launch", root.Position)
	local d = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
	task.spawn(function()
		for _ = 1, 8 do
			if not root.Parent then
				return
			end
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = d * 9000 + Vector3.new(0, 3500, 0)
			task.wait(0.05)
		end
		task.wait(0.8)
		if hum.Parent and hum.Health > 0 then
			hum.Health = 0
		end
	end)
end

-- THE EVENT
local function glitchChar(char)
	if char and not char:FindFirstChild("ErrorGlitch") then
		local h = Instance.new("Highlight")
		h.Name = "ErrorGlitch"
		h.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(30, 255, 90)
		h.FillTransparency = 0.15
		h.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		h.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
		h.Parent = char
	end
end
local function unglitchAll()
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local h = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("ErrorGlitch")
		if h then
			h:Destroy()
		end
	end
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
	p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
		if eventOn then
			task.wait(0.5)
			glitchChar(c)
		end
	end)
end)
local function startEvent(secs)
	if eventOn then
		return false, "The Error event is already running"
	end
	eventOn = true
	Workspace:SetAttribute("ErrorEvent", true)
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		glitchChar(p.Character)
		Core.notify(p, "ERROR EVENT: the system is breaking. Something may open again", true)
	end
	task.delay(secs or 60, function()
		eventOn = false
		Workspace:SetAttribute("ErrorEvent", false)
		unglitchAll()
	end)
	return true, "Error event started"
end
_G.KickArenaError = { startEvent = startEvent }

-- THE QUEST GAME
local function endGame(player, g, won)
	if g.done then
		return
	end
	g.done = true
	games[player] = nil
	slotUsed[g.slot] = nil
	player:SetAttribute("ErrorGame", nil)
	player:SetAttribute("ErrorDeadline", nil)
	player:SetAttribute("NoKick", nil)
	player:SetAttribute("KickImmune", nil)
	local char = player.Character
	if char and char.Parent and g.back then
		char:PivotTo(g.back)
		local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if hrp then
			hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		end
	end
	if g.folder then
		g.folder:Destroy()
	end
	FX:FireClient(player, won and "Won" or "Lost")
end

local function newPart(parent, name, size, cf, color, mat)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	p.Name = name
	p.Anchored = true
	p.Size = size
	p.CFrame = cf
	p.Color = color
	p.Material = mat or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

local GREEN = Color3.fromRGB(40, 255, 110)

local function buildGame(g, origin)
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = "ErrorGame"
	folder.Parent = Workspace
	g.folder = folder
	newPart(folder, "Floor", Vector3.new(130, 2, 130), CFrame.new(origin), Color3.fromRGB(8, 10, 9), Enum.Material.Slate)
	for i = -1, 1, 2 do
		newPart(folder, "Rim", Vector3.new(130, 0.4, 1), CFrame.new(origin + Vector3.new(0, 1.1, i * 64.5)), GREEN, Enum.Material.Neon)
		newPart(folder, "Rim", Vector3.new(1, 0.4, 130), CFrame.new(origin + Vector3.new(i * 64.5, 1.1, 0)), GREEN, Enum.Material.Neon)
		local w1 = newPart(folder, "Wall", Vector3.new(130, 60, 2), CFrame.new(origin + Vector3.new(0, 30, i * 65)), Color3.new(0, 0, 0))
		local w2 = newPart(folder, "Wall", Vector3.new(2, 60, 130), CFrame.new(origin + Vector3.new(i * 65, 30, 0)), Color3.new(0, 0, 0))
		w1.Transparency, w2.Transparency = 1, 1
	end
	for _ = 1, 22 do
		local s = math.random(3, 9)
		local c = newPart(folder, "Glitch", Vector3.new(s, s * math.random(1, 3), s), CFrame.new(origin + Vector3.new(math.random(-55, 55), s, math.random(-55, 55))), Color3.fromRGB(12, math.random(30, 60), 18), Enum.Material.Neon)
		c.Transparency = 0.35
		c.CanCollide = true
	end
	-- four fragments: each one holds one digit of the code
	g.code = ""
	g.problems = {}
	for i = 1, 4 do
		local q = math.random(1, 9)
		local d = math.random(3, 19)
		g.code ..= tostring(q)
		g.problems[i] = ("%d / %d"):format(q * d, d)
	end
	g.prompts = {}
	local used = {}
	for i = 1, 4 do
		local pos
		repeat
			pos = Vector3.new(math.random(-50, 50), 0, math.random(-50, 50))
			local okPos = pos.Magnitude > 14
			for _, u in ipairs(used) do
				if (u - pos).Magnitude < 28 then
					okPos = false
				end
			end
		until okPos
		table.insert(used, pos)
		local node = newPart(folder, "Fragment" .. i, Vector3.new(4, 7, 4), CFrame.new(origin + pos + Vector3.new(0, 4.5, 0)), GREEN, Enum.Material.Neon)
		local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 66)
		gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
		gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
		gui.Parent = node
		local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		t.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		t.Font = Enum.Font.Code
		t.TextSize = 16
		t.TextColor3 = GREEN
		t.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
		t.Text = "FRAGMENT 0" .. i .. "\n[ CORRUPTED ]"
		t.Parent = gui
		local pr = Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
		pr.ActionText = "Decode"
		pr.ObjectText = "Fragment 0" .. i
		pr.HoldDuration = 0.8
		pr.MaxActivationDistance = 9
		pr.RequiresLineOfSight = false
		pr.Enabled = false
		pr.Parent = node
		pr.Triggered:Connect(function(who)
			if who == g.player and g.state == "play" then
				pr.Enabled = false
				t.Text = ("FRAGMENT 0%d\n%s = ?\n(digit %d of the code)"):format(i, g.problems[i], i)
				t.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				FX:FireClient(who, "Decode")
			end
		end)
		g.prompts[i] = pr
	end
end

local function startGame(player)
	if games[player] or not player.Parent then
		return
	end
	local char, _, hrp = Core.getAlive(player)
	if not char or not hrp then
		return
	end
	local slot = 1
	while slotUsed[slot] do
		slot += 1
	end
	slotUsed[slot] = true
	local g = { player = player, slot = slot, state = "wait", back = hrp.CFrame }
	games[player] = g
	local origin = Vector3.new(slot * 900, 7000, 0)
	buildGame(g, origin)
	player:SetAttribute("NoKick", true)
	player:SetAttribute("KickImmune", true)
	player:SetAttribute("ErrorGame", "wait")
	char:PivotTo(CFrame.new(origin + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
	hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	local conn
	conn = player.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
		if g.state ~= "wait" then
			return
		end
		if string.lower((msg:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""))) == START_PHRASE then
			g.state = "play"
			conn:Disconnect()
			player:SetAttribute("ErrorGame", "play")
			player:SetAttribute("ErrorDeadline", Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + PLAY_LIMIT)
			for _, pr in ipairs(g.prompts) do
				pr.Enabled = true
			end
			g.startAt = os.clock()
			FX:FireClient(player, "Start")
		end
	end)
	task.spawn(function()
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while not g.done and player.Parent do
			task.wait(0.4)
			local c = player.Character
			local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not c or not root or (c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Health <= 0) then
				g.back = nil
				break
			end
			if root.Position.Y < origin.Y - 25 then
				c:PivotTo(CFrame.new(origin + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
				root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			if g.state == "wait" and os.clock() - t0 > WAIT_LIMIT then
				break
			end
			if g.state == "play" and os.clock() - g.startAt > PLAY_LIMIT then
				break
			end
		end
		if conn.Connected then
			conn:Disconnect()
		end
		endGame(player, g, false)
	end)
end

local lastTry = {}
Submit.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, code)
	local g = games[player]
	if not g or g.state ~= "play" or g.done or type(code) ~= "string" then
		return
	end
	if os.clock() - (lastTry[player] or 0) < 1 then
		return
	end
	lastTry[player] = os.clock()
	if code ~= g.code then
		FX:FireClient(player, "Denied")
		return
	end
	g.state = "done"
	local data = Core.questData[player]
	if data and not data.Unlocked.Error and claimSlot() then
		Core.unlockKick(player, "Error")
		endGame(player, g, true)
	else
		endGame(player, g, false)
	end
end)

-- hooks into the main kick system
Ext.kicked = function(model, victim, attacker, kickName, dir, inStone)
	if inStone then
		return
	end
	if kickName == "God" and victim then
		godHitAt[victim] = os.clock()
	elseif kickName == "Error" then
		launch(model, dir)
	end
end

Ability.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player)
	task.wait(0.2)
	local t = godHitAt[player]
	if not t or os.clock() - t > HIT_WINDOW or games[player] then
		return
	end
	if player:GetAttribute("EquippedKick") ~= "Stone" or player:GetAttribute("StoneActive") ~= true then
		return
	end
	godHitAt[player] = nil
	local data = Core.questData[player]
	if not data or data.Unlocked.Error or not capOpen() then
		return
	end
	if math.random() < CHANCE then
		startGame(player)
	end
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
	godHitAt[p] = nil
	lastTry[p] = nil
	local g = games[p]
	if g then
		g.back = nil
		endGame(p, g, false)
	end
end)

local SFX_ALT = {
	["button.wav"] = { "button.mp3", "switch.mp3", "clickfast.mp3" },
	["collide.wav"] = { "collide.mp3", "hit.mp3", "snap.mp3" },
	["Rocket whoosh 01.wav"] = { "swordlunge.wav", "swordlunge.mp3", "swordslash.mp3" },
	["glassbreak.wav"] = { "glassbreak.mp3", "collide.mp3", "hit.mp3" },
	["swordslash.wav"] = { "swordslash.mp3", "swordlunge.mp3" },
	["clickfast.wav"] = { "clickfast.mp3", "button.mp3" },
	["bass.mp3"] = { "bass.wav", "collide.mp3" },
	["snap.mp3"] = { "snap.wav", "hit.mp3" },
	["electronicpingshort.wav"] = { "electronicpingshort.mp3", "button.mp3" },
}
local sfxGood = {}
local function armSound(s, file)
	local alts = SFX_ALT[file]
	if not alts then
		return
	end
	if sfxGood[file] then
		s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/" .. sfxGood[file]
		return
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(0.35)
		if not s.Parent then
			return
		end
		if s.IsLoaded and s.TimeLength > 0 then
			sfxGood[file] = file
			return
		end
		for _, n in ipairs(alts) do
			s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/" .. n
			s:Play()
			task.wait(0.35)
			if not s.Parent then
				return
			end
			if s.IsLoaded and s.TimeLength > 0 then
				sfxGood[file] = n
				return
			end
		end
	end)
end

local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
local function snd(file, vol, pitch)
	local s = Instance.new("Sound")
	s.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/" .. file
	s.Volume = vol
	s.PlaybackSpeed = pitch
	s.Parent = SoundService
	s:Play()
	armSound(s, file)
	Debris:AddItem(s, 5)
end

local function shakeCam(power, secs)
	task.spawn(function()
		local hum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while hum and hum.Parent and os.clock() - t0 < secs do
			local k = power * (1 - (os.clock() - t0) / secs)
			hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(-k, k), rng:NextNumber(-k, k), 0)
			task.wait()
		end
		if hum and hum.Parent then
			hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero
		end
	end)
end

	end)
end

-- billboard glitch for the Error Kick stand
local function glitchText(s)
	local chars = { "#", "@", "%", "&", "$", "?", "!", "0", "1", "/" }
	local out = {}
	for i = 1, #s do
		local c = s:sub(i, i)
		if c ~= " " and c ~= "\n" and rng:NextNumber() < 0.22 then
			c = chars[rng:NextInteger(1, #chars)]
		end
		out[i] = c
	end
	return table.concat(out)
end
local tracked = {}
local function scan()
	for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BillboardGui") and d.Name == "InfoBillboard" and not tracked[d] then
			local found = false
			for _, l in ipairs(d:GetDescendants()) do
				if l:IsA("TextLabel") and l.Text == "Error Kick" then
					found = true
				end
			end
			if found then
				tracked[d] = {}
				for _, l in ipairs(d:GetDescendants()) do
					if l:IsA("TextLabel") then
						table.insert(tracked[d], { label = l, text = l.Text, color = l.TextColor3, pos = l.Position })
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		scan()
		task.wait(5)
	end
end)

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	local now = os.clock()

		nextGlitch = now + 0.07
